  refine ⟨dSwap_epm, U3_sub_dom_swap, swap3_image, h1, h2, h3, h4, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · rw [swap3_image, h1]; simp
  · rw [swap3_image, h1, h4]; simp
  · rw [swap3_image, h2, h3]; simp

/-- 两条逆向包含作为一般命题均不成立。 -/
theorem remark_reverse_fails :
    ¬ (∀ (X I : Type) (bot : X) (d : I → X → X → ENNReal), (∀ i, ExtendedPseudoMetric X bot (d i)) →
        ∀ (ϕ₁ ϕ₂ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X), U ⊆ dom (ϕ₂ * ϕ₁) →
          Iact d ϕ₁ U ∪ Iact d ϕ₂ (ϕ₁.func '' U) ⊆ Iact d (ϕ₂ * ϕ₁) U) ∧
    ¬ (∀ (X I : Type) (bot : X) (d : I → X → X → ENNReal), (∀ i, ExtendedPseudoMetric X bot (d i)) →
        ∀ (ϕ₁ ϕ₂ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X), U ⊆ dom (ϕ₂ * ϕ₁) →
          Iid d (ϕ₂ * ϕ₁) U ⊆ Iid d ϕ₁ U ∩ Iid d ϕ₂ (ϕ₁.func '' U)) := by
  obtain ⟨-, hU, -, -, -, -, -, -, h1, h2⟩ := remark_swap
  exact ⟨fun H => h1.2 (H P3 Unit P3.bot dSwap dSwap_epm swap3 swap3 U3 hU),
    fun H => h2.2 (H P3 Unit P3.bot dSwap dSwap_epm swap3 swap3 U3 hU)⟩

/-! ### CH03-P04-02 命题 3.3(ii)：七点、双分量反例 -/

inductive P7 | bot | a | b | p | q | r | s
  deriving DecidableEq

inductive I2 | i | m
  deriving DecidableEq, Fintype

/-- `d_i` 的零距类：把 `q` 并入 `p`。 -/
def cls7 : P7 → P7 := fun x => if x = .q then .p else x

/-- `d_i`：`p,q` 零距，其余不同点距 1；`d_m`：离散度量；吸收元隔离。 -/
noncomputable def d7 : I2 → P7 → P7 → ENNReal
  | .i => clsDist P7.bot cls7 1
  | .m => clsDist P7.bot id 1

theorem d7_epm : ∀ k, ExtendedPseudoMetric P7 P7.bot (d7 k) := by
  intro k; cases k
  · exact clsDist_epm _ _ _ ⟨P7.a, by decide⟩
  · exact clsDist_epm _ _ _ ⟨P7.a, by decide⟩

def phi7 : Operator P7 P7.bot where
  func := fun x => match x with
    | .a => .p
    | .b => .q
    | _ => .bot
  preserves_bot := rfl

def psi7 : Operator P7 P7.bot where
  func := fun x => match x with
    | .p => .r
    | .q => .s
    | _ => .bot
  preserves_bot := rfl

def U7 : Set P7 := {P7.a, P7.b}

/-- 命题 3.3(ii) -/
theorem prop33_ii_example :
    (∀ k, ExtendedPseudoMetric P7 P7.bot (d7 k)) ∧ U7 ⊆ dom (psi7 * phi7) ∧
    d7 .i P7.p P7.q = 0 ∧ d7 .m P7.p P7.q = 1 ∧ d7 .i P7.r P7.s = 1 ∧
    I2.i ∈ Iconst d7 phi7 U7 ∧ I2.i ∉ Iconst d7 (psi7 * phi7) U7 := by
  refine ⟨d7_epm, ?_, by simp [d7, clsDist, cls7], by simp [d7, clsDist],
    by simp [d7, clsDist, cls7], ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro x hx; simp only [U7, mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at hx
    rcases hx with rfl | rfl <;> simp [dom, psi7, phi7]
  · intro x hx y hy
    simp only [U7, mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at hx hy
    rcases hx with rfl | rfl <;> rcases hy with rfl | rfl <;> simp [d7, clsDist, cls7, phi7]
  · exact (not_mem_Iconst_iff d7).2 ⟨.a, by simp [U7], .b, by simp [U7],
      by simp [d7, clsDist, cls7, phi7, psi7]⟩

/-- 命题 3.3(ii) -/
theorem prop33_ii :
    ¬ (∀ (X I : Type) (bot : X) (d : I → X → X → ENNReal), (∀ i, ExtendedPseudoMetric X bot (d i)) →
        ∀ (ϕ ψ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (i : I), U ⊆ dom (ψ * ϕ) →
          i ∈ Iconst d ϕ U → i ∈ Iconst d (ψ * ϕ) U) := by
  intro H
  obtain ⟨-, hU, -, -, -, h1, h2⟩ := prop33_ii_example
  exact h2 (H P7 I2 P7.bot d7 d7_epm phi7 psi7 U7 I2.i hU h1)

/-! ### CH03-P11-03 命题 3.8(ii)：无穷输入距离的两点反例 -/

/-- 指标集任意（`DecidableEq`），给定输入分量 `i₀`：`d_{i₀}(a,b) = +∞`，其余分量 `d_k(a,b) = 1`。 -/
noncomputable def dInf {I : Type} [DecidableEq I] (i₀ : I) : I → P3 → P3 → ENNReal :=
  fun k => clsDist P3.bot id (if k = i₀ then ⊤ else 1)

theorem dInf_epm {I : Type} [DecidableEq I] (i₀ : I) :
    ∀ k, ExtendedPseudoMetric P3 P3.bot (dInf i₀ k) :=
  fun _ => clsDist_epm _ _ _ ⟨P3.a, by decide⟩

/-- 命题 3.8(ii) -/
theorem prop38_ii_example {I : Type} [DecidableEq I] (i j : I) :
    (∀ k, ExtendedPseudoMetric P3 P3.bot (dInf i k)) ∧ U3.Nonempty ∧
    U3 ⊆ dom (1 : Operator P3 P3.bot) ∧
    (∀ y ∈ U3, rN (dInf i) (1 : Operator P3 P3.bot) i j y U3 = ⊤) ∧
    nullMatrix (dInf i) (1 : Operator P3 P3.bot) U3 j i = ⊤ ∧ j ∉ Iconst (dInf i) (1 : Operator P3 P3.bot) U3 := by
  have hpt : ∀ y ∈ U3, rN (dInf i) (1 : Operator P3 P3.bot) i j y U3 = ⊤ := by
    intro y hy
    apply nullRadius_eq_top_of_forall
    intro r _ hrt x hx
    refine ⟨hx.2, ?_⟩
    have hxy : x = y := by
      by_contra hne
      have h1 := hx.1
      simp only [ball, mem_setOf_eq, U3, mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at h1 hy
      have hx2 := hx.2
      simp only [U3, mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at hx2
      rcases hx2 with rfl | rfl <;> rcases hy with rfl | rfl <;>
        simp_all [dInf, clsDist]
    subst hxy; exact (dInf_epm i j).dist_self _
  refine ⟨dInf_epm i, ⟨P3.a, by simp [U3]⟩, ?_, hpt, ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro x hx; simp only [U3, mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at hx
    rcases hx with rfl | rfl <;> simp [dom]
  · exact top_unique (le_uniform fun y hy => (hpt y hy).ge)
  · refine (not_mem_Iconst_iff (dInf i)).2 ⟨.a, by simp [U3], .b, by simp [U3], ?_⟩
    by_cases h : j = i <;> simp [dInf, clsDist, h]

/-- 命题 3.8(ii) -/
theorem prop38_ii :
    (¬ ∀ (X I : Type) [Fintype I] (bot : X) (d : I → X → X → ENNReal),
        (∀ k, ExtendedPseudoMetric X bot (d k)) → ∀ (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (i j : I),
          U.Nonempty → U ⊆ dom ϕ → i = j → nullMatrix d ϕ U j i = ⊤ → j ∈ Iconst d ϕ U) ∧
    (¬ ∀ (X I : Type) [Fintype I] (bot : X) (d : I → X → X → ENNReal),
        (∀ k, ExtendedPseudoMetric X bot (d k)) → ∀ (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (i j : I),
          U.Nonempty → U ⊆ dom ϕ → i ≠ j → nullMatrix d ϕ U j i = ⊤ → j ∈ Iconst d ϕ U) := by
  constructor
  · intro H
    obtain ⟨h0, hne, hdom, -, hN, hj⟩ := prop38_ii_example (I := Unit) () ()
    exact hj (H P3 Unit P3.bot _ h0 1 U3 () () hne hdom rfl hN)
  · intro H
    obtain ⟨h0, hne, hdom, -, hN, hj⟩ := prop38_ii_example (I := Bool) true false
    exact hj (H P3 Bool P3.bot _ h0 1 U3 true false hne hdom (by decide) hN)

end OETCh03



namespace OETCh03

open formal_oet.vol_01.ch_01_foundations
open formal_oet.vol_01.ch_02_operator_bounds
open Set

/-! ### 覆盖数的具体下界工具 -/

section CoverTools
variable {X : Type}

/-- 两点无公共内部中心时覆盖数至少为 2。 -/
theorem two_le_covering_number {δ : X → X → ENNReal} {ε : ENNReal} {U : Set X} {x y : X}
    (hx : x ∈ U) (hy : y ∈ U) (h : ∀ c ∈ U, δ x c < ε → δ y c < ε → False) :
    2 ≤ covering_number δ ε U := by
  apply le_iInf₂
  intro S hS
  obtain ⟨cx, hcx, hdx⟩ := hS.2 x hx
  obtain ⟨cy, hcy, hdy⟩ := hS.2 y hy
  have hne : cx ≠ cy := by rintro rfl; exact h cx (hS.1 hcx) hdx hdy
  have : 2 ≤ S.card := Finset.one_lt_card.2 ⟨cx, hcx, cy, hcy, hne⟩
  exact_mod_cast this

/-- 若 `T` 中每点只能被自身作为内部中心覆盖，则覆盖数至少为 `|T|`。 -/
theorem card_le_covering_number_of_isolated {δ : X → X → ENNReal} {ε : ENNReal} {U : Set X}
    (T : Finset X) (hT : (↑T : Set X) ⊆ U) (h : ∀ x ∈ T, ∀ c ∈ U, δ x c < ε → c = x) :
    (T.card : ENNReal) ≤ covering_number δ ε U := by
  apply le_iInf₂
  intro S hS
  have hsub : T ⊆ S := by
    intro x hx
    obtain ⟨c, hc, hd⟩ := hS.2 x (hT hx)
    rw [← h x hx c (hS.1 hc) hd]; exact hc
  exact_mod_cast Finset.card_le_card hsub

theorem covering_number_le_two {δ : X → X → ENNReal} {ε : ENNReal} {U : Set X} [DecidableEq X]
    (a b : X) (h : InternalEpsilonNet δ ε U {a, b}) : covering_number δ ε U ≤ 2 :=
  le_trans (covering_number_le_card h) (by exact_mod_cast Finset.card_le_two)

theorem covering_number_le_one {δ : X → X → ENNReal} {ε : ENNReal} {U : Set X}
    (a : X) (h : InternalEpsilonNet δ ε U {a}) : covering_number δ ε U ≤ 1 :=
  le_trans (covering_number_le_card h) (by simp)

end CoverTools

theorem log2_ereal_two : log2_ereal 2 = 1 := by
  simp [log2_ereal]

/-! ### 带吸收元的实直线 `ℝ ⊔ {⊥}` -/

/-- `ℝ ⊔ {⊥}`：`none` 为吸收元，`some x` 为实数点。 -/
abbrev RB := Option ℝ

/-- 把实数部分的距离按吸收元约定延拓：`d(⊥,⊥) = 0`，`⊥` 与实数点距离 `+∞`。 -/
noncomputable def liftDist (ρ : ℝ → ℝ → ENNReal) : RB → RB → ENNReal
  | none, none => 0
  | some _, none => ⊤
  | none, some _ => ⊤
  | some x, some y => ρ x y

@[simp] theorem liftDist_some (ρ : ℝ → ℝ → ENNReal) (x y : ℝ) :
    liftDist ρ (some x) (some y) = ρ x y := rfl

theorem liftDist_epm (ρ : ℝ → ℝ → ENNReal) (h0 : ∀ x, ρ x x = 0) (hs : ∀ x y, ρ x y = ρ y x)
    (ht : ∀ x y z, ρ x z ≤ ρ x y + ρ y z) : ExtendedPseudoMetric RB none (liftDist ρ) where
  nonabsorbing := ⟨some 0, by simp⟩
  dist_self x := by cases x <;> simp [liftDist, h0]
  symm x y := by cases x <;> cases y <;> simp [liftDist, hs]
  triangle x y z := by cases x <;> cases y <;> cases z <;> simp [liftDist, ht]
  isolate_bot x hx := by cases x <;> simp_all [liftDist]

/-- 通常距离 `|x - y|`。 -/
noncomputable def dR : RB → RB → ENNReal := liftDist (fun x y => ENNReal.ofReal |x - y|)

/-- 绝对值读数距离 `||x| - |y||`。 -/
noncomputable def dAbs : RB → RB → ENNReal := liftDist (fun x y => ENNReal.ofReal |(|x| - |y|)|)

@[simp] theorem dR_some (x y : ℝ) : dR (some x) (some y) = ENNReal.ofReal |x - y| := rfl
@[simp] theorem dAbs_some (x y : ℝ) : dAbs (some x) (some y) = ENNReal.ofReal |(|x| - |y|)| := rfl

theorem dR_epm : ExtendedPseudoMetric RB none dR :=
  liftDist_epm _ (fun x => by simp) (fun x y => by rw [abs_sub_comm])
    (fun x y z => (ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal (abs_sub_le x y z)).trans ENNReal.ofReal_add_le)

theorem dAbs_epm : ExtendedPseudoMetric RB none dAbs :=
  liftDist_epm _ (fun x => by simp) (fun x y => by rw [abs_sub_comm])
    (fun x y z => (ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal (abs_sub_le |x| |y| |z|)).trans ENNReal.ofReal_add_le)

/-- 单分量规范族（指标集 `Unit`）。 -/
noncomputable def gR : Unit → RB → RB → ENNReal := fun _ => dR
noncomputable def gAbs : Unit → RB → RB → ENNReal := fun _ => dAbs

theorem gR_epm : ∀ i, ExtendedPseudoMetric RB none (gR i) := fun _ => dR_epm
theorem gAbs_epm : ∀ i, ExtendedPseudoMetric RB none (gAbs i) := fun _ => dAbs_epm

/-- 实值全映射提升为保持吸收元的算子。 -/
def realOp (h : ℝ → ℝ) : Operator RB none := ⟨fun z => z.map h, rfl⟩

/-- 偏映射（`none` 表示未定义）提升为保持吸收元的算子。 -/
def partialOp (g : ℝ → Option ℝ) : Operator RB none := ⟨fun z => z.bind g, rfl⟩

@[simp] theorem realOp_some (h : ℝ → ℝ) (x : ℝ) : (realOp h).func (some x) = some (h x) := rfl
@[simp] theorem partialOp_some (g : ℝ → Option ℝ) (x : ℝ) :
    (partialOp g).func (some x) = g x := rfl

theorem some_mem_dom_realOp (h : ℝ → ℝ) (x : ℝ) : some x ∈ dom (realOp h) := by simp [dom]

theorem image_some_sub_dom_realOp (h : ℝ → ℝ) (S : Set ℝ) : some '' S ⊆ dom (realOp h) := by
  rintro _ ⟨x, -, rfl⟩; exact some_mem_dom_realOp h x

theorem ofReal_eq_toReal {ε : ENNReal} (h0 : 0 < ε) (ht : ε < ⊤) :
    ∃ e : ℝ, 0 < e ∧ ε = ENNReal.ofReal e :=
  ⟨ε.toReal, ENNReal.toReal_pos h0.ne' ht.ne, (ENNReal.ofReal_toReal ht.ne).symm⟩

/-! ### CH03-P10 反例 3.1：阶跃算子 -/

/-- 阶跃函数 `f(x) = 0 (x < 0)`，`1 (x ≥ 0)`。 -/
noncomputable def stepF (x : ℝ) : ℝ := if x < 0 then 0 else 1

noncomputable def stepOp : Operator RB none := realOp stepF

def stepSet : Set ℝ := Ico (-1) 0 ∪ Ioc 0 1

/-- `U = [-1,0) ∪ (0,1] ⊆ ℝ ⊔ {⊥}`。 -/
def Ustep : Set RB := some '' stepSet

/-- 反例 3.1 的结构合法性 -/
theorem ex_step_structure :
    (∀ i, ExtendedPseudoMetric RB none (gR i)) ∧ stepOp.func none = none ∧
    Ustep ⊆ dom stepOp ∧ Ustep.Nonempty ∧ none ∉ Ustep :=
  ⟨gR_epm, rfl, image_some_sub_dom_realOp _ _,
    ⟨some 1, mem_image_of_mem _ (Or.inr ⟨one_pos, le_rfl⟩)⟩, by simp [Ustep]⟩

theorem stepF_eq_of_close {x y : ℝ} (hy : y ≠ 0) (h : |x - y| < |y|) : stepF x = stepF y := by
  rcases lt_or_gt_of_ne hy with hy | hy
  · rw [abs_of_neg hy] at h
    have : x < 0 := by linarith [le_abs_self (x - y)]
    simp [stepF, this, hy]
  · rw [abs_of_pos hy] at h
    have : 0 < x := by linarith [neg_abs_le (x - y)]
    simp [stepF, not_lt.2 this.le, not_lt.2 hy.le]

theorem stepSet_ne_zero {y : ℝ} (hy : y ∈ stepSet) : y ≠ 0 := by
  rcases hy with ⟨-, h⟩ | ⟨h, -⟩ <;> [exact h.ne; exact h.ne']

/-- 半径超过 `|y|` 时，存在 `U` 中异号点落入开球。 -/
theorem step_witness {y : ℝ} (hy : y ∈ stepSet) {r : ℝ} (hr : |y| < r) :
    ∃ x ∈ stepSet, |x - y| < r ∧ stepF x ≠ stepF y := by
  obtain ⟨m, hm0, hm1, hm2⟩ : ∃ m : ℝ, 0 < m ∧ m ≤ 1 ∧ m ≤ r - |y| :=
    ⟨min (r - |y|) 1, lt_min (by linarith) one_pos, min_le_right _ _, min_le_left _ _⟩
  rcases hy with ⟨hy1, hy2⟩ | ⟨hy1, hy2⟩
  · rw [abs_of_neg hy2] at hm2
    refine ⟨m / 2, Or.inr ⟨by linarith, by linarith⟩, ?_, ?_⟩
    · rw [abs_of_pos (by linarith)]; linarith
    · simp [stepF, hy2, not_lt.2 (by linarith : (0:ℝ) ≤ m / 2)]
  · rw [abs_of_pos hy1] at hm2
    refine ⟨-(m / 2), Or.inl ⟨by linarith, by linarith⟩, ?_, ?_⟩
    · rw [abs_of_neg (by linarith)]; linarith
    · simp [stepF, not_lt.2 hy1.le, (by linarith : -(m / 2) < 0)]

/-- 反例 3.1 -/
theorem ex_step_adm (y : ℝ) (hy : y ∈ stepSet) :
    admRadii dR dR stepOp (some y) Ustep = Ioc 0 (ENNReal.ofReal |y|) := by
  ext r
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨h0, ht, hsub⟩
    refine ⟨h0, ?_⟩
    by_contra hlt
    rw [not_le] at hlt
    obtain ⟨e, he0, rfl⟩ := ofReal_eq_toReal h0 ht
    have hye : |y| < e := ((ENNReal.ofReal_lt_ofReal_iff he0).1 hlt)
    obtain ⟨x, hx, hxy, hne⟩ := step_witness hy hye
    have hmem : some x ∈ ball dR (some y) (ENNReal.ofReal e) ∩ Ustep :=
      ⟨by simpa [ball] using (ENNReal.ofReal_lt_ofReal_iff he0).2 hxy, mem_image_of_mem _ hx⟩
    have h0' := (hsub hmem).2
    simp only [stepOp, realOp_some, dR_some, ENNReal.ofReal_eq_zero] at h0'
    exact hne (by linarith [abs_nonneg (stepF x - stepF y), abs_le.1 (le_of_le_of_eq h0' rfl)])
  · rintro ⟨h0, hle⟩
    refine ⟨h0, lt_of_le_of_lt hle ENNReal.ofReal_lt_top, ?_⟩
    rintro _ ⟨hb, ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩⟩
    refine ⟨mem_image_of_mem _ hx, ?_⟩
    have hb' : ENNReal.ofReal |x - y| < ENNReal.ofReal |y| := lt_of_lt_of_le hb hle
    have := stepF_eq_of_close (stepSet_ne_zero hy) (ENNReal.ofReal_lt_ofReal_iff'.1 hb').1
    simp [stepOp, this]

/-- 反例 3.1 -/
theorem ex_step_radius (y : ℝ) (hy : y ∈ stepSet) :
    nullRadius dR dR stepOp (some y) Ustep = ENNReal.ofReal |y| ∧
    0 < nullRadius dR dR stepOp (some y) Ustep := by
  have hpos : 0 < ENNReal.ofReal |y| :=
    ENNReal.ofReal_pos.2 (abs_pos.2 (stepSet_ne_zero hy))
  have : nullRadius dR dR stepOp (some y) Ustep = ENNReal.ofReal |y| := by
    rw [nullRadius, ex_step_adm y hy, csSup_Ioc hpos]
  exact ⟨this, this ▸ hpos⟩

/-- 反例 3.1 -/
theorem ex_step_uniform : uNR dR dR stepOp Ustep = 0 ∧ nullMatrix gR stepOp Ustep () () = 0 := by
  have h : uNR dR dR stepOp Ustep = 0 := by
    apply le_antisymm _ (zero_le _)
    apply ENNReal.le_of_forall_pos_le_add
    intro ε hε _
    rw [zero_add]
    have hε' : (0 : ℝ) < ε := NNReal.coe_pos.2 hε
    have hy : min 1 (ε : ℝ) ∈ stepSet := Or.inr ⟨lt_min one_pos hε', min_le_left _ _⟩
    calc uNR dR dR stepOp Ustep ≤ nullRadius dR dR stepOp (some (min 1 (ε : ℝ))) Ustep :=
          uniform_le_nullRadius (mem_image_of_mem _ hy)
      _ = ENNReal.ofReal |min 1 (ε : ℝ)| := (ex_step_radius _ hy).1
      _ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal ε := ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal
          (by rw [abs_of_pos (lt_min one_pos hε')]; exact min_le_right _ _)
      _ = ε := ENNReal.ofReal_coe_nnreal
  exact ⟨h, h⟩

/-- 反例 3.1 总结 -/
theorem counterexample31 :
    (∀ y ∈ Ustep, 0 < nullRadius dR dR stepOp y Ustep) ∧ uNR dR dR stepOp Ustep = 0 := by
  refine ⟨?_, ex_step_uniform.1⟩
  rintro _ ⟨y, hy, rfl⟩
  exact (ex_step_radius y hy).2

/-! ### CH03-P17-04 命题 3.14(iii)：直径尺度的二点/单点反例 -/

theorem diam_pair_01 : diam dR {some 0, some 1} = 1 := by
  apply le_antisymm
  · refine iSup₂_le fun x hx => iSup₂_le fun y hy => ?_
    simp only [mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at hx hy
    rcases hx with rfl | rfl <;> rcases hy with rfl | rfl <;> norm_num
  · simpa using le_diam dR (A := {some 0, some 1}) (x := some 1) (y := some 0) (by simp) (by simp)

theorem diam_single_0 : diam dR {some 0} = 0 := by
  apply le_antisymm _ (zero_le _)
  refine iSup₂_le fun x hx => iSup₂_le fun y hy => ?_
  simp only [mem_singleton_iff] at hx hy
  subst hx; subst hy; simp

/-- 命题 3.14(iii) -/
theorem prop314_iii :
    let A : Set RB := {some 0, some 1}
    let B : Set RB := {some 0}
    B.Nonempty ∧ B ⊆ A ∧ A ⊆ dom (1 : Operator RB none) ∧
    (diamScale gR).σ () B = 0 ∧ (diamScale gR).σ () A = 1 ∧
    (diamScale gR).σ () B < 1 / 2 ∧ 1 / 2 ≤ (diamScale gR).σ () A := by
  intro A B
  have hB : (diamScale gR).σ () B = 0 := diam_single_0
  have hA : (diamScale gR).σ () A = 1 := diam_pair_01
  refine ⟨⟨some 0, rfl⟩, by simp [A, B], ?_, hB, hA, ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro x hx; simp only [A, mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at hx
    rcases hx with rfl | rfl <;> simp [dom]
  · rw [hB]; norm_num
  · rw [hA]; exact ENNReal.half_le_self

/-- 命题 3.14(iii) -/
theorem prop314_iii_not :
    ¬ ∀ (A B : Set RB), B.Nonempty → B ⊆ A → A ⊆ dom (1 : Operator RB none) →
      (diamScale gR).σ () B < 1 / 2 → (diamScale gR).σ () A < 1 / 2 := by
  intro H
  obtain ⟨h1, h2, h3, -, -, h6, h7⟩ := prop314_iii
  exact absurd (H _ _ h1 h2 h3 h6) (not_lt.2 h7)

/-! ### CH03-P20 命题 3.16 后的二点覆盖反例 -/

theorem cov_pair_01_half : covering_number dR (1 / 2) {some 0, some 1} = 2 := by
  classical
  apply le_antisymm
  · apply covering_number_le_two (some 0) (some 1)
    refine ⟨by simp, fun x hx => ?_⟩
    simp only [mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at hx
    rcases hx with rfl | rfl
    · exact ⟨some 0, by simp, by simp⟩
    · exact ⟨some 1, by simp, by simp⟩
  · apply two_le_covering_number (x := some 0) (y := some 1) (by simp) (by simp)
    intro c hc h1 h2
    simp only [mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at hc
    rcases hc with rfl | rfl
    · norm_num at h2
    · norm_num at h1

/-- 命题 3.16 后正文反例 -/
theorem remark316 (τ : ENNReal) (hτ0 : 0 < τ) (hτt : τ < ⊤) :
    ∃ a : ℝ, 0 < a ∧ a ≤ 1 ∧ ENNReal.ofReal (2 * a) < τ ∧
      ({some (-a), some a} : Set RB) ⊆ Ustep ∧
      covering_number dR τ {some (-a), some a} = 1 ∧
      stepOp.func '' {some (-a), some a} = {some 0, some 1} ∧
      covering_number dR (1 / 2) (stepOp.func '' {some (-a), some a}) = 2 := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨e, he0, rfl⟩ := ofReal_eq_toReal hτ0 hτt
  set a := min 1 (e / 4) with ha
  have ha0 : 0 < a := lt_min one_pos (by linarith)
  have ha1 : a ≤ 1 := min_le_left _ _
  have ha2 : a ≤ e / 4 := min_le_right _ _
  have h2a : ENNReal.ofReal (2 * a) < ENNReal.ofReal e :=
    (ENNReal.ofReal_lt_ofReal_iff he0).2 (by linarith)
  have himg : stepOp.func '' {some (-a), some a} = {some 0, some 1} := by
    rw [image_insert_eq, image_singleton]
    simp [stepOp, stepF, ha0, not_lt.2 ha0.le]
  refine ⟨a, ha0, ha1, h2a, ?_, ?_, himg, by rw [himg]; exact cov_pair_01_half⟩
  · intro x hx
    simp only [mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at hx
    rcases hx with rfl | rfl
    · exact mem_image_of_mem _ (Or.inl ⟨by linarith, by linarith⟩)
    · exact mem_image_of_mem _ (Or.inr ⟨ha0, ha1⟩)
  · apply le_antisymm
    · apply covering_number_le_one (some a)
      refine ⟨by simp, fun x hx => ⟨some a, by simp, ?_⟩⟩
      simp only [mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at hx
      rcases hx with rfl | rfl
      · simp only [dR_some]
        rw [show -a - a = -(2 * a) by ring, abs_neg, abs_of_pos (by linarith)]
        exact h2a
      · simpa using ENNReal.ofReal_pos.2 he0
    · exact one_le_covering_number_of_nonempty _ _ _ ⟨some a, by simp⟩

/-- 点态正半径不能代替统一阈值 -/
theorem remark316_no_uniform_scale (τ : ENNReal) (hτ0 : 0 < τ) (hτt : τ < ⊤) :
    ∃ B ⊆ Ustep, covering_number dR τ B < covering_number dR (1 / 2) (stepOp.func '' B) := by
  obtain ⟨a, -, -, -, hB, h1, -, h2⟩ := remark316 τ hτ0 hτt
  refine ⟨_, hB, ?_⟩
  rw [h1, h2]; norm_num

end OETCh03



namespace OETCh03

open formal_oet.vol_01.ch_01_foundations
open formal_oet.vol_01.ch_02_operator_bounds
open Set

theorem dR_lt_iff (x y e : ℝ) (he : 0 < e) :
    dR (some x) (some y) < ENNReal.ofReal e ↔ |x - y| < e := by
  rw [dR_some, ENNReal.ofReal_lt_ofReal_iff he]

theorem dAbs_lt_iff (x y e : ℝ) (he : 0 < e) :
    dAbs (some x) (some y) < ENNReal.ofReal e ↔ |(|x| - |y|)| < e := by
  rw [dAbs_some, ENNReal.ofReal_lt_ofReal_iff he]

/-! ### CH03-P26 习题 3.1 -/

/-- `ϕ(x) = 2x - 1`，`ϕ(⊥) = ⊥`。 -/
noncomputable def affOp : Operator RB none := realOp (fun x => 2 * x - 1)

def U01 : Set RB := {some 0, some 1}

theorem U01_sub_dom_realOp (h : ℝ → ℝ) : U01 ⊆ dom (realOp h) := by
  intro x hx; simp only [U01, mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at hx
  rcases hx with rfl | rfl <;> exact some_mem_dom_realOp _ _

/-- 习题 3.1(i-a) -/
theorem ex31_i_a :
    (∀ i, ExtendedPseudoMetric RB none (gAbs i)) ∧ affOp.func none = none ∧ U01 ⊆ dom affOp ∧
    affOp.func (some 0) = some (-1) ∧ affOp.func (some 1) = some 1 ∧
    Fib gAbs affOp {()} (affOp.func (some 0)) U01 = U01 ∧ () ∈ Iconst gAbs affOp U01 := by
  have hc : () ∈ Iconst gAbs affOp U01 := by
    intro x hx y hy
    simp only [U01, mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at hx hy
    rcases hx with rfl | rfl <;> rcases hy with rfl | rfl <;> norm_num [gAbs, affOp]
  refine ⟨gAbs_epm, rfl, U01_sub_dom_realOp _, by norm_num [affOp], by norm_num [affOp], ?_, hc⟩
  exact (prop35_vi_b gAbs gAbs_epm affOp U01 () hc _ (mem_image_of_mem _ (by simp [U01]))).1

/-- 习题 3.1(i-b) -/
theorem ex31_i_b :
    U01 ⊆ dom (affOp * affOp) ∧ (affOp * affOp).func (some 0) = some (-3) ∧
    (affOp * affOp).func (some 1) = some 1 ∧
    Fib gAbs (affOp * affOp) {()} ((affOp * affOp).func (some 0)) U01 = {some 0} ∧
    () ∈ Iact gAbs (affOp * affOp) U01 := by
  have e0 : (affOp * affOp).func (some 0) = some (-3) := by norm_num [affOp]
  have e1 : (affOp * affOp).func (some 1) = some 1 := by norm_num [affOp]
  refine ⟨?_, e0, e1, ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro x hx; simp only [U01, mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at hx
    rcases hx with rfl | rfl
    · show (affOp * affOp).func (some 0) ≠ none; rw [e0]; simp
    · show (affOp * affOp).func (some 1) ≠ none; rw [e1]; simp
  · rw [Fib_singleton, e0]
    ext x
    simp only [Fib1, U01, mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff, mem_setOf_eq]
    constructor
    · rintro ⟨hx, h⟩
      rcases hx with rfl | rfl
      · rfl
      · rw [e1] at h; norm_num [gAbs] at h
    · rintro rfl; exact ⟨Or.inl rfl, by rw [e0]; simp [gAbs]⟩
  · rw [mem_Iact_iff]
    constructor
    · rw [not_mem_Iconst_iff]
      exact ⟨some 0, by simp [U01], some 1, by simp [U01], by rw [e0, e1]; norm_num [gAbs]⟩
    · intro h
      have := h (some 0) (by simp [U01])
      rw [e0] at this; norm_num [gAbs] at this

/-- 习题 3.1(ii) -/
theorem ex31_ii (ψ : Operator RB none) (c : ℝ) (h0 : ψ.func (some 0) = some c)
    (h1 : ψ.func (some 1) = some c) :
    (ψ * ψ).func (some 0) = (ψ * ψ).func (some 1) ∧
    gAbs () ((ψ * ψ).func (some 0)) ((ψ * ψ).func (some 1)) = 0 ∧
    Fib gAbs (ψ * ψ) {()} ((ψ * ψ).func (some 0)) U01 =
      Fib gAbs (ψ * ψ) {()} ((ψ * ψ).func (some 1)) U01 ∧
    (ψ.func (some c) ≠ none → U01 ⊆ dom (ψ * ψ) ∧
      Fib gAbs (ψ * ψ) {()} ((ψ * ψ).func (some 0)) U01 = U01 ∧ () ∈ Iconst gAbs (ψ * ψ) U01) ∧
    (ψ.func (some c) = none → U01 ∩ dom (ψ * ψ) = ∅ ∧ ¬ U01 ⊆ dom (ψ * ψ)) := by
  have e : (ψ * ψ).func (some 0) = (ψ * ψ).func (some 1) := by simp [h0, h1]
  have hall : ∀ x ∈ U01, (ψ * ψ).func x = ψ.func (some c) := by
    intro x hx; simp only [U01, mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at hx
    rcases hx with rfl | rfl <;> simp [h0, h1]
  have hconst : () ∈ Iconst gAbs (ψ * ψ) U01 := by
    intro x hx y hy; rw [hall x hx, hall y hy]; exact dAbs_epm.dist_self _
  refine ⟨e, by rw [e]; exact dAbs_epm.dist_self _, by rw [e], fun hne => ⟨?_, ?_, hconst⟩,
    fun hn => ⟨?_, ?_⟩⟩
  · intro x hx; show (ψ * ψ).func x ≠ none; rw [hall x hx]; exact hne
  · exact (prop35_vi_b gAbs gAbs_epm _ U01 () hconst _
      (mem_image_of_mem _ (by simp [U01] : some 0 ∈ U01))).1
  · ext x
    simp only [mem_inter_iff, mem_empty_iff_false, iff_false, not_and]
    intro hx hd; exact hd (by rw [hall x hx]; exact hn)
  · intro hsub
    exact hsub (by simp [U01] : some 0 ∈ U01) (by rw [hall _ (by simp [U01])]; exact hn)

/-! ### CH03-P28 习题 3.3 -/

/-- 平移 `ϕ(x) = x + 1`。 -/
noncomputable def shiftOp : Operator RB none := realOp (fun x => x + 1)

def U33 : Set RB := {some (-3), some (-2), some 0, some 2}

theorem mem_U33 {x : RB} :
    x ∈ U33 ↔ x = some (-3) ∨ x = some (-2) ∨ x = some 0 ∨ x = some 2 := by
  simp [U33]

theorem U33_sub_dom_realOp (h : ℝ → ℝ) : U33 ⊆ dom (realOp h) := by
  intro x hx; rw [mem_U33] at hx
  rcases hx with rfl | rfl | rfl | rfl <;> exact some_mem_dom_realOp _ _

theorem U33_sub_dom_one : U33 ⊆ dom (1 : Operator RB none) := by
  intro x hx; rw [mem_U33] at hx
  rcases hx with rfl | rfl | rfl | rfl <;> simp [dom]

theorem fib33 (h : ℝ → ℝ) (t : ℝ) :
    Fib gAbs (realOp h) {()} (some t) U33 =
      {x | x ∈ U33 ∧ ∃ s, x = some s ∧ |(|h s| - |t|)| ≤ 0} := by
  rw [Fib_singleton]
  ext x
  simp only [Fib1, mem_setOf_eq]
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨hx, hd⟩
    have hx' := hx; rw [mem_U33] at hx'
    rcases hx' with rfl | rfl | rfl | rfl <;>
      exact ⟨hx, _, rfl, by simpa [gAbs, ENNReal.ofReal_eq_zero] using hd⟩
  · rintro ⟨hx, s, rfl, hs⟩
    exact ⟨hx, by simpa [gAbs, ENNReal.ofReal_eq_zero] using hs⟩

theorem one_eq_realOp_id : (1 : Operator RB none) = realOp id := by
  apply Operator.ext; funext z; cases z <;> rfl

/-- 习题 3.3(i) -/
theorem ex33_i :
    (∀ i, ExtendedPseudoMetric RB none (gAbs i)) ∧ U33 ⊆ dom (1 : Operator RB none) ∧
    U33 ⊆ dom shiftOp ∧
    (fun y => Fib gAbs (1 : Operator RB none) {()} y U33) '' ((1 : Operator RB none).func '' U33) =
      {{some (-3)}, {some (-2), some 2}, {some 0}} ∧
    (fun y => Fib gAbs shiftOp {()} y U33) '' (shiftOp.func '' U33) =
      {{some (-3)}, {some (-2), some 0}, {some 2}} ∧
    IsPartitionOf U33 {{some (-3)}, {some (-2), some 2}, {some 0}} ∧
    IsPartitionOf U33 {{some (-3)}, {some (-2), some 0}, {some 2}} := by
  have hid : (fun y => Fib gAbs (1 : Operator RB none) {()} y U33) '' ((1 : Operator RB none).func '' U33) =
      {{some (-3)}, {some (-2), some 2}, {some 0}} := by
    rw [one_eq_realOp_id]
    simp only [U33, image_insert_eq, image_singleton, realOp_some, id]
    rw [← U33]
    simp only [fib33]
    have e1 : {x | x ∈ U33 ∧ ∃ s, x = some s ∧ |(|id s| - |(-3 : ℝ)|)| ≤ 0} = {some (-3)} := by
      ext x; simp only [mem_setOf_eq, mem_singleton_iff, mem_U33, id]
      constructor
      · rintro ⟨hx, s, rfl, hs⟩
        rcases hx with h | h | h | h <;> simp only [Option.some.injEq] at h <;> subst h <;> (revert hs; norm_num)
      · rintro rfl; exact ⟨Or.inl rfl, _, rfl, by norm_num⟩
    have e2 : ∀ t : ℝ, |t| = 2 → {x | x ∈ U33 ∧ ∃ s, x = some s ∧ |(|id s| - |t|)| ≤ 0} = {some (-2), some 2} := by
      intro t ht
      ext x; simp only [mem_setOf_eq, mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff, mem_U33, id, ht]
      constructor
      · rintro ⟨hx, s, rfl, hs⟩
        rcases hx with h | h | h | h <;> simp only [Option.some.injEq] at h <;> subst h <;> (revert hs; norm_num)
      · rintro (rfl | rfl)
        · exact ⟨Or.inr (Or.inl rfl), _, rfl, by norm_num⟩
        · exact ⟨Or.inr (Or.inr (Or.inr rfl)), _, rfl, by norm_num⟩
    have e3 : {x | x ∈ U33 ∧ ∃ s, x = some s ∧ |(|id s| - |(0 : ℝ)|)| ≤ 0} = {some 0} := by
      ext x; simp only [mem_setOf_eq, mem_singleton_iff, mem_U33, id]
      constructor
      · rintro ⟨hx, s, rfl, hs⟩
        rcases hx with h | h | h | h <;> simp only [Option.some.injEq] at h <;> subst h <;> (revert hs; norm_num)
      · rintro rfl; exact ⟨Or.inr (Or.inr (Or.inl rfl)), _, rfl, by norm_num⟩
    rw [e1, e2 (-2) (by norm_num), e2 2 (by norm_num), e3]
    ext S; simp only [mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff]; tauto
  have hsh : (fun y => Fib gAbs shiftOp {()} y U33) '' (shiftOp.func '' U33) =
      {{some (-3)}, {some (-2), some 0}, {some 2}} := by
    simp only [shiftOp, U33, image_insert_eq, image_singleton, realOp_some]
    rw [← U33]
    simp only [fib33]
    have e1 : {x | x ∈ U33 ∧ ∃ s, x = some s ∧ |(|s + 1| - |(-3 : ℝ) + 1|)| ≤ 0} = {some (-3)} := by
      ext x; simp only [mem_setOf_eq, mem_singleton_iff, mem_U33]
      constructor
      · rintro ⟨hx, s, rfl, hs⟩
        rcases hx with h | h | h | h <;> simp only [Option.some.injEq] at h <;> subst h <;> (revert hs; norm_num)
      · rintro rfl; exact ⟨Or.inl rfl, _, rfl, by norm_num⟩
    have e2 : ∀ t : ℝ, |t + 1| = 1 → {x | x ∈ U33 ∧ ∃ s, x = some s ∧ |(|s + 1| - |t + 1|)| ≤ 0} = {some (-2), some 0} := by
      intro t ht
      ext x; simp only [mem_setOf_eq, mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff, mem_U33, ht]
      constructor
      · rintro ⟨hx, s, rfl, hs⟩
        rcases hx with h | h | h | h <;> simp only [Option.some.injEq] at h <;> subst h <;> (revert hs; norm_num)
      · rintro (rfl | rfl)
        · exact ⟨Or.inr (Or.inl rfl), _, rfl, by norm_num⟩
        · exact ⟨Or.inr (Or.inr (Or.inl rfl)), _, rfl, by norm_num⟩
    have e3 : {x | x ∈ U33 ∧ ∃ s, x = some s ∧ |(|s + 1| - |(2 : ℝ) + 1|)| ≤ 0} = {some 2} := by
      ext x; simp only [mem_setOf_eq, mem_singleton_iff, mem_U33]
      constructor
      · rintro ⟨hx, s, rfl, hs⟩
        rcases hx with h | h | h | h <;> simp only [Option.some.injEq] at h <;> subst h <;> (revert hs; norm_num)
      · rintro rfl; exact ⟨Or.inr (Or.inr (Or.inr rfl)), _, rfl, by norm_num⟩
    rw [e1, e2 (-2) (by norm_num), e2 0 (by norm_num), e3]
    ext S; simp only [mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff]; tauto
  refine ⟨gAbs_epm, U33_sub_dom_one, U33_sub_dom_realOp _, hid, hsh, ?_, ?_⟩
  · rw [← hid]; exact (prop35_ii_b gAbs gAbs_epm _ U33 {()}).2
  · rw [← hsh]; exact (prop35_ii_b gAbs gAbs_epm _ U33 {()}).2

theorem ex33_image : shiftOp.func '' U33 = {some (-2), some (-1), some 1, some 3} := by
  simp only [shiftOp, U33, image_insert_eq, image_singleton, realOp_some]; norm_num

theorem ofReal_three_halves : ENNReal.ofReal (3 / 2) = 3 / 2 := by
  rw [ENNReal.ofReal_div_of_pos two_pos]; simp

/-- 习题 3.3(ii) -/
theorem ex33_ii :
    covering_number dAbs (ENNReal.ofReal (3 / 2)) U33 = 2 ∧
    covering_number dAbs (ENNReal.ofReal (3 / 2)) (shiftOp.func '' U33) = 1 ∧
    ∃ h : EntropyDiffDomain gAbs {()} {()} (ENNReal.ofReal (3 / 2)) shiftOp U33,
      mapEntropyDiff gAbs {()} {()} (ENNReal.ofReal (3 / 2)) shiftOp U33 h = 1 := by
  classical
  have h32 : (0 : ℝ) < 3 / 2 := by norm_num
  have hcU : covering_number dAbs (ENNReal.ofReal (3 / 2)) U33 = 2 := by
    apply le_antisymm
    · apply covering_number_le_two (some 0) (some 2)
      refine ⟨?_, fun x hx => ?_⟩
      · intro c hc; simp only [Finset.coe_insert, Finset.coe_singleton, mem_insert_iff,
          mem_singleton_iff] at hc
        rcases hc with rfl | rfl <;> simp [U33]
      · rw [mem_U33] at hx
        rcases hx with rfl | rfl | rfl | rfl
        · exact ⟨some 2, by simp, by rw [dAbs_lt_iff _ _ _ h32]; norm_num⟩
        · exact ⟨some 2, by simp, by rw [dAbs_lt_iff _ _ _ h32]; norm_num⟩
        · exact ⟨some 0, by simp, by rw [dAbs_lt_iff _ _ _ h32]; norm_num⟩
        · exact ⟨some 2, by simp, by rw [dAbs_lt_iff _ _ _ h32]; norm_num⟩
    · apply two_le_covering_number (x := some 0) (y := some 2) (by simp [U33]) (by simp [U33])
      intro c hc h1 h2
      rw [mem_U33] at hc
      rcases hc with rfl | rfl | rfl | rfl <;>
        rw [dAbs_lt_iff _ _ _ h32] at h1 h2 <;> revert h1 h2 <;> norm_num
  have hcI : covering_number dAbs (ENNReal.ofReal (3 / 2)) (shiftOp.func '' U33) = 1 := by
    rw [ex33_image]
    apply le_antisymm
    · apply covering_number_le_one (some (-2))
      refine ⟨by simp, fun x hx => ⟨some (-2), by simp, ?_⟩⟩
      simp only [mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at hx
      rcases hx with rfl | rfl | rfl | rfl <;> rw [dAbs_lt_iff _ _ _ h32] <;> norm_num
    · exact one_le_covering_number_of_nonempty _ _ _ ⟨some (-2), by simp⟩
  refine ⟨hcU, hcI, entropyDiffDomain_single gAbs () () _ shiftOp U33 ⟨some 0, by simp [U33]⟩
    (U33_sub_dom_realOp _) (by simpa using h32) ENNReal.ofReal_lt_top
    (Or.inl (by show covering_number dAbs _ U33 < ⊤; rw [hcU]; exact ENNReal.ofNat_lt_top)), ?_⟩
  rw [mapEntropyDiff_single]
  show log2_ereal (covering_number dAbs _ U33) - log2_ereal (covering_number dAbs _ _) = 1
  rw [hcU, hcI, log2_ereal_two, log2_ereal_one, sub_zero]

end OETCh03



namespace OETCh03

open formal_oet.vol_01.ch_01_foundations
open formal_oet.vol_01.ch_02_operator_bounds
open Set

/-! ### CH03-P29 习题 3.4 -/

/-- `f(x) = ⌊x⌋`，`f(⊥) = ⊥`。 -/
noncomputable def floorOp : Operator RB none := realOp (fun x => ((⌊x⌋ : ℤ) : ℝ))

@[simp] theorem floorOp_some (x : ℝ) : floorOp.func (some x) = some ((⌊x⌋ : ℤ) : ℝ) := rfl

def D1 : Set RB := some '' Icc 0 (1 / 2)
def D2 : Set RB := some '' Icc (9 / 10) (11 / 10)
def D3 : Set RB := some '' {0, 2}
def D4 : Set RB := some '' Icc 0 2

theorem diam_Icc (a b : ℝ) (hab : a ≤ b) : diam dR (some '' Icc a b) = ENNReal.ofReal (b - a) := by
  apply le_antisymm
  · refine iSup₂_le fun z hz => iSup₂_le fun w hw => ?_
    obtain ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩ := hz
    obtain ⟨y, hy, rfl⟩ := hw
    rw [dR_some]
    apply ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal
    rw [abs_le]; constructor <;> linarith [hx.1, hx.2, hy.1, hy.2]
  · have := le_diam dR (A := some '' Icc a b) (x := some b) (y := some a)
      ⟨b, ⟨hab, le_rfl⟩, rfl⟩ ⟨a, ⟨le_rfl, hab⟩, rfl⟩
    rwa [dR_some, abs_of_nonneg (by linarith)] at this

/-- 基点 `1` 左侧有 `[9/10, 1)` 时，`r^N(1|D) = 0`（不存在合法有限正半径）。 -/
theorem floor_radius_one_zero (D : Set ℝ) (hD : Ico (9 / 10 : ℝ) 1 ⊆ D) :
    nullRadius dR dR floorOp (some 1) (some '' D) = 0 := by
  apply le_antisymm _ (zero_le _)
  apply sSup_le
  rintro r ⟨h0, ht, hsub⟩
  exfalso
  obtain ⟨e, he, rfl⟩ := ofReal_eq_toReal h0 ht
  obtain ⟨m, hm0, hm1, hm2⟩ : ∃ m : ℝ, 0 < m ∧ m ≤ e / 2 ∧ m ≤ 1 / 10 :=
    ⟨min (e / 2) (1 / 10), lt_min (by linarith) (by norm_num), min_le_left _ _, min_le_right _ _⟩
  have hxD : 1 - m ∈ D := hD ⟨by linarith, by linarith⟩
  have hball : some (1 - m) ∈ ball dR (some 1) (ENNReal.ofReal e) ∩ some '' D := by
    refine ⟨?_, mem_image_of_mem _ hxD⟩
    show dR (some (1 - m)) (some 1) < ENNReal.ofReal e
    rw [dR_lt_iff _ _ _ he, show 1 - m - 1 = -m by ring, abs_neg, abs_of_pos hm0]; linarith
  have h := (hsub hball).2
  have hfl : ⌊(1 - m : ℝ)⌋ = 0 := Int.floor_eq_zero_iff.2 ⟨by linarith, by linarith⟩
  norm_num [hfl, ENNReal.ofReal_eq_zero] at h

theorem floor_D1 : ∀ x ∈ D1, floorOp.func x = some 0 := by
  rintro _ ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩
  simp [Int.floor_eq_zero_iff.2 ⟨hx.1, by linarith [hx.2]⟩]

theorem uNR_D1 : uNR dR dR floorOp D1 = ⊤ := by
  refine top_unique (le_uniform fun y hy => (nullRadius_eq_top_of_forall ?_).ge)
  intro r _ _ x hx
  exact ⟨hx.2, by rw [floor_D1 x hx.2, floor_D1 y hy]; exact dR_epm.dist_self _⟩

theorem uNR_zero_of (D : Set ℝ) (h1 : (1 : ℝ) ∈ D) (hD : Ico (9 / 10 : ℝ) 1 ⊆ D) :
    uNR dR dR floorOp (some '' D) = 0 :=
  le_antisymm ((uniform_le_nullRadius (mem_image_of_mem _ h1)).trans
    (floor_radius_one_zero D hD).le) (zero_le _)

theorem two_eq_ofReal : (2 : ENNReal) = ENNReal.ofReal 2 := by simp

theorem D3_radius (y : ℝ) (hy : y = 0 ∨ y = 2) : nullRadius dR dR floorOp (some y) D3 = 2 := by
  apply le_antisymm
  · apply sSup_le
    rintro r ⟨h0, ht, hsub⟩
    by_contra hlt
    rw [not_le] at hlt
    obtain ⟨e, he, rfl⟩ := ofReal_eq_toReal h0 ht
    rw [two_eq_ofReal, ENNReal.ofReal_lt_ofReal_iff he] at hlt
    have hz : some (2 - y) ∈ ball dR (some y) (ENNReal.ofReal e) ∩ D3 := by
      refine ⟨?_, ⟨2 - y, by rcases hy with rfl | rfl <;> norm_num, rfl⟩⟩
      show dR (some (2 - y)) (some y) < ENNReal.ofReal e
      rw [dR_lt_iff _ _ _ he]
      rcases hy with rfl | rfl <;> norm_num <;> linarith
    have h := (hsub hz).2
    rcases hy with rfl | rfl <;> norm_num [ENNReal.ofReal_eq_zero] at h
  · apply le_nullRadius
    refine ⟨two_pos, ENNReal.ofNat_lt_top, ?_⟩
    rintro _ ⟨hb, ⟨t, ht, rfl⟩⟩
    refine ⟨⟨t, ht, rfl⟩, ?_⟩
    have hb' : dR (some t) (some y) < ENNReal.ofReal 2 := by rw [← two_eq_ofReal]; exact hb
    rw [dR_lt_iff _ _ _ two_pos] at hb'
    have hty : t = y := by
      simp only [mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at ht
      rcases ht with rfl | rfl <;> rcases hy with rfl | rfl <;> revert hb' <;> norm_num
    rw [hty]; exact dR_epm.dist_self _

theorem uNR_D3 : uNR dR dR floorOp D3 = 2 := by
  apply le_antisymm
  · calc uNR dR dR floorOp D3 ≤ nullRadius dR dR floorOp (some 0) D3 :=
          uniform_le_nullRadius ⟨0, by simp, rfl⟩
      _ = 2 := D3_radius 0 (Or.inl rfl)
  · apply le_uniform
    rintro _ ⟨y, hy, rfl⟩
    rw [D3_radius y (by simpa using hy)]

theorem diam_D3 : diam dR D3 = 2 := by
  apply le_antisymm
  · refine iSup₂_le fun z hz => iSup₂_le fun w hw => ?_
    obtain ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩ := hz
    obtain ⟨y, hy, rfl⟩ := hw
    simp only [mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at hx hy
    rcases hx with rfl | rfl <;> rcases hy with rfl | rfl <;> norm_num
  · have := le_diam dR (A := D3) (x := some 2) (y := some 0) ⟨2, by simp, rfl⟩ ⟨0, by simp, rfl⟩
    simpa using this

/-- 习题 3.4(i-a) -/
theorem ex34_D1 :
    D1 ⊆ dom floorOp ∧ diam dR D1 = ENNReal.ofReal (1 / 2) ∧ uNR dR dR floorOp D1 = ⊤ ∧
    thresholdType (diamScale gR) 1 1 gR floorOp D1 () () = (1, 1) := by
  have hd : diam dR D1 = ENNReal.ofReal (1 / 2) := by
    rw [D1, diam_Icc 0 (1 / 2) (by norm_num)]; norm_num
  refine ⟨image_some_sub_dom_realOp _ _, hd, uNR_D1, ?_⟩
  apply (lemma313_cases gR (diamScale gR) 1 1 floorOp D1 () ()).1.2
  refine ⟨?_, ?_⟩
  · show diam dR D1 < 1; rw [hd]; exact ENNReal.ofReal_lt_one.2 (by norm_num)
  · show 1 ≤ uNR dR dR floorOp D1; rw [uNR_D1]; exact le_top

/-- 习题 3.4(i-b) -/
theorem ex34_D2 :
    D2 ⊆ dom floorOp ∧ diam dR D2 = ENNReal.ofReal (1 / 5) ∧ uNR dR dR floorOp D2 = 0 ∧
    thresholdType (diamScale gR) 1 1 gR floorOp D2 () () = (1, 0) := by
  have hd : diam dR D2 = ENNReal.ofReal (1 / 5) := by
    rw [D2, diam_Icc (9 / 10) (11 / 10) (by norm_num)]; norm_num
  have hu : uNR dR dR floorOp D2 = 0 :=
    uNR_zero_of _ ⟨by norm_num, by norm_num⟩ fun x hx => ⟨hx.1, by linarith [hx.2]⟩
  refine ⟨image_some_sub_dom_realOp _ _, hd, hu, ?_⟩
  apply (lemma313_cases gR (diamScale gR) 1 1 floorOp D2 () ()).2.1.2
  refine ⟨?_, ?_⟩
  · show diam dR D2 < 1; rw [hd]; exact ENNReal.ofReal_lt_one.2 (by norm_num)
  · show uNR dR dR floorOp D2 < 1; rw [hu]; exact one_pos

/-- 习题 3.4(i-c) -/
theorem ex34_D3 :
    D3 ⊆ dom floorOp ∧ diam dR D3 = 2 ∧ uNR dR dR floorOp D3 = 2 ∧
    thresholdType (diamScale gR) 1 1 gR floorOp D3 () () = (0, 1) := by
  refine ⟨image_some_sub_dom_realOp _ _, diam_D3, uNR_D3, ?_⟩
  apply (lemma313_cases gR (diamScale gR) 1 1 floorOp D3 () ()).2.2.1.2
  refine ⟨?_, ?_⟩
  · show 1 ≤ diam dR D3; rw [diam_D3]; exact one_le_two
  · show 1 ≤ uNR dR dR floorOp D3; rw [uNR_D3]; exact one_le_two

/-- 习题 3.4(i-d) -/
theorem ex34_D4 :
    D4 ⊆ dom floorOp ∧ diam dR D4 = 2 ∧ uNR dR dR floorOp D4 = 0 ∧
    thresholdType (diamScale gR) 1 1 gR floorOp D4 () () = (0, 0) := by
  have hd : diam dR D4 = 2 := by rw [D4, diam_Icc 0 2 (by norm_num)]; norm_num
  have hu : uNR dR dR floorOp D4 = 0 :=
    uNR_zero_of _ ⟨by norm_num, by norm_num⟩ fun x hx => ⟨by linarith [hx.1], by linarith [hx.2]⟩
  refine ⟨image_some_sub_dom_realOp _ _, hd, hu, ?_⟩
  apply (lemma313_cases gR (diamScale gR) 1 1 floorOp D4 () ()).2.2.2.1.2
  refine ⟨?_, ?_⟩
  · show 1 ≤ diam dR D4; rw [hd]; exact one_le_two
  · show uNR dR dR floorOp D4 < 1; rw [hu]; exact one_pos

/-- 习题 3.4(ii) -/
theorem ex34_ii :
    (¬ ∀ D : Set ℝ, D.Nonempty → 1 ≤ uNR dR dR floorOp (some '' D) →
        ∀ x ∈ D, ∀ y ∈ D, dR (floorOp.func (some x)) (floorOp.func (some y)) = 0) ∧
    uNR dR dR floorOp D3 = 2 ∧ dR (some 0) (some 2) = 2 ∧
    dR (floorOp.func (some 0)) (floorOp.func (some 2)) = 2 := by
  have hgap : dR (some 0) (some 2) = 2 := by norm_num
  have hout : dR (floorOp.func (some 0)) (floorOp.func (some 2)) = 2 := by norm_num
  refine ⟨fun H => ?_, uNR_D3, hgap, hout⟩
  have := H {0, 2} ⟨0, by simp⟩ (by rw [← D3, uNR_D3]; exact one_le_two) 0 (by simp) 2 (by simp)
  rw [hout] at this; exact two_ne_zero this

/-! ### CH03-P30 习题 3.5 -/

def U4 : Set RB := {some 0, some 1, some 2, some 3}

theorem mem_U4 {x : RB} : x ∈ U4 ↔ x = some 0 ∨ x = some 1 ∨ x = some 2 ∨ x = some 3 := by
  simp [U4]

/-- `f` 在 `U` 上为恒等，在 `X \ U` 上取 `⊥`。 -/
noncomputable def fPart (x : ℝ) : Option ℝ := if x = 0 ∨ x = 1 ∨ x = 2 ∨ x = 3 then some x else none

/-- `g`：`0 ↦ 0, 1 ↦ 3, 2 ↦ 1, 3 ↦ 2`，在 `X \ U` 上取 `⊥`。 -/
noncomputable def gPart (x : ℝ) : Option ℝ :=
  if x = 0 then some 0 else if x = 1 then some 3 else if x = 2 then some 1 else if x = 3 then some 2
  else none

noncomputable def f5 : Operator RB none := partialOp fPart
noncomputable def g5 : Operator RB none := partialOp gPart

theorem f5_fix : ∀ x ∈ U4, f5.func x = x := by
  intro x hx; rw [mem_U4] at hx
  rcases hx with rfl | rfl | rfl | rfl <;> norm_num [f5, fPart]

theorem g5_v0 : g5.func (some 0) = some 0 := by norm_num [g5, gPart]
theorem g5_v1 : g5.func (some 1) = some 3 := by norm_num [g5, gPart]
theorem g5_v2 : g5.func (some 2) = some 1 := by norm_num [g5, gPart]
theorem g5_v3 : g5.func (some 3) = some 2 := by norm_num [g5, gPart]

/-- 两个算子在 `X \ U` 上取 `⊥`，在 `U` 上有定义。 -/
theorem ex35_structure :
    ExtendedPseudoMetric RB none dR ∧ f5.func none = none ∧ g5.func none = none ∧
    (∀ x ∉ U4, f5.func x = none ∧ g5.func x = none) ∧ U4 ⊆ dom f5 ∧ U4 ⊆ dom g5 := by
  refine ⟨dR_epm, rfl, rfl, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro x hx
    cases x with
    | none => exact ⟨rfl, rfl⟩
    | some t =>
      rw [mem_U4] at hx
      simp only [Option.some.injEq, not_or] at hx
      obtain ⟨h0, h1, h2, h3⟩ := hx
      simp [f5, g5, fPart, gPart, h0, h1, h2, h3]
  · intro x hx; show f5.func x ≠ none; rw [f5_fix x hx]
    rw [mem_U4] at hx; rcases hx with rfl | rfl | rfl | rfl <;> simp
  · intro x hx; show g5.func x ≠ none
    rw [mem_U4] at hx; rcases hx with rfl | rfl | rfl | rfl <;> simp [g5_v0, g5_v1, g5_v2, g5_v3]

theorem U4_sep : ∀ x ∈ U4, ∀ y ∈ U4, x ≠ y → 1 ≤ dR x y := by
  intro x hx y hy hne
  rw [mem_U4] at hx hy
  rcases hx with rfl | rfl | rfl | rfl <;> rcases hy with rfl | rfl | rfl | rfl <;>
    revert hne <;> norm_num

theorem U4_finset_card : (({some 0, some 1, some 2, some 3} : Finset RB).card) = 4 := by
  rw [Finset.card_insert_of_notMem (by norm_num), Finset.card_insert_of_notMem (by norm_num),
    Finset.card_pair (by norm_num)]

/-- 习题 3.5(i) -/
theorem cov4 (e : ℝ) (he : 0 < e) :
    covering_number dR (ENNReal.ofReal e) U4 = if e ≤ 1 then 4 else if e ≤ 2 then 2 else 1 := by
  classical
  have hepos : 0 < ENNReal.ofReal e := ENNReal.ofReal_pos.2 he
  split_ifs with h1 h2
  · have hnet : InternalEpsilonNet dR (ENNReal.ofReal e) U4 {some 0, some 1, some 2, some 3} := by
      refine ⟨by simp [U4], fun x hx => ⟨x, ?_, by rw [dR_epm.dist_self]; exact hepos⟩⟩
      rw [mem_U4] at hx; rcases hx with rfl | rfl | rfl | rfl <;> simp
    apply le_antisymm
    · calc covering_number dR (ENNReal.ofReal e) U4 ≤ _ := covering_number_le_card hnet
        _ = 4 := by rw [U4_finset_card]; rfl
    · have h4 : (4 : ENNReal) = (({some 0, some 1, some 2, some 3} : Finset RB).card : ENNReal) := by
        rw [U4_finset_card]; rfl
      rw [h4]
      apply card_le_covering_number_of_isolated _ hnet.1
      intro x hx c hc hd
      by_contra hne
      have := U4_sep x (hnet.1 hx) c hc (Ne.symm hne)
      exact absurd (lt_of_le_of_lt this (lt_of_lt_of_le hd (ENNReal.ofReal_le_one.2 h1))) (lt_irrefl _)
  · apply le_antisymm
    · apply covering_number_le_two (some 0) (some 2)
      refine ⟨?_, fun x hx => ?_⟩
      · intro c hc
        simp only [Finset.coe_insert, Finset.coe_singleton, mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at hc
        rcases hc with rfl | rfl <;> simp [U4]
      · rw [mem_U4] at hx
        rcases hx with rfl | rfl | rfl | rfl
        · exact ⟨some 0, by simp, by rw [dR_lt_iff _ _ _ he]; norm_num; linarith⟩
        · exact ⟨some 0, by simp, by rw [dR_lt_iff _ _ _ he]; norm_num; linarith⟩
        · exact ⟨some 2, by simp, by rw [dR_lt_iff _ _ _ he]; norm_num; linarith⟩
        · exact ⟨some 2, by simp, by rw [dR_lt_iff _ _ _ he]; norm_num; linarith⟩
    · apply two_le_covering_number (x := some 0) (y := some 3) (by simp [U4]) (by simp [U4])
      intro c hc hc1 hc2
      rw [mem_U4] at hc
      rcases hc with rfl | rfl | rfl | rfl <;> rw [dR_lt_iff _ _ _ he] at hc1 hc2 <;>
        norm_num at hc1 hc2 <;> linarith
  · apply le_antisymm
    · apply covering_number_le_one (some 1)
      refine ⟨by simp [U4], fun x hx => ⟨some 1, by simp, ?_⟩⟩
      rw [mem_U4] at hx
      rcases hx with rfl | rfl | rfl | rfl <;> rw [dR_lt_iff _ _ _ he] <;> norm_num <;> linarith
    · exact one_le_covering_number_of_nonempty _ _ _ ⟨some 0, by simp [U4]⟩

/-- 习题 3.5(i) 的 `ε ∈ (0,+∞)`（`ENNReal`）表述。 -/
theorem cov4' (ε : ENNReal) (h0 : 0 < ε) (ht : ε < ⊤) :
    covering_number dR ε U4 = if ε ≤ 1 then 4 else if ε ≤ 2 then 2 else 1 := by
  obtain ⟨e, he, rfl⟩ := ofReal_eq_toReal h0 ht
  rw [cov4 e he]
  have h2 : ENNReal.ofReal e ≤ 2 ↔ e ≤ 2 := by
    rw [two_eq_ofReal, ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal_iff (by norm_num)]
  simp only [ENNReal.ofReal_le_one, h2]

/-- 习题 3.5(i) -/
theorem ex35_i_center :
    some (3 / 2) ∉ U4 ∧ (∀ x ∈ U4, dR x (some (3 / 2)) < ENNReal.ofReal (7 / 4)) ∧
    ¬ InternalEpsilonNet dR (ENNReal.ofReal (7 / 4)) U4 {some (3 / 2)} ∧
    covering_number dR (ENNReal.ofReal (7 / 4)) U4 = 2 := by
  have hn : some (3 / 2) ∉ U4 := by rw [mem_U4]; norm_num
  refine ⟨hn, ?_, fun h => hn (h.1 (by simp)), ?_⟩
  · intro x hx; rw [mem_U4] at hx
    rcases hx with rfl | rfl | rfl | rfl <;> rw [dR_lt_iff _ _ _ (by norm_num)] <;> norm_num
  · rw [cov4 _ (by norm_num), if_neg (by norm_num), if_pos (by norm_num)]

theorem f5_image : f5.func '' U4 = U4 := by
  ext y; constructor
  · rintro ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩; rw [f5_fix x hx]; exact hx
  · intro hy; exact ⟨y, hy, f5_fix y hy⟩

theorem g5_image : g5.func '' U4 = U4 := by
  simp only [U4, image_insert_eq, image_singleton, g5_v0, g5_v1, g5_v2, g5_v3]
  ext x; simp only [mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff]; tauto

theorem U4_finite : U4.Finite := by simp [U4]

theorem ex35_dE (ϕ : Operator RB none) (himg : ϕ.func '' U4 = U4) (hdom : U4 ⊆ dom ϕ)
    (ε : ENNReal) (h0 : 0 < ε) (ht : ε < ⊤) :
    ∃ h : EntropyDiffDomain gR {()} {()} ε ϕ U4, mapEntropyDiff gR {()} {()} ε ϕ U4 h = 0 := by
  have hne : U4.Nonempty := ⟨some 0, by simp [U4]⟩
  have hfin : covering_number dR ε U4 < ⊤ :=
    covering_number_lt_top_of_finite dR_epm.dist_self h0 U4_finite
  refine ⟨entropyDiffDomain_single gR () () ε ϕ U4 hne hdom h0 ht (Or.inl hfin), ?_⟩
  rw [mapEntropyDiff_single]
  show metric_entropy dR ε U4 - metric_entropy dR ε (ϕ.func '' U4) = 0
  rw [himg, metric_entropy_real hne hfin, ← EReal.coe_sub, sub_self, EReal.coe_zero]

/-- 习题 3.5(ii) -/
theorem ex35_ii :
    f5.func '' U4 = U4 ∧ g5.func '' U4 = U4 ∧
    (∀ ε : ENNReal, 0 < ε → ε < ⊤ →
      covering_number dR ε (f5.func '' U4) = covering_number dR ε U4 ∧
      covering_number dR ε (g5.func '' U4) = covering_number dR ε U4 ∧
      (∃ h : EntropyDiffDomain gR {()} {()} ε f5 U4, mapEntropyDiff gR {()} {()} ε f5 U4 h = 0) ∧
      (∃ h : EntropyDiffDomain gR {()} {()} ε g5 U4, mapEntropyDiff gR {()} {()} ε g5 U4 h = 0)) := by
  obtain ⟨-, -, -, -, hdf, hdg⟩ := ex35_structure
  refine ⟨f5_image, g5_image, fun ε h0 ht => ⟨by rw [f5_image], by rw [g5_image],
    ex35_dE f5 f5_image hdf ε h0 ht, ex35_dE g5 g5_image hdg ε h0 ht⟩⟩

theorem ofReal_le_three_mul {a b : ℝ} (h : a ≤ 3 * b) :
    ENNReal.ofReal a ≤ 3 * ENNReal.ofReal b := by
  rw [show (3 : ENNReal) = ENNReal.ofReal 3 by simp, ← ENNReal.ofReal_mul (by norm_num)]
  exact ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal h

/-- 习题 3.5(iii-a) -/
theorem ex35_lip :
    IsLeast {L : ENNReal | ∀ x ∈ U4, ∀ y ∈ U4, dR (f5.func x) (f5.func y) ≤ L * dR x y} 1 ∧
    IsLeast {L : ENNReal | ∀ x ∈ U4, ∀ y ∈ U4, dR (g5.func x) (g5.func y) ≤ L * dR x y} 3 := by
  refine ⟨⟨fun x hx y hy => by rw [f5_fix x hx, f5_fix y hy, one_mul], fun L hL => ?_⟩,
    ⟨fun x hx y hy => ?_, fun L hL => ?_⟩⟩
  · have := hL (some 1) (by simp [U4]) (some 0) (by simp [U4])
    rw [f5_fix _ (by simp [U4]), f5_fix _ (by simp [U4])] at this
    norm_num at this; exact this
  · rw [mem_U4] at hx hy
    rcases hx with rfl | rfl | rfl | rfl <;> rcases hy with rfl | rfl | rfl | rfl <;>
      simp only [g5_v0, g5_v1, g5_v2, g5_v3, dR_some] <;> apply ofReal_le_three_mul <;> norm_num
  · have := hL (some 1) (by simp [U4]) (some 0) (by simp [U4])
    rw [g5_v1, g5_v0] at this
    norm_num at this; exact this

theorem ofReal_seven_fourths : ENNReal.ofReal (7 / 4) = 7 / 4 := by
  rw [ENNReal.ofReal_div_of_pos (by norm_num)]; simp

/-- 习题 3.5(iii-b) -/
theorem ex35_iii_b :
    covering_number dR (ENNReal.ofReal (7 / 4)) (f5.func '' U4) ≤
      covering_number dR (ENNReal.ofReal (7 / 4) / 1) U4 ∧
    covering_number dR (ENNReal.ofReal (7 / 4) / 1) U4 = 2 ∧
    covering_number dR (ENNReal.ofReal (7 / 4)) (f5.func '' U4) = 2 ∧
    covering_number dR (ENNReal.ofReal (7 / 4)) (g5.func '' U4) ≤
      covering_number dR (ENNReal.ofReal (7 / 4) / 3) U4 ∧
    covering_number dR (ENNReal.ofReal (7 / 4) / 3) U4 = 4 ∧
    covering_number dR (ENNReal.ofReal (7 / 4)) (g5.func '' U4) = 2 := by
  obtain ⟨⟨hf, -⟩, ⟨hg, -⟩⟩ := ex35_lip
  have h74 : covering_number dR (ENNReal.ofReal (7 / 4)) U4 = 2 := (ex35_i_center).2.2.2
  have h712 : ENNReal.ofReal (7 / 4) / 3 = ENNReal.ofReal (7 / 12) := by
    rw [show (3 : ENNReal) = ENNReal.ofReal 3 by simp, ← ENNReal.ofReal_div_of_pos (by norm_num)]
    norm_num
  refine ⟨prop319_cover gR f5 U4 () () _ 1 one_pos ENNReal.one_lt_top hf, by rw [div_one, h74],
    by rw [f5_image, h74], prop319_cover gR g5 U4 () () _ 3 (by norm_num) ENNReal.ofNat_lt_top hg,
    ?_, by rw [g5_image, h74]⟩
  rw [h712, cov4 _ (by norm_num), if_pos (by norm_num)]

end OETCh03

提交来源

正常交卷；三种评分采用实际交付。

判定与主要理由

  • 计分依据见下方逐项评审。

原始逐项评审

CH03-D01

判定： pass

审核说明： 常值优先、恒等排除常值、有效取补集；全域与U域分开。

CH03-D02

判定： pass

审核说明： 实际U中对任意目标的严格零距纤维；复用冻结子族sup，零距与每分量零距等价。

CH03-D03

判定： pass

审核说明： Req点值相等与Rsim全族零距区分；actual image quotient及等价关系、代表类公式正确，mem_Rsim_iff_mem_Fib给纤维关系桥。

CH03-D04

判定： pass

审核说明： 有限正合法半径开球交U取sup；空sup0与top边界沿实际ENNReal定义；zeroSet_eq_Fib给解释桥。

CH03-D05

判定： pass

审核说明： A仅为取inf基点集，U仍为评估域；零化矩阵行j输出列i输入。

CH03-D06

判定： pass

审核说明： 尺度对非空子集单调，空值不用于数学断言，文字明确等价；直径特例与两个精确阈值位元正确。

CH03-D07

判定： pass

审核说明： 冻结内部有限中心定义及真实covering_number复用；有覆盖取最小、无覆盖top、空集0与非空≥1以及熵接口均已证。

CH03-D08

判定： pass

审核说明： EntropyDiffDomain完整原域及至少一端有限；域外不定义映射熵差，mapEntropyDiff_cases验证所有有限/top分支。

CH03-P01-01

判定： pass

审核说明： 零行上界、正输入对应coLip条目为0、恒等双向保距均由定义与三角/正乘积推出。

CH03-P01-02

判定： pass

审核说明： 零行上界、正输入对应coLip条目为0、恒等双向保距均由定义与三角/正乘积推出。

完整原答

I compiled the chapter into one file, Main.lean, and all 92 obligations in obligations.json are marked complete in the alignment below.

Verification, run in the given environment:

  • Build: lake env lean Main.lean exits 0 with no errors. The only warning is one try 'simp' instead of 'simpa' lint.
  • Axioms: every one of the 594 OETCh03.* constants, including the auxiliary ones, depends only on propext, Classical.choice and Quot.sound.
  • Forbidden features: there is no sorry, admit, new axiom, native_decide, implemented_by or extern.
  • Alignment check: all 92 IDs are covered exactly once, every dependency is a valid ID, and all 248 declarations it names exist in the compiled module.
  • Imports: only the frozen formal_oet ch01/ch02 modules (no import Mathlib), which pull in the fixed mathlib transitively. For D07 the alignment also cites frozen declarations from formal_oet/vol_01/ch_01_foundations/metric_entropy.lean, which that obligation explicitly allows.

Representation choices worth checking against the source:

  • Composition: ϕ₂∘ϕ₁ is the frozen operator monoid product ϕ₂ * ϕ₁.
  • Finite chains: these are the first n terms of ϕ : ℕ → Operator, indexed from 0, so ϕ m is step m+1. chainComp 0 is the identity.
  • ℝ⊔{⊥}: this is Option ℝ with none as ⊥.
  • Specific numbers: they appear as ENNReal.ofReal (3/2) and ENNReal.ofReal (7/4); the file proves these equal 3/2 and 7/4.
  • Mapping entropy difference: it is only defined when you pass a proof of EntropyDiffDomain, which contains all of the source's preconditions. So ∞−∞ is never given a value.
  • Input scale family: monotonicity is required only on nonempty sets. Its value on ∅ never appears in any statement.
  • More general statements: some theorems assume less than the text (for example, an unneeded nonemptiness condition is dropped). In every such case the result is stronger and implies the textbook version.

The mathematical content is from Zhe Li (Glen Li / glenzli), The Operator Evolution Theory, Vol. I, Ch. 3 (CC BY 4.0, https://glenzli.com/books/oet/). The Lean formalization is new.

/- MG-C003：卷1第3章（算子局部退化）形式化。数学内容来源 Zhe Li, The Operator Evolution Theory, Vol. I, Ch. 3 (CC BY 4.0, https://glenzli.com/books/oet/)。 -/
import formal_oet.vol_01.ch_01_foundations.gauge_space
import formal_oet.vol_01.ch_01_foundations.metric_entropy
import formal_oet.vol_01.ch_01_foundations.operator_space
import formal_oet.vol_01.ch_02_operator_bounds.lip_matrix
import formal_oet.vol_01.ch_02_operator_bounds.decoupling
import formal_oet.vol_01.ch_02_operator_bounds.single_channel

set_option linter.style.longLine false
set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false
set_option linter.unusedVariables false

namespace OETCh03

open formal_oet.vol_01.ch_01_foundations
open formal_oet.vol_01.ch_02_operator_bounds
open Set

variable {X I : Type} {bot : X}

/-! ### General helpers about extended pseudometrics and operators -/

theorem epm_zero_trans {d : X → X → ENNReal} (hd : ExtendedPseudoMetric X bot d)
    {x y z : X} (h1 : d x y = 0) (h2 : d y z = 0) : d x z = 0 :=
  le_antisymm (by simpa [h1, h2] using hd.triangle x y z) (zero_le _)

theorem epm_eq_of_zero_left {d : X → X → ENNReal} (hd : ExtendedPseudoMetric X bot d)
    {x x' : X} (h : d x x' = 0) (y : X) : d x y = d x' y := by
  apply le_antisymm
  · calc d x y ≤ d x x' + d x' y := hd.triangle _ _ _
      _ = d x' y := by rw [h, zero_add]
  · calc d x' y ≤ d x' x + d x y := hd.triangle _ _ _
      _ = d x y := by rw [hd.symm, h, zero_add]

theorem epm_eq_of_zero_right {d : X → X → ENNReal} (hd : ExtendedPseudoMetric X bot d)
    {y y' : X} (h : d y y' = 0) (x : X) : d x y = d x y' := by
  rw [hd.symm x y, hd.symm x y']; exact epm_eq_of_zero_left hd h x

@[simp] theorem mul_func (ϕ₂ ϕ₁ : Operator X bot) (x : X) :
    (ϕ₂ * ϕ₁).func x = ϕ₂.func (ϕ₁.func x) := rfl

@[simp] theorem one_func (x : X) : (1 : Operator X bot).func x = x := rfl

/-- 复合定义直接保证 -/
theorem mem_dom_mul {ϕ₂ ϕ₁ : Operator X bot} {x : X} (hx : x ∈ dom (ϕ₂ * ϕ₁)) :
    x ∈ dom ϕ₁ ∧ ϕ₁.func x ∈ dom ϕ₂ := by
  refine ⟨fun h => hx ?_, hx⟩
  change ϕ₂.func (ϕ₁.func x) = bot
  rw [h, ϕ₂.preserves_bot]

theorem image_subset_dom_of_subset_dom_mul {ϕ₂ ϕ₁ : Operator X bot} {U : Set X}
    (hU : U ⊆ dom (ϕ₂ * ϕ₁)) : ϕ₁.func '' U ⊆ dom ϕ₂ := by
  rintro _ ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩; exact (mem_dom_mul (hU hx)).2

/-- 子族复合伪度量为零当且仅当子族每个分量为零。 -/
theorem subfamily_pseudometric_eq_zero_iff (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (J : Set I) (a b : X) :
    subfamily_pseudometric d J a b = 0 ↔ ∀ j ∈ J, d j a b = 0 := by
  simp [subfamily_pseudometric, ENNReal.iSup_eq_zero]

theorem subfamily_pseudometric_self (d : I → X → X → ENNReal)
    (hd : ∀ i, ExtendedPseudoMetric X bot (d i)) (J : Set I) (a : X) :
    subfamily_pseudometric d J a a = 0 :=
  (subfamily_pseudometric_eq_zero_iff d J a a).2 fun j _ => (hd j).dist_self a

theorem subfamily_pseudometric_comm (d : I → X → X → ENNReal)
    (hd : ∀ i, ExtendedPseudoMetric X bot (d i)) (J : Set I) (a b : X) :
    subfamily_pseudometric d J a b = subfamily_pseudometric d J b a := by
  simp only [subfamily_pseudometric]; congr with j; exact (hd j.1).symm a b

theorem le_subfamily_pseudometric (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) {J : Set I} {j : I} (hj : j ∈ J)
    (a b : X) : d j a b ≤ subfamily_pseudometric d J a b :=
  le_iSup (fun k : {k // k ∈ J} => d k.1 a b) ⟨j, hj⟩

/-! ### CH03-D01 分量分划 -/

/-- 常值分量 `I_const(ϕ|U)`。 -/
def Iconst (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) : Set I :=
  {i | ∀ x ∈ U, ∀ y ∈ U, d i (ϕ.func x) (ϕ.func y) = 0}

/-- 恒等分量 `I_id(ϕ|U)`：不在常值分量中（常值优先），且逐点输出与输入零距。 -/
def Iid (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) : Set I :=
  {i | i ∉ Iconst d ϕ U ∧ ∀ x ∈ U, d i (ϕ.func x) x = 0}

/-- 有效分量 `I_act(ϕ|U) = I \ (I_const ∪ I_id)`（`I` 由全集 `univ` 表示）。 -/
def Iact (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) : Set I :=
  univ \ (Iconst d ϕ U ∪ Iid d ϕ U)

/-- 全域分划（`U = dom ϕ`，省略限制后缀）。 -/
abbrev IconstG (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (ϕ : Operator X bot) : Set I := Iconst d ϕ (dom ϕ)
abbrev IidG (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (ϕ : Operator X bot) : Set I := Iid d ϕ (dom ϕ)
abbrev IactG (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (ϕ : Operator X bot) : Set I := Iact d ϕ (dom ϕ)

theorem mem_Iact_iff {d : I → X → X → ENNReal} {ϕ : Operator X bot} {U : Set X} {i : I} :
    i ∈ Iact d ϕ U ↔ i ∉ Iconst d ϕ U ∧ ¬ ∀ x ∈ U, d i (ϕ.func x) x = 0 := by
  simp only [Iact, Iid, mem_diff, mem_univ, mem_union, mem_setOf_eq, true_and]; tauto

theorem not_mem_Iact_iff {d : I → X → X → ENNReal} {ϕ : Operator X bot} {U : Set X} {i : I} :
    i ∉ Iact d ϕ U ↔ i ∈ Iconst d ϕ U ∨ ∀ x ∈ U, d i (ϕ.func x) x = 0 := by
  rw [mem_Iact_iff]; tauto

/-- 三类分量两两不交且覆盖 `I`（分划的表示对应）。 -/
theorem component_partition (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) :
    Iconst d ϕ U ∪ Iid d ϕ U ∪ Iact d ϕ U = univ ∧
    Disjoint (Iconst d ϕ U) (Iid d ϕ U) ∧ Disjoint (Iconst d ϕ U) (Iact d ϕ U) ∧
    Disjoint (Iid d ϕ U) (Iact d ϕ U) := by
  refine ⟨?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · ext i; simp only [Iact, mem_union, mem_diff, mem_univ, true_and, iff_true]; tauto
  · rw [Set.disjoint_left]; intro i h1 h2; exact h2.1 h1
  · rw [Set.disjoint_left]; intro i h1 h2; exact h2.2 (Or.inl h1)
  · rw [Set.disjoint_left]; intro i h1 h2; exact h2.2 (Or.inr h1)

/-! ### CH03-D02 计算纤维 -/

/-- 计算纤维 `Fib_{ϕ,J}(y|U) = {x ∈ U | d_J(ϕ x, y) = 0}`，`d_J` 为第 1 章子族复合伪度量。 -/
noncomputable def Fib (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (ϕ : Operator X bot) (J : Set I) (y : X)
    (U : Set X) : Set X :=
  {x | x ∈ U ∧ subfamily_pseudometric d J (ϕ.func x) y = 0}

/-- 单分量计算纤维 `Fib_{ϕ,j}(y|U) = {x ∈ U | d_j(ϕ x, y) = 0}`。 -/
def Fib1 (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (ϕ : Operator X bot) (j : I) (y : X) (U : Set X) : Set X :=
  {x | x ∈ U ∧ d j (ϕ.func x) y = 0}

/-- 全域纤维 `Fib_{ϕ,J}(y)`（`U = dom ϕ`）。 -/
noncomputable abbrev FibG (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (ϕ : Operator X bot) (J : Set I) (y : X) :
    Set X := Fib d ϕ J y (dom ϕ)

theorem mem_Fib_iff {d : I → X → X → ENNReal} {ϕ : Operator X bot} {J : Set I} {y x : X}
    {U : Set X} : x ∈ Fib d ϕ J y U ↔ x ∈ U ∧ ∀ j ∈ J, d j (ϕ.func x) y = 0 := by
  simp [Fib, subfamily_pseudometric_eq_zero_iff]

/-- 单元素子族的纤维即单分量纤维（命题 3.5(v) 的单分量等式）。 -/
theorem Fib_singleton (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (ϕ : Operator X bot) (j : I) (y : X)
    (U : Set X) : Fib d ϕ {j} y U = Fib1 d ϕ j y U := by
  ext x; simp [mem_Fib_iff, Fib1]

/-! ### CH03-D03 点值/零距点对关系、像商、纤维预序 -/

/-- `R_=(ϕ|U) = {(x,y) ∈ U × U | ϕ x = ϕ y}`。 -/
def Req (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) : Set (X × X) :=
  {p | p.1 ∈ U ∧ p.2 ∈ U ∧ ϕ.func p.1 = ϕ.func p.2}

/-- `R_∼(ϕ|U) = {(x,y) ∈ U × U | ϕ x ∼_G ϕ y}`。 -/
def Rsim (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) : Set (X × X) :=
  {p | p.1 ∈ U ∧ p.2 ∈ U ∧ GaugeZeroRelated d (ϕ.func p.1) (ϕ.func p.2)}

/-- 第二类点对集是计算纤维的关系表达 -/
theorem mem_Rsim_iff_mem_Fib (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (hd : ∀ i, ExtendedPseudoMetric X bot (d i))
    (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) {x y : X} (hx : x ∈ U) (hy : y ∈ U) :
    (x, y) ∈ Rsim d ϕ U ↔ y ∈ Fib d ϕ univ (ϕ.func x) U := by
  simp only [Rsim, mem_setOf_eq, mem_Fib_iff, mem_univ, true_implies, GaugeZeroRelated]
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨-, -, h⟩; exact ⟨hy, fun j => by rw [(hd j).symm]; exact h j⟩
  · rintro ⟨-, h⟩; exact ⟨hx, hy, fun j => by rw [(hd j).symm]; exact h j⟩

theorem Req_subset_Rsim (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (hd : ∀ i, ExtendedPseudoMetric X bot (d i))
    (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) : Req ϕ U ⊆ Rsim d ϕ U := by
  rintro ⟨x, y⟩ ⟨hx, hy, h⟩
  exact ⟨hx, hy, fun i => by simp only at h ⊢; rw [h]; exact (hd i).dist_self _⟩

/-- 族零距关系的 setoid（由第 1 章引理 1.1 给出等价性）。 -/
def gaugeSetoid (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (hd : ∀ i, ExtendedPseudoMetric X bot (d i)) :
    Setoid X :=
  ⟨GaugeZeroRelated d, gauge_zero_related_equivalence d (fun i => (hd i).dist_self)
    (fun i => (hd i).symm) (fun i => (hd i).triangle)⟩

/-- 像商 `ϕ(U)/∼_G`：实际像集（子类型）按族零距关系取商。 -/
abbrev ImageQuot (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (hd : ∀ i, ExtendedPseudoMetric X bot (d i))
    (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) : Type :=
  Quotient ((gaugeSetoid d hd).comap (Subtype.val : ↥(ϕ.func '' U) → X))

/-- 像类 `[ϕ(x)] ∈ ϕ(U)/∼_G`（`x ∈ U`）。 -/
def imgCls (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (hd : ∀ i, ExtendedPseudoMetric X bot (d i))
    (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (x : X) (hx : x ∈ U) : ImageQuot d hd ϕ U :=
  Quotient.mk _ ⟨ϕ.func x, mem_image_of_mem _ hx⟩

theorem imgCls_eq_iff (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (hd : ∀ i, ExtendedPseudoMetric X bot (d i))
    (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) {x y : X} (hx : x ∈ U) (hy : y ∈ U) :
    imgCls d hd ϕ U x hx = imgCls d hd ϕ U y hy ↔ GaugeZeroRelated d (ϕ.func x) (ϕ.func y) :=
  Quotient.eq (r := (gaugeSetoid d hd).comap (Subtype.val : ↥(ϕ.func '' U) → X))

/-- 零测距纤维预序 `ϕA ⪰_{fiber,U} ϕB :⇔ R_∼(ϕA|U) ⊆ R_∼(ϕB|U)`。 -/
def fiberGE (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (U : Set X) (ϕA ϕB : Operator X bot) : Prop :=
  Rsim d ϕA U ⊆ Rsim d ϕB U

/-! ### CH03-D04 点态零化内切半径 -/

/-- 合法有限正半径集 `{r ∈ (0,+∞) | B_{d_in}(y,r) ∩ U ⊆ {x ∈ U | d_out(ϕ x, ϕ y) = 0}}`。 -/
def admRadii (din dout : X → X → ENNReal) (ϕ : Operator X bot) (y : X) (U : Set X) :
    Set ENNReal :=
  {r | 0 < r ∧ r < ⊤ ∧ ball din y r ∩ U ⊆ {x | x ∈ U ∧ dout (ϕ.func x) (ϕ.func y) = 0}}

/-- 零化内切半径 `r^N_{ϕ,d_in,d_out}(y|U) = sup(admRadii)`，`sup ∅ = 0`，可取 `+∞`。 -/
noncomputable def nullRadius (din dout : X → X → ENNReal) (ϕ : Operator X bot) (y : X)
    (U : Set X) : ENNReal :=
  sSup (admRadii din dout ϕ y U)

/-- 简写 `r^N_{ϕ,i,J}(y|U) := r^N_{ϕ,d_i,d_J}(y|U)`。 -/
noncomputable abbrev rNJ (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (ϕ : Operator X bot) (i : I) (J : Set I)
    (y : X) (U : Set X) : ENNReal :=
  nullRadius (d i) (subfamily_pseudometric d J) ϕ y U

/-- 简写 `r^N_{ϕ,i,j}(y|U) := r^N_{ϕ,d_i,d_j}(y|U)`。 -/
noncomputable abbrev rN (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (ϕ : Operator X bot) (i j : I) (y : X)
    (U : Set X) : ENNReal :=
  nullRadius (d i) (d j) ϕ y U

/-- 全域简写 `r^N_{ϕ,d_in,d_out}(y) := r^N(y | dom ϕ)`。 -/
noncomputable abbrev nullRadiusG (din dout : X → X → ENNReal) (ϕ : Operator X bot) (y : X) :
    ENNReal :=
  nullRadius din dout ϕ y (dom ϕ)

theorem rNJ_singleton (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (ϕ : Operator X bot) (i j : I) (y : X)
    (U : Set X) : rNJ d ϕ i {j} y U = rN d ϕ i j y U := by
  simp only [rNJ, rN, subfamily_pseudometric_singleton]

/-- 输出取 `d_J` 时，输出零测距集合就是计算纤维 `Fib_{ϕ,J}(ϕ y | U)`。 -/
theorem zeroSet_eq_Fib (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (ϕ : Operator X bot) (J : Set I) (y : X)
    (U : Set X) :
    {x | x ∈ U ∧ subfamily_pseudometric d J (ϕ.func x) (ϕ.func y) = 0} = Fib d ϕ J (ϕ.func y) U :=
  rfl

theorem zeroSet_eq_Fib1 (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (ϕ : Operator X bot) (j : I) (y : X)
    (U : Set X) :
    {x | x ∈ U ∧ d j (ϕ.func x) (ϕ.func y) = 0} = Fib1 d ϕ j (ϕ.func y) U := rfl

section NullRadiusCore
variable {din dout : X → X → ENNReal} {ϕ : Operator X bot} {y : X} {U : Set X}

theorem admRadii_mono {r r' : ENNReal} (hr : 0 < r) (hrr' : r ≤ r')
    (h : r' ∈ admRadii din dout ϕ y U) : r ∈ admRadii din dout ϕ y U := by
  refine ⟨hr, lt_of_le_of_lt hrr' h.2.1, fun x hx => h.2.2 ⟨lt_of_lt_of_le hx.1 hrr', hx.2⟩⟩

theorem le_nullRadius {r : ENNReal} (h : r ∈ admRadii din dout ϕ y U) :
    r ≤ nullRadius din dout ϕ y U := le_sSup h

/-- 严格小于点态上确界时，可提取合法有限正半径。 -/
theorem exists_adm_of_lt_nullRadius {a : ENNReal} (h : a < nullRadius din dout ϕ y U) :
    ∃ ρ ∈ admRadii din dout ϕ y U, a < ρ := by
  simpa using (lt_sSup_iff.1 h)

/-- 核心判据 -/
theorem zero_of_lt_nullRadius {x : X} (hx : x ∈ U) (h : din x y < nullRadius din dout ϕ y U) :
    dout (ϕ.func x) (ϕ.func y) = 0 := by
  obtain ⟨ρ, hρ, hlt⟩ := exists_adm_of_lt_nullRadius h
  exact (hρ.2.2 ⟨hlt, hx⟩).2

/-- 若所有严格小于 `M` 的有限正半径都合法，则 `M ≤ r^N`。 -/
theorem le_nullRadius_of_forall {M : ENNReal}
    (h : ∀ r : ENNReal, 0 < r → r < ⊤ → r < M →
      ball din y r ∩ U ⊆ {x | x ∈ U ∧ dout (ϕ.func x) (ϕ.func y) = 0}) :
    M ≤ nullRadius din dout ϕ y U := by
  apply le_of_forall_lt
  intro c hc
  obtain ⟨c', hcc', hc'M⟩ := exists_between hc
  have hpos : 0 < c' := lt_of_le_of_lt (zero_le _) hcc'
  have hfin : c' < ⊤ := lt_of_lt_of_le hc'M le_top
  exact lt_of_lt_of_le hcc' (le_nullRadius ⟨hpos, hfin, h c' hpos hfin hc'M⟩)

/-- 所有有限正半径均合法时 `r^N = +∞`。 -/
theorem nullRadius_eq_top_of_forall
    (h : ∀ r : ENNReal, 0 < r → r < ⊤ →
      ball din y r ∩ U ⊆ {x | x ∈ U ∧ dout (ϕ.func x) (ϕ.func y) = 0}) :
    nullRadius din dout ϕ y U = ⊤ :=
  top_unique (le_nullRadius_of_forall fun r h0 h1 _ => h r h0 h1)

end NullRadiusCore

/-! ### CH03-D05 统一零化半径与零化矩阵 -/

/-- 统一零化半径 `r̲^N_{ϕ,d_in,d_out}(A|U) = inf_{y∈A} r^N_{ϕ,d_in,d_out}(y|U)`。 -/
noncomputable def uniformNullRadius (din dout : X → X → ENNReal) (ϕ : Operator X bot)
    (A U : Set X) : ENNReal :=
  ⨅ y ∈ A, nullRadius din dout ϕ y U

/-- 简写 `r̲^N_{ϕ,d_in,d_out}|_U := r̲^N(U|U)`。 -/
noncomputable abbrev uNR (din dout : X → X → ENNReal) (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) :
    ENNReal := uniformNullRadius din dout ϕ U U

/-- 全域简写 `r̲^N_{ϕ,d_in,d_out} := r̲^N(dom ϕ | dom ϕ)`。 -/
noncomputable abbrev uNRG (din dout : X → X → ENNReal) (ϕ : Operator X bot) : ENNReal :=
  uniformNullRadius din dout ϕ (dom ϕ) (dom ϕ)

/-- 零化矩阵 `[N(ϕ)|_U]_{ji} := r̲^N_{ϕ,i,j}|_U`：第一个参数 `j` 为输出（行），第二个 `i` 为输入（列）。 -/
noncomputable def nullMatrix (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) :
    I → I → ENNReal :=
  fun j i => uNR (d i) (d j) ϕ U

/-- 全域零化矩阵 `N(ϕ)`。 -/
noncomputable abbrev nullMatrixG (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (ϕ : Operator X bot) : I → I → ENNReal :=
  nullMatrix d ϕ (dom ϕ)

theorem uniform_le_nullRadius {din dout : X → X → ENNReal} {ϕ : Operator X bot} {A U : Set X}
    {y : X} (hy : y ∈ A) : uniformNullRadius din dout ϕ A U ≤ nullRadius din dout ϕ y U :=
  iInf₂_le y hy

theorem le_uniform {din dout : X → X → ENNReal} {ϕ : Operator X bot} {A U : Set X} {M : ENNReal}
    (h : ∀ y ∈ A, M ≤ nullRadius din dout ϕ y U) : M ≤ uniformNullRadius din dout ϕ A U :=
  le_iInf₂ h

/-! ### CH03-D06 输入尺度函数族与阈值分类 -/

/-- 输入尺度函数族：`σ_i` 在非空子集上关于包含单调（空集上的取值不被使用）。 -/
structure InputScaleFamily (X I : Type) where
  σ : I → Set X → ENNReal
  mono : ∀ (i : I) (A₁ A₂ : Set X), A₁.Nonempty → A₁ ⊆ A₂ → σ i A₁ ≤ σ i A₂

/-- 直径 `diam_d(A) = sup_{x,y∈A} d(x,y)`。 -/
noncomputable def diam (d : X → X → ENNReal) (A : Set X) : ENNReal := ⨆ x ∈ A, ⨆ y ∈ A, d x y

theorem diam_mono (d : X → X → ENNReal) {A B : Set X} (h : A ⊆ B) : diam d A ≤ diam d B :=
  iSup₂_le fun x hx => iSup₂_le fun y hy => le_iSup₂_of_le x (h hx) (le_iSup₂_of_le y (h hy) le_rfl)

theorem le_diam (d : X → X → ENNReal) {A : Set X} {x y : X} (hx : x ∈ A) (hy : y ∈ A) :
    d x y ≤ diam d A := le_iSup₂_of_le x hx (le_iSup₂_of_le y hy le_rfl)

/-- 默认直径尺度 `σ_i = diam_{d_i}`，它是输入尺度函数族。 -/
noncomputable def diamScale (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) : InputScaleFamily X I where
  σ i A := diam (d i) A
  mono i _ _ _ h := diam_mono (d i) h

open Classical in
/-- 阈值分类 `Type_{f,A}(i,j) = (1{σ_i(A) < τσ}, 1{r̲^N_{f,i,j}|_A ≥ τN}) ∈ {0,1}²`。 -/
noncomputable def thresholdType (σ : InputScaleFamily X I) (τσ τN : ENNReal)
    (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (f : Operator X bot) (A : Set X) (i j : I) : Fin 2 × Fin 2 :=
  (if σ.σ i A < τσ then 1 else 0, if τN ≤ uNR (d i) (d j) f A then 1 else 0)

/-! ### CH03-D07 内部覆盖数、熵与全有界接口（复用第 1 章冻结定义） -/

/-- 全有界：`∀ r ∈ (0,+∞), N(r, U) < +∞`。 -/
def TotallyBoundedIn (d : X → X → ENNReal) (U : Set X) : Prop :=
  ∀ r : ENNReal, 0 < r → r < ⊤ → covering_number d r U < ⊤

theorem log2_ereal_mono {a b : ENNReal} (h : a ≤ b) : log2_ereal a ≤ log2_ereal b := by
  unfold log2_ereal
  by_cases ha0 : a = 0
  · simp [ha0]
  by_cases hbt : b = ⊤
  · simp [hbt]
  have hb0 : b ≠ 0 := fun hb => ha0 (le_antisymm (hb ▸ h) (zero_le _))
  have hat : a ≠ ⊤ := fun ha => hbt (top_unique (ha ▸ h))
  simp only [ha0, hat, hb0, hbt, if_false]
  exact EReal.coe_le_coe_iff.2 (Real.logb_le_logb_of_le one_lt_two
    (ENNReal.toReal_pos ha0 hat) ((ENNReal.toReal_le_toReal hat hbt).2 h))

theorem log2_ereal_natCast (n : ℕ) (hn : n ≠ 0) :
    log2_ereal (n : ENNReal) = ((Real.logb 2 n : ℝ) : EReal) := by
  simp [log2_ereal, hn]

theorem log2_ereal_one : log2_ereal 1 = 0 := by
  simp [log2_ereal]

theorem log2_ereal_lt_top {a : ENNReal} (h : a < ⊤) : log2_ereal a < ⊤ := by
  unfold log2_ereal
  split_ifs with h0
  · exact bot_lt_top
  · exact absurd ‹a = ⊤› h.ne
  · exact EReal.coe_lt_top _

theorem log2_ereal_eq_top_iff {a : ENNReal} : log2_ereal a = ⊤ ↔ a = ⊤ := by
  constructor
  · intro h; by_contra ha; exact (log2_ereal_lt_top (lt_top_iff_ne_top.2 ha)).ne h
  · intro h; simp [log2_ereal, h]

/-- 正有限覆盖数的熵是有限实数。 -/
theorem log2_ereal_real_of_pos_fin {a : ENNReal} (h0 : a ≠ 0) (ht : a ≠ ⊤) :
    log2_ereal a = ((Real.logb 2 a.toReal : ℝ) : EReal) := by
  simp [log2_ereal, h0, ht]

/-- D07 表示对应 -/
theorem covering_entropy_interface (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (i : I) (ε : ENNReal) (U : Set X) :
    ((∃ S : Finset X, InternalEpsilonNet (d i) ε U S) →
        ∃ S : Finset X, InternalEpsilonNet (d i) ε U S ∧ covering_number (d i) ε U = S.card ∧
          ∀ T : Finset X, InternalEpsilonNet (d i) ε U T → S.card ≤ T.card) ∧
    (covering_number (d i) ε U = ⊤ ↔ ¬ ∃ S : Finset X, InternalEpsilonNet (d i) ε U S) ∧
    covering_number (d i) ε (∅ : Set X) = 0 ∧
    (U.Nonempty → 1 ≤ covering_number (d i) ε U) ∧
    metric_entropy (d i) ε U = log2_ereal (covering_number (d i) ε U) ∧
    (U.Nonempty → 0 ≤ metric_entropy (d i) ε U) ∧
    metric_entropy (d i) ε (∅ : Set X) = ⊥ ∧
    subfamily_metric_entropy d {i} ε U = metric_entropy (d i) ε U ∧
    covering_number (subfamily_pseudometric d {i}) ε U = covering_number (d i) ε U := by
  refine ⟨covering_number_attained (d i) ε U, covering_number_eq_top_iff (d i) ε U,
    covering_number_empty (d i) ε, one_le_covering_number_of_nonempty (d i) ε U, rfl,
    metric_entropy_nonneg (d i) ε U, metric_entropy_empty (d i) ε,
    subfamily_metric_entropy_singleton d i ε U, by rw [subfamily_pseudometric_singleton]⟩

theorem covering_number_le_card {d : X → X → ENNReal} {ε : ENNReal} {U : Set X} {S : Finset X}
    (h : InternalEpsilonNet d ε U S) : covering_number d ε U ≤ S.card :=
  iInf_le_of_le S (iInf_le_of_le h le_rfl)

theorem covering_number_ne_zero {d : X → X → ENNReal} {ε : ENNReal} {U : Set X}
    (hU : U.Nonempty) : covering_number d ε U ≠ 0 :=
  (lt_of_lt_of_le zero_lt_one (one_le_covering_number_of_nonempty d ε U hU)).ne'

/-- 非空且覆盖数有限时，熵是有限实数。 -/
theorem metric_entropy_real {d : X → X → ENNReal} {ε : ENNReal} {U : Set X}
    (hU : U.Nonempty) (hfin : covering_number d ε U < ⊤) :
    metric_entropy d ε U = ((Real.logb 2 (covering_number d ε U).toReal : ℝ) : EReal) :=
  log2_ereal_real_of_pos_fin (covering_number_ne_zero hU) hfin.ne

theorem metric_entropy_mono_cov {d d' : X → X → ENNReal} {ε ε' : ENNReal} {U U' : Set X}
    (h : covering_number d ε U ≤ covering_number d' ε' U') :
    metric_entropy d ε U ≤ metric_entropy d' ε' U' := log2_ereal_mono h

/-! ### CH03-D08 映射熵差 -/

/-- 映射熵差的定义域：非空 `U ⊆ dom ϕ`、非空两端子族、有限正精度，且两端覆盖数至少一个有限
（从而排除 `+∞ - +∞`）。 -/
structure EntropyDiffDomain (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (Jin Jout : Set I) (ε : ENNReal)
    (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) : Prop where
  U_nonempty : U.Nonempty
  U_sub_dom : U ⊆ dom ϕ
  Jin_nonempty : Jin.Nonempty
  Jout_nonempty : Jout.Nonempty
  ε_pos : 0 < ε
  ε_lt_top : ε < ⊤
  finite_side : covering_number (subfamily_pseudometric d Jin) ε U < ⊤ ∨
    covering_number (subfamily_pseudometric d Jout) ε (ϕ.func '' U) < ⊤

/-- 映射熵差 `ΔE_{ε,J_in,J_out}(ϕ|U) = E_{ε,J_in}(U) - E_{ε,J_out}(ϕ(U)) ∈ [-∞,+∞]`，
仅在定义域证明 `h` 给出时定义。 -/
noncomputable def mapEntropyDiff (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (Jin Jout : Set I) (ε : ENNReal)
    (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (_h : EntropyDiffDomain d Jin Jout ε ϕ U) : EReal :=
  subfamily_metric_entropy d Jin ε U - subfamily_metric_entropy d Jout ε (ϕ.func '' U)

/-- 定义域内两端熵均非 `-∞`（非空），至多一个为 `+∞`；并验证 `[-∞,+∞]` 算术的三种情形。 -/
theorem mapEntropyDiff_cases (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (Jin Jout : Set I) (ε : ENNReal)
    (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (h : EntropyDiffDomain d Jin Jout ε ϕ U) :
    let Ein := subfamily_metric_entropy d Jin ε U
    let Eout := subfamily_metric_entropy d Jout ε (ϕ.func '' U)
    0 ≤ Ein ∧ 0 ≤ Eout ∧ ¬ (Ein = ⊤ ∧ Eout = ⊤) ∧
    (Ein = ⊤ → Eout < ⊤ → mapEntropyDiff d Jin Jout ε ϕ U h = ⊤) ∧
    (Ein < ⊤ → Eout = ⊤ → mapEntropyDiff d Jin Jout ε ϕ U h = ⊥) ∧
    (∀ a b : ℝ, Ein = a → Eout = b → mapEntropyDiff d Jin Jout ε ϕ U h = ((a - b : ℝ) : EReal)) := by
  intro Ein Eout
  have h0in : 0 ≤ Ein := metric_entropy_nonneg _ ε U h.U_nonempty
  have h0out : 0 ≤ Eout := metric_entropy_nonneg _ ε _ (h.U_nonempty.image _)
  refine ⟨h0in, h0out, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · rintro ⟨h1, h2⟩
    rcases h.finite_side with hf | hf
    · exact (log2_ereal_lt_top hf).ne h1
    · exact (log2_ereal_lt_top hf).ne h2
  · intro h1 h2
    show Ein - Eout = ⊤
    rw [h1]
    have hb : Eout ≠ ⊥ := ne_bot_of_le_ne_bot (by simp) h0out
    lift Eout to ℝ using ⟨h2.ne, hb⟩ with b
    simp
  · intro h1 h2
    show Ein - Eout = ⊥
    rw [h2]
    have hb : Ein ≠ ⊥ := ne_bot_of_le_ne_bot (by simp) h0in
    lift Ein to ℝ using ⟨h1.ne, hb⟩ with a
    simp
  · intro a b ha hb
    show Ein - Eout = _
    rw [ha, hb, EReal.coe_sub]

/-- 简写 `ΔE_{ε,i,j} := ΔE_{ε,{i},{j}}`，其两端熵即单分量熵。 -/
theorem mapEntropyDiff_single (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (i j : I) (ε : ENNReal)
    (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (h : EntropyDiffDomain d {i} {j} ε ϕ U) :
    mapEntropyDiff d {i} {j} ε ϕ U h = metric_entropy (d i) ε U - metric_entropy (d j) ε (ϕ.func '' U) := by
  simp [mapEntropyDiff, subfamily_metric_entropy_singleton]

/-- 单分量情形的定义域构造。 -/
theorem entropyDiffDomain_single (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (i j : I) (ε : ENNReal)
    (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (hU : U.Nonempty) (hUd : U ⊆ dom ϕ) (hε : 0 < ε)
    (hεt : ε < ⊤)
    (hfin : covering_number (d i) ε U < ⊤ ∨ covering_number (d j) ε (ϕ.func '' U) < ⊤) :
    EntropyDiffDomain d {i} {j} ε ϕ U :=
  ⟨hU, hUd, singleton_nonempty i, singleton_nonempty j, hε, hεt, by
    simpa [subfamily_pseudometric_singleton] using hfin⟩

/-! ### 有限复合链 -/

/-- `c_n = ϕ_{n-1} * ⋯ * ϕ_0`（0 起标号：`ϕ m` 是第 `m+1` 步），`c_0 = id`。 -/
def chainComp (ϕ : ℕ → Operator X bot) : ℕ → Operator X bot
  | 0 => 1
  | n + 1 => ϕ n * chainComp ϕ n

/-- 第 `m` 步（0 起标号）的实际输入域：`stepDom 0 = U`，`stepDom (m+1) = ϕ m (stepDom m)`。 -/
def stepDom (ϕ : ℕ → Operator X bot) (U : Set X) : ℕ → Set X
  | 0 => U
  | m + 1 => (ϕ m).func '' stepDom ϕ U m

theorem stepDom_eq_image (ϕ : ℕ → Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (n : ℕ) :
    stepDom ϕ U n = (chainComp ϕ n).func '' U := by
  induction n with
  | zero => simp [stepDom, chainComp]
  | succ n ih =>
    simp only [stepDom, chainComp, ih, image_image]; rfl

end OETCh03



namespace OETCh03

open formal_oet.vol_01.ch_01_foundations
open formal_oet.vol_01.ch_02_operator_bounds
open Set

variable {X I : Type} {bot : X}
variable (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (hd : ∀ i, ExtendedPseudoMetric X bot (d i))

theorem not_mem_Iconst_iff {ϕ : Operator X bot} {U : Set X} {i : I} :
    i ∉ Iconst d ϕ U ↔ ∃ x ∈ U, ∃ y ∈ U, d i (ϕ.func x) (ϕ.func y) ≠ 0 := by
  simp [Iconst]

/-! ### CH03-P01 命题 3.1 -/

/-- 命题 3.1(i) -/
theorem prop31_i [Fintype I] [DecidableEq I] (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (hU : U ⊆ dom ϕ)
    (i : I) (hi : i ∈ Iconst d ϕ U) :
    (∀ x ∈ U, ∀ y ∈ U, d i (ϕ.func x) (ϕ.func y) ≤ ∑ k : I, (0 : ENNReal) * d k x y) ∧
    (∀ x y : X, ∑ k : I, (0 : ENNReal) * d k x y = 0) ∧
    (∀ L : I → I → ENNReal, IsLipMatrix d ϕ U L →
      IsLipMatrix d ϕ U (fun k j => if j = i then 0 else L k j)) := by
  refine ⟨fun x hx y hy => by simp [hi x hx y hy], fun x y => by simp, fun L hL => ⟨hL.1, ?_⟩⟩
  intro x hx y hy j
  by_cases hj : j = i
  · subst hj; simp [hi x hx y hy]
  · simpa [hj] using hL.2 x hx y hy j

/-- 命题 3.1(ii) -/
theorem prop31_ii [Fintype I] (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (i : I)
    (hi : i ∈ Iconst d ϕ U) (K : I → I → ENNReal) (hK : IsCoLipMatrix d ϕ U K) (k : I)
    (hk : ∃ x ∈ U, ∃ y ∈ U, 0 < d k x y) : K k i = 0 := by
  obtain ⟨x, hx, y, hy, hpos⟩ := hk
  exact colip_entry_eq_zero_of_output_zero_input_pos d ϕ U K k i x y hK hx hy (hi x hx y hy) hpos

include hd in
/-- 命题 3.1(iii) -/
theorem prop31_iii (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (i : I) (hi : i ∈ Iid d ϕ U) :
    ∀ x ∈ U, ∀ y ∈ U, d i (ϕ.func x) (ϕ.func y) = d i x y := by
  intro x hx y hy
  rw [epm_eq_of_zero_left (hd i) (hi.2 x hx), epm_eq_of_zero_right (hd i) (hi.2 y hy)]

/-! ### CH03-P02 命题 3.2 -/

include hd in
/-- 命题 3.2(i) -/
theorem prop32_i (ϕ₁ ϕ₂ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (hU : U ⊆ dom (ϕ₂ * ϕ₁)) :
    Iact d (ϕ₂ * ϕ₁) U ⊆ Iact d ϕ₁ U ∪ Iact d ϕ₂ (ϕ₁.func '' U) := by
  intro i hi
  by_contra hcon
  simp only [mem_union, not_or] at hcon
  obtain ⟨h1, h2⟩ := hcon
  rw [not_mem_Iact_iff] at h1 h2
  apply hi.2
  rcases h2 with h2 | h2
  · -- 外侧常值（情形 (b)(d)）
    left
    intro x hx y hy
    exact h2 _ (mem_image_of_mem _ hx) _ (mem_image_of_mem _ hy)
  · rcases h1 with h1 | h1
    · -- 情形 (c)：内侧常值、外侧恒等
      left
      intro x hx y hy
      simp only [mul_func]
      rw [epm_eq_of_zero_left (hd i) (h2 _ (mem_image_of_mem _ hx)),
        epm_eq_of_zero_right (hd i) (h2 _ (mem_image_of_mem _ hy))]
      exact h1 x hx y hy
    · -- 情形 (a)：两步恒等 ⇒ 复合逐点零距，属于恒等或常值
      by_cases hc : i ∈ Iconst d (ϕ₂ * ϕ₁) U
      · left; exact hc
      · right
        refine ⟨hc, fun x hx => ?_⟩
        exact epm_zero_trans (hd i) (h2 _ (mem_image_of_mem _ hx)) (h1 x hx)

include hd in
/-- 零步复合 `c_0 = id` 没有有效分量。 -/
theorem Iact_chain_zero (ϕ : ℕ → Operator X bot) (U : Set X) :
    Iact d (chainComp ϕ 0) U = ∅ := by
  ext i
  simp only [mem_empty_iff_false, iff_false]
  rw [not_mem_Iact_iff]
  right; intro x _; exact (hd i).dist_self x

include hd in
/-- 命题 3.2(ii) -/
theorem prop32_ii (ϕ : ℕ → Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (n : ℕ)
    (hU : U ⊆ dom (chainComp ϕ n)) :
    Iact d (chainComp ϕ n) U ⊆ ⋃ m ∈ Finset.range n, Iact d (ϕ m) (stepDom ϕ U m) := by
  induction n with
  | zero => rw [Iact_chain_zero d hd]; exact empty_subset _
  | succ n ih =>
    have hU' : U ⊆ dom (chainComp ϕ n) := fun x hx => (mem_dom_mul (hU hx)).1
    intro i hi
    have := prop32_i d hd (chainComp ϕ n) (ϕ n) U hU hi
    rw [← stepDom_eq_image] at this
    rcases this with h | h
    · obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := mem_iUnion.1 (ih hU' h)
      obtain ⟨hm, hm'⟩ := mem_iUnion.1 hm
      exact mem_iUnion₂.2 ⟨m, Finset.mem_range.2 (lt_trans (Finset.mem_range.1 hm) n.lt_succ_self), hm'⟩
    · exact mem_iUnion₂.2 ⟨n, Finset.mem_range.2 n.lt_succ_self, h⟩

include hd in
/-- 命题 3.2(ii) 的换言之形式 -/
theorem prop32_ii_contra (ϕ : ℕ → Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (n : ℕ)
    (hU : U ⊆ dom (chainComp ϕ n)) (i : I)
    (h : ∀ m < n, i ∉ Iact d (ϕ m) (stepDom ϕ U m)) : i ∉ Iact d (chainComp ϕ n) U := by
  intro hi
  obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := mem_iUnion.1 (prop32_ii d hd ϕ U n hU hi)
  obtain ⟨hm, hm'⟩ := mem_iUnion.1 hm
  exact h m (Finset.mem_range.1 hm) hm'

include hd in
/-- 命题 3.2(iii) -/
theorem prop32_iii (ϕ₁ ϕ₂ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (hU : U ⊆ dom (ϕ₂ * ϕ₁)) :
    Iid d ϕ₁ U ∩ Iid d ϕ₂ (ϕ₁.func '' U) ⊆ Iid d (ϕ₂ * ϕ₁) U := by
  rintro i ⟨h1, h2⟩
  refine ⟨?_, fun x hx => epm_zero_trans (hd i) (h2.2 _ (mem_image_of_mem _ hx)) (h1.2 x hx)⟩
  rw [not_mem_Iconst_iff]
  obtain ⟨x, hx, y, hy, hne⟩ := (not_mem_Iconst_iff d).1 h1.1
  refine ⟨x, hx, y, hy, ?_⟩
  simp only [mul_func]
  rw [prop31_iii d hd ϕ₂ _ i h2 _ (mem_image_of_mem _ hx) _ (mem_image_of_mem _ hy)]
  exact hne

/-! ### CH03-P04-01 命题 3.3(i) -/

/-- 命题 3.3(i) -/
theorem prop33_i (ϕ ψ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (hU : U ⊆ dom (ψ * ϕ)) (i : I)
    (hi : i ∈ Iconst d ψ (ϕ.func '' U)) : i ∈ Iconst d (ψ * ϕ) U :=
  fun x hx y hy => hi _ (mem_image_of_mem _ hx) _ (mem_image_of_mem _ hy)

/-! ### CH03-P05-01 推论 3.4 -/

/-- 推论 3.4 -/
theorem cor34 [Fintype I] (ϕ ψ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (hU : U ⊆ dom (ψ * ϕ)) (j : I)
    (hj : j ∈ Iconst d ϕ U)
    (hL : ∃ L : I → I → ENNReal, IsLipMatrix d ψ (ϕ.func '' U) L ∧ ∀ k, k ≠ j → L k j = 0) :
    j ∈ Iconst d (ψ * ϕ) U := by
  obtain ⟨L, hL, hrow⟩ := hL
  intro x hx y hy
  have h := lip_bound_of_single_input_row d ψ (ϕ.func '' U) L j j hL hrow _
    (mem_image_of_mem _ hx) _ (mem_image_of_mem _ hy)
  rw [hj x hx y hy, mul_zero] at h
  exact le_antisymm h (zero_le _)

/-! ### CH03-P06 命题 3.5 -/

include hd in
/-- 命题 3.5(i) -/
theorem prop35_i (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (J : Set I) {y y' : X}
    (h : subfamily_pseudometric d J y y' = 0) : Fib d ϕ J y U = Fib d ϕ J y' U := by
  rw [subfamily_pseudometric_eq_zero_iff] at h
  ext x
  simp only [mem_Fib_iff]
  constructor
