  · rintro ⟨hx, hj⟩; exact ⟨hx, fun j hjJ => by rw [← epm_eq_of_zero_right (hd j) (h j hjJ)]; exact hj j hjJ⟩
  · rintro ⟨hx, hj⟩; exact ⟨hx, fun j hjJ => by rw [epm_eq_of_zero_right (hd j) (h j hjJ)]; exact hj j hjJ⟩

include hd in
/-- 命题 3.5(ii-a) -/
theorem prop35_ii_a (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (J : Set I) :
    (∀ y ∈ ϕ.func '' U, (Fib d ϕ J y U).Nonempty) ∧
    (∀ x ∈ U, x ∈ Fib d ϕ J (ϕ.func x) U) ∧
    (⋃ y ∈ ϕ.func '' U, Fib d ϕ J y U) = U := by
  have hself : ∀ x ∈ U, x ∈ Fib d ϕ J (ϕ.func x) U :=
    fun x hx => ⟨hx, subfamily_pseudometric_self d hd J _⟩
  refine ⟨?_, hself, ?_⟩
  · rintro _ ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩; exact ⟨x, hself x hx⟩
  · apply Subset.antisymm
    · intro x hx
      obtain ⟨y, hy⟩ := mem_iUnion.1 hx
      obtain ⟨_, hx'⟩ := mem_iUnion.1 hy
      exact hx'.1
    · intro x hx; exact mem_iUnion₂.2 ⟨_, mem_image_of_mem _ hx, hself x hx⟩

/-- `U` 的分划：成员非空、包含于 `U`，且 `U` 中每点恰属于一个成员。 -/
def IsPartitionOf (U : Set X) (F : Set (Set X)) : Prop :=
  (∀ S ∈ F, S.Nonempty ∧ S ⊆ U) ∧ ∀ x ∈ U, ∃! S, S ∈ F ∧ x ∈ S

include hd in
/-- 两个纤维或相等或不交（对任意目标成立）。 -/
theorem Fib_eq_or_disjoint (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (J : Set I) (y₁ y₂ : X) :
    Fib d ϕ J y₁ U = Fib d ϕ J y₂ U ∨ Disjoint (Fib d ϕ J y₁ U) (Fib d ϕ J y₂ U) := by
  by_cases h : ∃ x, x ∈ Fib d ϕ J y₁ U ∧ x ∈ Fib d ϕ J y₂ U
  · left
    obtain ⟨x, h1, h2⟩ := h
    apply prop35_i d hd ϕ U J
    rw [subfamily_pseudometric_eq_zero_iff]
    intro j hj
    have a := (mem_Fib_iff.1 h1).2 j hj
    have b := (mem_Fib_iff.1 h2).2 j hj
    rw [(hd j).symm] at a
    exact epm_zero_trans (hd j) a b
  · right
    rw [Set.disjoint_left]
    intro x h1 h2
    exact h ⟨x, h1, h2⟩

include hd in
/-- 命题 3.5(ii-b) -/
theorem prop35_ii_b (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (J : Set I) :
    (∀ y₁ ∈ ϕ.func '' U, ∀ y₂ ∈ ϕ.func '' U,
      Fib d ϕ J y₁ U = Fib d ϕ J y₂ U ∨ Disjoint (Fib d ϕ J y₁ U) (Fib d ϕ J y₂ U)) ∧
    IsPartitionOf U ((fun y => Fib d ϕ J y U) '' (ϕ.func '' U)) := by
  refine ⟨fun y₁ _ y₂ _ => Fib_eq_or_disjoint d hd ϕ U J y₁ y₂, ?_, ?_⟩
  · rintro S ⟨y, hy, rfl⟩
    exact ⟨(prop35_ii_a d hd ϕ U J).1 y hy, fun x hx => hx.1⟩
  · intro x hx
    refine ⟨Fib d ϕ J (ϕ.func x) U, ⟨⟨_, mem_image_of_mem _ hx, rfl⟩,
      (prop35_ii_a d hd ϕ U J).2.1 x hx⟩, ?_⟩
    rintro S ⟨⟨y, hy, rfl⟩, hxS⟩
    rcases Fib_eq_or_disjoint d hd ϕ U J y (ϕ.func x) with h | h
    · exact h
    · exact absurd ((prop35_ii_a d hd ϕ U J).2.1 x hx) (Set.disjoint_left.1 h hxS)

/-- 命题 3.5(iii) -/
theorem prop35_iii (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (J : Set I) (hJ : J.Nonempty) (y : X) :
    Fib d ϕ J y U = ⋂ j ∈ J, Fib1 d ϕ j y U := by
  ext x
  simp only [mem_Fib_iff, mem_iInter, Fib1, mem_setOf_eq]
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨hx, h⟩ j hj; exact ⟨hx, h j hj⟩
  · intro h
    obtain ⟨j₀, hj₀⟩ := hJ
    exact ⟨(h j₀ hj₀).1, fun j hj => (h j hj).2⟩

/-- 命题 3.5(iv) -/
theorem prop35_iv (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) {J K : Set I} (hJK : J ⊆ K) (y : X) :
    Fib d ϕ K y U ⊆ Fib d ϕ J y U := by
  intro x hx
  rw [mem_Fib_iff] at hx ⊢
  exact ⟨hx.1, fun j hj => hx.2 j (hJK hj)⟩

/-- 命题 3.5(v) -/
theorem prop35_v (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (y : X) (i : I) :
    Fib d ϕ {i} y U = Fib1 d ϕ i y U ∧
    Fib1 d ϕ i y U = {x | x ∈ U ∧ d i (ϕ.func x) y = 0} ∧
    Fib d ϕ univ y U ⊆ Fib1 d ϕ i y U := by
  refine ⟨Fib_singleton d ϕ i y U, rfl, ?_⟩
  rw [← Fib_singleton]; exact prop35_iv d ϕ U (subset_univ _) y

include hd in
/-- 命题 3.5(vi-a) -/
theorem prop35_vi_a (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (j : I) (hj : j ∈ Iid d ϕ U) (y : X) :
    Fib d ϕ {j} y U = {x | x ∈ U ∧ d j x y = 0} := by
  rw [Fib_singleton]
  ext x
  simp only [Fib1, mem_setOf_eq]
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨hx, h⟩; exact ⟨hx, by rw [← epm_eq_of_zero_left (hd j) (hj.2 x hx)]; exact h⟩
  · rintro ⟨hx, h⟩; exact ⟨hx, by rw [epm_eq_of_zero_left (hd j) (hj.2 x hx)]; exact h⟩

include hd in
/-- 命题 3.5(vi-b) -/
theorem prop35_vi_b (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (j : I) (hj : j ∈ Iconst d ϕ U)
    (y₀ : X) (hy₀ : y₀ ∈ ϕ.func '' U) :
    Fib d ϕ {j} y₀ U = U ∧ ∀ y : X, 0 < d j y y₀ → Fib d ϕ {j} y U = ∅ := by
  obtain ⟨x₀, hx₀, rfl⟩ := hy₀
  rw [Fib_singleton]
  refine ⟨Subset.antisymm (fun x hx => hx.1) (fun x hx => ⟨hx, hj x hx x₀ hx₀⟩), ?_⟩
  intro y hy
  rw [Fib_singleton, eq_empty_iff_forall_notMem]
  rintro x ⟨hx, hxy⟩
  have h1 : d j y (ϕ.func x) = 0 := by rw [(hd j).symm]; exact hxy
  exact hy.ne' (epm_zero_trans (hd j) h1 (hj x hx x₀ hx₀))

/-! ### CH03-P07 命题 3.6 -/

/-- 像值变换 `T : ϕA(U) → X` 满足 `T ∘ ϕA = ϕB`（在 `U` 上）。 -/
def IsImageFactor (ϕA ϕB : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (T : ↥(ϕA.func '' U) → X) : Prop :=
  ∀ x (hx : x ∈ U), T ⟨ϕA.func x, mem_image_of_mem _ hx⟩ = ϕB.func x

/-- 命题 3.6(i) 必要性。 -/
theorem prop36_nec (ϕA ϕB : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (T : ↥(ϕA.func '' U) → X)
    (hT : IsImageFactor ϕA ϕB U T) : Req ϕA U ⊆ Req ϕB U := by
  rintro ⟨x, y⟩ ⟨hx, hy, hxy⟩
  refine ⟨hx, hy, ?_⟩
  simp only at hxy ⊢
  rw [← hT x hx, ← hT y hy]
  congr 1
  exact Subtype.ext hxy

/-- 显式构造：对 `y ∈ ϕA(U)` 选代表元 `x ∈ U`，令 `T(y) = ϕB(x)`。 -/
noncomputable def factorMap (ϕA ϕB : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) : ↥(ϕA.func '' U) → X :=
  fun y => ϕB.func (Classical.choose y.2)

/-- 命题 3.6(i) 充分性 -/
theorem prop36_suff (ϕA ϕB : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (h : Req ϕA U ⊆ Req ϕB U) :
    IsImageFactor ϕA ϕB U (factorMap ϕA ϕB U) := by
  intro x hx
  have hc := Classical.choose_spec (mem_image_of_mem ϕA.func hx)
  exact (h (show (Classical.choose (mem_image_of_mem ϕA.func hx), x) ∈ Req ϕA U from ⟨hc.1, hx, hc.2⟩)).2.2

/-- 命题 3.6(i) 充分性的空集边界 -/
theorem prop36_suff_empty (ϕA ϕB : Operator X bot) :
    Req ϕA ∅ ⊆ Req ϕB ∅ ∧ IsImageFactor ϕA ϕB ∅ (factorMap ϕA ϕB ∅) ∧ IsEmpty ↥(ϕA.func '' ∅) := by
  refine ⟨fun p hp => absurd hp.1 (notMem_empty _), fun x hx => absurd hx (notMem_empty _), ?_⟩
  rw [image_empty]; exact Set.isEmpty_coe_sort.2 rfl

/-- 命题 3.6(i)。 -/
theorem prop36_i (ϕA ϕB : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (hU : U ⊆ dom ϕA ∩ dom ϕB) :
    (∃ T, IsImageFactor ϕA ϕB U T) ↔ Req ϕA U ⊆ Req ϕB U :=
  ⟨fun ⟨T, hT⟩ => prop36_nec ϕA ϕB U T hT, fun h => ⟨_, prop36_suff ϕA ϕB U h⟩⟩

/-- 命题 3.6(ii) -/
theorem prop36_ii (ϕA ϕB : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (hU : U ⊆ dom ϕA ∩ dom ϕB) :
    ((∃ T, IsImageFactor ϕA ϕB U T) ∧ (∃ T', IsImageFactor ϕB ϕA U T')) ↔
      Req ϕA U = Req ϕB U := by
  rw [prop36_i ϕA ϕB U hU, prop36_i ϕB ϕA U (fun x hx => ⟨(hU hx).2, (hU hx).1⟩)]
  exact ⟨fun ⟨a, b⟩ => Subset.antisymm a b, fun h => ⟨h.subset, h.symm.subset⟩⟩

/-- 命题 3.6(iii) -/
theorem prop36_iii (δ : X → X → ENNReal) (ϕA ϕB : Operator X bot) (U : Set X)
    (hU : U ⊆ dom ϕA ∩ dom ϕB) (T : ↥(ϕA.func '' U) → X) (hT : IsImageFactor ϕA ϕB U T)
    (KA LB : ENNReal) (hKA0 : 0 < KA) (hKAt : KA < ⊤) (hLBt : LB < ⊤)
    (hA : ∀ x ∈ U, ∀ x' ∈ U, KA * δ x x' ≤ δ (ϕA.func x) (ϕA.func x'))
    (hB : ∀ x ∈ U, ∀ x' ∈ U, δ (ϕB.func x) (ϕB.func x') ≤ LB * δ x x') :
    ∀ y y' : ↥(ϕA.func '' U), δ (T y) (T y') ≤ LB / KA * δ y.1 y'.1 := by
  rintro ⟨_, x, hx, rfl⟩ ⟨_, x', hx', rfl⟩
  rw [hT x hx, hT x' hx']
  have h1 : δ x x' ≤ δ (ϕA.func x) (ϕA.func x') / KA := by
    rw [ENNReal.le_div_iff_mul_le (Or.inl hKA0.ne') (Or.inl hKAt.ne), mul_comm]
    exact hA x hx x' hx'
  calc δ (ϕB.func x) (ϕB.func x') ≤ LB * δ x x' := hB x hx x' hx'
    _ ≤ LB * (δ (ϕA.func x) (ϕA.func x') / KA) := by gcongr
    _ = LB / KA * δ (ϕA.func x) (ϕA.func x') := by
      simp only [div_eq_mul_inv]; ring

/-- 命题 3.6(iii) 的矩阵形式 -/
theorem prop36_iii_matrix [Fintype I] [Subsingleton I] (i : I) (ϕA ϕB : Operator X bot)
    (U : Set X) (hU : U ⊆ dom ϕA ∩ dom ϕB) (T : ↥(ϕA.func '' U) → X)
    (hT : IsImageFactor ϕA ϕB U T) (K L : I → I → ENNReal) (hK : IsCoLipMatrix d ϕA U K)
    (hL : IsLipMatrix d ϕB U L) (hK0 : 0 < K i i) (hKt : K i i < ⊤) (hLt : L i i < ⊤) :
    ∀ y y' : ↥(ϕA.func '' U), d i (T y) (T y') ≤ L i i / K i i * d i y.1 y'.1 := by
  have hsum : ∀ (M : I → I → ENNReal) (x x' : X), ∑ k : I, M k i * d k x x' = M i i * d i x x' := by
    intro M x x'
    rw [Finset.sum_eq_single i (fun k _ hk => absurd (Subsingleton.elim k i) hk)
      (fun h => absurd (Finset.mem_univ i) h)]
  refine prop36_iii (d i) ϕA ϕB U hU T hT (K i i) (L i i) hK0 hKt hLt ?_ ?_
  · intro x hx x' hx'; have := hK.2.2 x hx x' hx' i; rw [hsum] at this; exact this
  · intro x hx x' hx'; exact single_component_lip_matrix_ineq d ϕB U L i hL x hx x' hx'

/-! ### CH03-P08 命题 3.7 -/

/-- 商映射 `T̄ : ϕA(U)/∼ → ϕB(U)/∼` 满足规定赋值公式 `T̄[ϕA x] = [ϕB x]`。 -/
def IsQuotFactor (ϕA ϕB : Operator X bot) (U : Set X)
    (Tb : ImageQuot d hd ϕA U → ImageQuot d hd ϕB U) : Prop :=
  ∀ x (hx : x ∈ U), Tb (imgCls d hd ϕA U x hx) = imgCls d hd ϕB U x hx

/-- 代表元无关的商映射构造（需 `R_∼(ϕA|U) ⊆ R_∼(ϕB|U)`）。 -/
noncomputable def quotFactorMap (ϕA ϕB : Operator X bot) (U : Set X)
    (h : Rsim d ϕA U ⊆ Rsim d ϕB U) : ImageQuot d hd ϕA U → ImageQuot d hd ϕB U :=
  Quotient.lift (fun y : ↥(ϕA.func '' U) =>
      imgCls d hd ϕB U (Classical.choose y.2) (Classical.choose_spec y.2).1)
    (by
      rintro ⟨y, hy⟩ ⟨y', hy'⟩ hyy'
      apply (imgCls_eq_iff d hd ϕB U _ _).2
      have h1 := Classical.choose_spec hy
      have h2 := Classical.choose_spec hy'
      have hmem : (Classical.choose hy, Classical.choose hy') ∈ Rsim d ϕA U := by
        refine ⟨h1.1, h2.1, ?_⟩
        simp only
        rw [h1.2, h2.2]
        exact hyy'
      exact (h hmem).2.2)

theorem quotFactorMap_spec (ϕA ϕB : Operator X bot) (U : Set X)
    (h : Rsim d ϕA U ⊆ Rsim d ϕB U) :
    IsQuotFactor d hd ϕA ϕB U (quotFactorMap d hd ϕA ϕB U h) := by
  intro x hx
  have hx' : ϕA.func x ∈ ϕA.func '' U := mem_image_of_mem _ hx
  have hc := Classical.choose_spec hx'
  show imgCls d hd ϕB U (Classical.choose hx') hc.1 = imgCls d hd ϕB U x hx
  apply (imgCls_eq_iff d hd ϕB U _ _).2
  have : (Classical.choose hx', x) ∈ Rsim d ϕA U :=
    Req_subset_Rsim d hd ϕA U ⟨hc.1, hx, hc.2⟩
  exact (h this).2.2

/-- 命题 3.7 单向 -/
theorem prop37_one (ϕA ϕB : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (hU : U ⊆ dom ϕA ∩ dom ϕB) :
    (∃ Tb, IsQuotFactor d hd ϕA ϕB U Tb) ↔ Rsim d ϕA U ⊆ Rsim d ϕB U := by
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨Tb, hTb⟩ ⟨x, y⟩ ⟨hx, hy, hxy⟩
    refine ⟨hx, hy, ?_⟩
    have e1 : imgCls d hd ϕA U x hx = imgCls d hd ϕA U y hy := (imgCls_eq_iff d hd ϕA U hx hy).2 hxy
    have e2 := congrArg Tb e1
    rw [hTb x hx, hTb y hy] at e2
    exact (imgCls_eq_iff d hd ϕB U hx hy).1 e2
  · intro h; exact ⟨_, quotFactorMap_spec d hd ϕA ϕB U h⟩

/-- 命题 3.7 双向 -/
theorem prop37_two (ϕA ϕB : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (hU : U ⊆ dom ϕA ∩ dom ϕB) :
    ((∃ Tb, IsQuotFactor d hd ϕA ϕB U Tb) ∧ (∃ Tb', IsQuotFactor d hd ϕB ϕA U Tb')) ↔
      Rsim d ϕA U = Rsim d ϕB U := by
  rw [prop37_one d hd ϕA ϕB U hU, prop37_one d hd ϕB ϕA U (fun x hx => ⟨(hU hx).2, (hU hx).1⟩)]
  exact ⟨fun ⟨a, b⟩ => Subset.antisymm a b, fun h => ⟨h.subset, h.symm.subset⟩⟩

/-- 规范族在集合 `S` 上分离：`S` 内全分量零距蕴含点相等。 -/
def SeparatingOn (S : Set X) : Prop :=
  ∀ y ∈ S, ∀ y' ∈ S, GaugeZeroRelated d y y' → y = y'

include hd in
theorem Req_eq_Rsim_of_sep (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X)
    (hsep : SeparatingOn d (ϕ.func '' U)) : Req ϕ U = Rsim d ϕ U := by
  apply Subset.antisymm (Req_subset_Rsim d hd ϕ U)
  rintro ⟨x, y⟩ ⟨hx, hy, h⟩
  exact ⟨hx, hy, hsep _ (mem_image_of_mem _ hx) _ (mem_image_of_mem _ hy) h⟩

/-- 命题 3.7 末段 -/
theorem prop37_sep (ϕA ϕB : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (hU : U ⊆ dom ϕA ∩ dom ϕB)
    (hsepA : SeparatingOn d (ϕA.func '' U)) (hsepB : SeparatingOn d (ϕB.func '' U)) :
    Req ϕA U = Rsim d ϕA U ∧ Req ϕB U = Rsim d ϕB U ∧
    ((∃ T, IsImageFactor ϕA ϕB U T) ↔ ∃ Tb, IsQuotFactor d hd ϕA ϕB U Tb) ∧
    (((∃ T, IsImageFactor ϕA ϕB U T) ∧ (∃ T', IsImageFactor ϕB ϕA U T')) ↔
      ((∃ Tb, IsQuotFactor d hd ϕA ϕB U Tb) ∧ (∃ Tb', IsQuotFactor d hd ϕB ϕA U Tb'))) := by
  have eA := Req_eq_Rsim_of_sep d hd ϕA U hsepA
  have eB := Req_eq_Rsim_of_sep d hd ϕB U hsepB
  refine ⟨eA, eB, ?_, ?_⟩
  · rw [prop36_i ϕA ϕB U hU, prop37_one d hd ϕA ϕB U hU, eA, eB]
  · rw [prop36_ii ϕA ϕB U hU, prop37_two d hd ϕA ϕB U hU, eA, eB]

/-! ### CH03-P09 纤维预序的传递 -/

theorem fiberGE_refl (U : Set X) (ϕ : Operator X bot) : fiberGE d U ϕ ϕ := subset_rfl

theorem fiberGE_trans (U : Set X) {ϕ ψ χ : Operator X bot} (h1 : fiberGE d U ϕ ψ)
    (h2 : fiberGE d U ψ χ) : fiberGE d U ϕ χ := subset_trans h1 h2

/-- 固定公共域时 `⪰_{fiber,U}` 是预序（自反、传递）。 -/
instance fiberGE_isPreorder (U : Set X) : IsPreorder (Operator X bot) (fiberGE d U) where
  refl := fiberGE_refl d U
  trans _ _ _ := fiberGE_trans d U

/-- 有限链 `ϕ_0, …, ϕ_m` 的逐步包含推出任意两层 `a < b ≤ m` 的包含。 -/
theorem fiber_chain (U : Set X) (ϕ : ℕ → Operator X bot) (m : ℕ)
    (h : ∀ r, 1 ≤ r → r ≤ m → fiberGE d U (ϕ (r - 1)) (ϕ r)) :
    ∀ a b, a < b → b ≤ m → fiberGE d U (ϕ a) (ϕ b) := by
  have key : ∀ a b, a ≤ b → b ≤ m → fiberGE d U (ϕ a) (ϕ b) := by
    intro a b hab
    induction b, hab using Nat.le_induction with
    | base => intro _; exact fiberGE_refl d U _
    | succ b hab ih =>
      intro hb
      exact fiberGE_trans d U (ih (by omega)) (by simpa using h (b + 1) (by omega) hb)
  exact fun a b hab hbm => key a b hab.le hbm

/-- 链中某一步真包含推出端点真包含，并给出差集见证 `R_r \ R_{r-1} ⊆ R_m \ R_0`。 -/
theorem fiber_chain_strict (U : Set X) (ϕ : ℕ → Operator X bot) (m : ℕ)
    (h : ∀ r, 1 ≤ r → r ≤ m → fiberGE d U (ϕ (r - 1)) (ϕ r)) (r : ℕ) (hr1 : 1 ≤ r)
    (hrm : r ≤ m) (hs : Rsim d (ϕ (r - 1)) U ⊂ Rsim d (ϕ r) U) :
    (Rsim d (ϕ r) U \ Rsim d (ϕ (r - 1)) U ⊆ Rsim d (ϕ m) U \ Rsim d (ϕ 0) U) ∧
    (Rsim d (ϕ r) U \ Rsim d (ϕ (r - 1)) U).Nonempty ∧
    Rsim d (ϕ 0) U ⊂ Rsim d (ϕ m) U := by
  have hle : ∀ a b, a ≤ b → b ≤ m → fiberGE d U (ϕ a) (ϕ b) := by
    intro a b hab hbm
    rcases hab.lt_or_eq with hlt | heq
    · exact fiber_chain d U ϕ m h a b hlt hbm
    · subst heq; exact fiberGE_refl d U _
  have h0 : Rsim d (ϕ 0) U ⊆ Rsim d (ϕ (r - 1)) U := hle 0 (r - 1) (Nat.zero_le _) (by omega)
  have hm : Rsim d (ϕ r) U ⊆ Rsim d (ϕ m) U := hle r m hrm le_rfl
  have hdiff : Rsim d (ϕ r) U \ Rsim d (ϕ (r - 1)) U ⊆ Rsim d (ϕ m) U \ Rsim d (ϕ 0) U :=
    fun p hp => ⟨hm hp.1, fun h0p => hp.2 (h0 h0p)⟩
  have hne : (Rsim d (ϕ r) U \ Rsim d (ϕ (r - 1)) U).Nonempty := by
    obtain ⟨p, hp1, hp2⟩ := Set.exists_of_ssubset hs
    exact ⟨p, hp1, hp2⟩
  refine ⟨hdiff, hne, ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨p, hp⟩ := hne
  exact ssubset_of_subset_of_ne (hle 0 m (Nat.zero_le _) (by omega))
    (fun heq => (hdiff hp).2 (heq ▸ (hdiff hp).1))

end OETCh03



namespace OETCh03

open formal_oet.vol_01.ch_01_foundations
open formal_oet.vol_01.ch_02_operator_bounds
open Set

variable {X I : Type} {bot : X}
variable (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (hd : ∀ i, ExtendedPseudoMetric X bot (d i))

/-! ### CH03-P11 命题 3.8 -/

/-- 命题 3.8(i-a) -/
theorem prop38_i_a (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (hU : U.Nonempty) (hUd : U ⊆ dom ϕ)
    (J : Set I) (hJ : J.Nonempty) (hJc : J ⊆ Iconst d ϕ U) :
    ∀ y ∈ U, Fib d ϕ J (ϕ.func y) U = U := by
  intro y hy
  apply Subset.antisymm (fun x hx => hx.1)
  intro x hx
  rw [mem_Fib_iff]
  exact ⟨hx, fun j hj => hJc hj x hx y hy⟩

/-- 命题 3.8(i-b) -/
theorem prop38_i_b (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (hU : U.Nonempty) (hUd : U ⊆ dom ϕ)
    (J : Set I) (hJ : J.Nonempty) (hJc : J ⊆ Iconst d ϕ U) (i : I) :
    (∀ y ∈ U, rNJ d ϕ i J y U = ⊤) ∧
    uNR (d i) (subfamily_pseudometric d J) ϕ U = ⊤ := by
  have hpt : ∀ y ∈ U, rNJ d ϕ i J y U = ⊤ := by
    intro y hy
    apply nullRadius_eq_top_of_forall
    intro r _ _ x hx
    rw [zeroSet_eq_Fib, prop38_i_a d ϕ U hU hUd J hJ hJc y hy]
    exact hx.2
  exact ⟨hpt, top_unique (le_uniform fun y hy => (hpt y hy).ge)⟩

/-! ### CH03-P12 命题 3.9 -/

include hd in
/-- 命题 3.9(i) -/
theorem prop39_i (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (hUd : U ⊆ dom ϕ) (y : X) (hy : y ∈ U)
    (i : I) (hi : i ∈ Iid d ϕ U) :
    Fib1 d ϕ i (ϕ.func y) U = {x | x ∈ U ∧ d i x y = 0} := by
  ext x
  simp only [Fib1, mem_setOf_eq]
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨hx, h⟩; exact ⟨hx, by rwa [← prop31_iii d hd ϕ U i hi x hx y hy]⟩
  · rintro ⟨hx, h⟩; exact ⟨hx, by rwa [prop31_iii d hd ϕ U i hi x hx y hy]⟩

include hd in
/-- 命题 3.9(ii) -/
theorem prop39_ii (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (hUd : U ⊆ dom ϕ) (y : X) (hy : y ∈ U)
    (i : I) (hi : i ∈ Iid d ϕ U) (r₀ : ENNReal) (hr0 : 0 < r₀) (hr0t : r₀ < ⊤)
    (hball : ball (d i) y r₀ ∩ U ⊆ {x | x ∈ U ∧ d i x y = 0}) :
    r₀ ≤ rN d ϕ i i y U ∧ 0 < rN d ϕ i i y U := by
  have hadm : r₀ ∈ admRadii (d i) (d i) ϕ y U := by
    refine ⟨hr0, hr0t, ?_⟩
    rw [zeroSet_eq_Fib1, prop39_i d hd ϕ U hUd y hy i hi]
    exact hball
  exact ⟨le_nullRadius hadm, lt_of_lt_of_le hr0 (le_nullRadius hadm)⟩

/-! ### CH03-P13 命题 3.10 -/

/-- 目标子族合法当且仅当对每个分量合法（由分量交集律）。 -/
theorem admRadii_subfamily (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (i : I) (J : Set I)
    (hJ : J.Nonempty) (y : X) (r : ENNReal) :
    r ∈ admRadii (d i) (subfamily_pseudometric d J) ϕ y U ↔
      0 < r ∧ r < ⊤ ∧ ∀ j ∈ J, r ∈ admRadii (d i) (d j) ϕ y U := by
  simp only [admRadii, mem_setOf_eq]
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨h0, ht, h⟩
    refine ⟨h0, ht, fun j hj => ⟨h0, ht, fun x hx => ?_⟩⟩
    have := h hx
    exact ⟨this.1, (subfamily_pseudometric_eq_zero_iff d J _ _).1 this.2 j hj⟩
  · rintro ⟨h0, ht, h⟩
    refine ⟨h0, ht, fun x hx => ⟨hx.2, (subfamily_pseudometric_eq_zero_iff d J _ _).2
      fun j hj => ((h j hj).2.2 hx).2⟩⟩

/-- 命题 3.10（点态） -/
theorem prop310_pointwise (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (i : I) (J : Set I)
    (hJ : J.Nonempty) (y : X) :
    rNJ d ϕ i J y U = ⨅ j ∈ J, rN d ϕ i j y U := by
  apply le_antisymm
  · refine le_iInf₂ fun j hj => sSup_le_sSup fun r hr => ?_
    exact ((admRadii_subfamily d ϕ U i J hJ y r).1 hr).2.2 j hj
  · apply le_of_forall_lt
    intro c hc
    obtain ⟨c', hcc', hc'⟩ := exists_between hc
    have hpos : 0 < c' := lt_of_le_of_lt (zero_le _) hcc'
    have hfin : c' < ⊤ := lt_of_lt_of_le hc' le_top
    have hadm : c' ∈ admRadii (d i) (subfamily_pseudometric d J) ϕ y U := by
      refine (admRadii_subfamily d ϕ U i J hJ y c').2 ⟨hpos, hfin, fun j hj => ?_⟩
      obtain ⟨ρ, hρ, hlt⟩ := exists_adm_of_lt_nullRadius (lt_of_lt_of_le hc' (iInf₂_le j hj))
      exact admRadii_mono hpos hlt.le hρ
    exact lt_of_lt_of_le hcc' (le_nullRadius hadm)

/-- 命题 3.10（统一） -/
theorem prop310_uniform (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (hU : U.Nonempty) (hUd : U ⊆ dom ϕ)
    (i : I) (J : Set I) (hJ : J.Nonempty) :
    uNR (d i) (subfamily_pseudometric d J) ϕ U = ⨅ j ∈ J, nullMatrix d ϕ U j i := by
  simp only [uNR, uniformNullRadius, nullMatrix]
  simp_rw [show ∀ y, nullRadius (d i) (subfamily_pseudometric d J) ϕ y U = ⨅ j ∈ J, rN d ϕ i j y U
    from prop310_pointwise d ϕ U i J hJ]
  exact iInf₂_comm _

/-- 有限非空子族上的下确界是可取到的最小值。 -/
theorem iInf_finite_attained [Finite I] (J : Set I) (hJ : J.Nonempty) (f : I → ENNReal) :
    ∃ j ∈ J, ⨅ j' ∈ J, f j' = f j := by
  obtain ⟨j, hj, hmin⟩ := Set.exists_min_image J f (Set.toFinite J) hJ
  exact ⟨j, hj, le_antisymm (iInf₂_le j hj) (le_iInf₂ hmin)⟩

/-- 命题 3.10（反单调） -/
theorem prop310_anti (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (hU : U.Nonempty) (hUd : U ⊆ dom ϕ)
    (i : I) {J K : Set I} (hJ : J.Nonempty) (hJK : J ⊆ K) :
    uNR (d i) (subfamily_pseudometric d K) ϕ U ≤ uNR (d i) (subfamily_pseudometric d J) ϕ U := by
  rw [prop310_uniform d ϕ U hU hUd i J hJ, prop310_uniform d ϕ U hU hUd i K (hJ.mono hJK)]
  exact biInf_mono fun j hj => hJK hj

/-! ### CH03-P14 命题 3.11 -/

include hd in
/-- 命题 3.11（纤维成员条件）。 -/
theorem prop311_fib (ϕ₂ : Operator X bot) (U₂ : Set X) (hU₂ : U₂ ⊆ dom ϕ₂) (j : I) {y y' : X}
    (hy : y ∈ U₂) (hy' : y' ∈ U₂) (h : y' ∈ Fib1 d ϕ₂ j (ϕ₂.func y) U₂) :
    d j (ϕ₂.func y) (ϕ₂.func y') = 0 := by
  rw [(hd j).symm]; exact h.2

include hd in
/-- 命题 3.11（半径条件） -/
theorem prop311_radius (ϕ₂ : Operator X bot) (U₂ : Set X) (hU₂ : U₂ ⊆ dom ϕ₂) (i j : I)
    {y y' : X} (hy : y ∈ U₂) (hy' : y' ∈ U₂) (h : d i y y' < rN d ϕ₂ i j y U₂) :
    d j (ϕ₂.func y) (ϕ₂.func y') = 0 := by
  obtain ⟨ρ, hρ, hlt⟩ := exists_adm_of_lt_nullRadius h
  have hmem : y' ∈ ball (d i) y ρ ∩ U₂ := ⟨by rw [ball, mem_setOf_eq, (hd i).symm]; exact hlt, hy'⟩
  exact prop311_fib d hd ϕ₂ U₂ hU₂ j hy hy' (hρ.2.2 hmem)

include hd in
/-- 命题 3.11（复合实例） -/
theorem prop311_comp (ϕ₁ ϕ₂ : Operator X bot) (U₂ : Set X) (hU₂ : U₂ ⊆ dom ϕ₂) (i j : I)
    {x x' : X} (hx : x ∈ dom ϕ₁) (hx' : x' ∈ dom ϕ₁) (h1 : ϕ₁.func x ∈ U₂)
    (h2 : ϕ₁.func x' ∈ U₂) :
    (ϕ₁.func x' ∈ Fib1 d ϕ₂ j (ϕ₂.func (ϕ₁.func x)) U₂ →
      d j ((ϕ₂ * ϕ₁).func x) ((ϕ₂ * ϕ₁).func x') = 0) ∧
    (d i (ϕ₁.func x) (ϕ₁.func x') < rN d ϕ₂ i j (ϕ₁.func x) U₂ →
      d j ((ϕ₂ * ϕ₁).func x) ((ϕ₂ * ϕ₁).func x') = 0) :=
  ⟨fun h => prop311_fib d hd ϕ₂ U₂ hU₂ j h1 h2 h,
    fun h => prop311_radius d hd ϕ₂ U₂ hU₂ i j h1 h2 h⟩

/-! ### CH03-P15 命题 3.12 -/

/-- 命题 3.12(i) -/
theorem prop312_i (ϕ₁ ϕ₂ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (hU : U.Nonempty)
    (hUd : U ⊆ dom (ϕ₂ * ϕ₁)) (i j l : I)
    (hpres : ∀ y ∈ ϕ₁.func '' U, ∀ y' ∈ ϕ₁.func '' U, d l y y' = 0 →
      d j (ϕ₂.func y) (ϕ₂.func y') = 0) :
    nullMatrix d ϕ₁ U l i ≤ nullMatrix d (ϕ₂ * ϕ₁) U j i := by
  apply le_uniform
  intro x hx
  apply le_nullRadius_of_forall
  intro r _ _ hrM x' hx'
  have hlt : d i x' x < nullRadius (d i) (d l) ϕ₁ x U :=
    lt_of_lt_of_le (lt_of_lt_of_le hx'.1 hrM.le) (uniform_le_nullRadius hx)
  have h0 := zero_of_lt_nullRadius hx'.2 hlt
  exact ⟨hx'.2, hpres _ (mem_image_of_mem _ hx'.2) _ (mem_image_of_mem _ hx) h0⟩

/-- 命题 3.12(ii) -/
theorem prop312_ii (ϕ₁ ϕ₂ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (hU : U.Nonempty)
    (hUd : U ⊆ dom (ϕ₂ * ϕ₁)) (i j l : I) (L : ENNReal) (hL0 : 0 < L) (hLt : L < ⊤)
    (hLip : ∀ x ∈ U, ∀ x' ∈ U, d l (ϕ₁.func x) (ϕ₁.func x') ≤ L * d i x x') :
    nullMatrix d ϕ₂ (ϕ₁.func '' U) j l / L ≤ nullMatrix d (ϕ₂ * ϕ₁) U j i := by
  apply le_uniform
  intro x hx
  apply le_nullRadius_of_forall
  intro r _ _ hrM x' hx'
  have h1 : d i x' x * L < nullMatrix d ϕ₂ (ϕ₁.func '' U) j l := by
    have := ENNReal.mul_lt_mul_left hL0.ne' hLt.ne (lt_trans hx'.1 hrM)
    rwa [ENNReal.div_mul_cancel hL0.ne' hLt.ne] at this
  have h2 : d l (ϕ₁.func x') (ϕ₁.func x) < nullRadius (d l) (d j) ϕ₂ (ϕ₁.func x) (ϕ₁.func '' U) :=
    calc d l (ϕ₁.func x') (ϕ₁.func x) ≤ L * d i x' x := hLip x' hx'.2 x hx
      _ = d i x' x * L := mul_comm _ _
      _ < _ := h1
      _ ≤ _ := uniform_le_nullRadius (mem_image_of_mem _ hx)
  have h3 := zero_of_lt_nullRadius (mem_image_of_mem ϕ₁.func hx'.2) h2
  exact ⟨hx'.2, h3⟩

/-- 命题 3.12(ii) 的 `+∞` 端点 -/
theorem prop312_ii_top (ϕ₁ ϕ₂ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (hU : U.Nonempty)
    (hUd : U ⊆ dom (ϕ₂ * ϕ₁)) (i j l : I) (L : ENNReal) (hL0 : 0 < L) (hLt : L < ⊤)
    (hLip : ∀ x ∈ U, ∀ x' ∈ U, d l (ϕ₁.func x) (ϕ₁.func x') ≤ L * d i x x')
    (htop : nullMatrix d ϕ₂ (ϕ₁.func '' U) j l = ⊤) : nullMatrix d (ϕ₂ * ϕ₁) U j i = ⊤ := by
  have := prop312_ii d ϕ₁ ϕ₂ U hU hUd i j l L hL0 hLt hLip
  rw [htop, ENNReal.top_div_of_ne_top hLt.ne] at this
  exact top_unique this

/-! ### CH03-P16 引理 3.13 -/

section Threshold
variable (σ : InputScaleFamily X I) (τσ τN : ENNReal) (f : Operator X bot) (A : Set X)

theorem thresholdType_fst_eq_one (i j : I) :
    (thresholdType σ τσ τN d f A i j).1 = 1 ↔ σ.σ i A < τσ := by
  unfold thresholdType; by_cases h : σ.σ i A < τσ <;> simp [h]

theorem thresholdType_snd_eq_one (i j : I) :
    (thresholdType σ τσ τN d f A i j).2 = 1 ↔ τN ≤ uNR (d i) (d j) f A := by
  unfold thresholdType; by_cases h : τN ≤ uNR (d i) (d j) f A <;> simp [h]

/-- 引理 3.13 -/
theorem lemma313_cases (i j : I) :
    (thresholdType σ τσ τN d f A i j = (1, 1) ↔ σ.σ i A < τσ ∧ τN ≤ uNR (d i) (d j) f A) ∧
    (thresholdType σ τσ τN d f A i j = (1, 0) ↔ σ.σ i A < τσ ∧ uNR (d i) (d j) f A < τN) ∧
    (thresholdType σ τσ τN d f A i j = (0, 1) ↔ τσ ≤ σ.σ i A ∧ τN ≤ uNR (d i) (d j) f A) ∧
    (thresholdType σ τσ τN d f A i j = (0, 0) ↔ τσ ≤ σ.σ i A ∧ uNR (d i) (d j) f A < τN) ∧
    thresholdType σ τσ τN d f A i j ∈ ({(1, 1), (1, 0), (0, 1), (0, 0)} : Set (Fin 2 × Fin 2)) := by
  unfold thresholdType
  by_cases h1 : σ.σ i A < τσ <;> by_cases h2 : τN ≤ uNR (d i) (d j) f A <;>
    simp [h1, h2, not_lt.1, not_le.1]

/-- 引理 3.13 -/
theorem lemma313_complement (i j : I) :
    (¬ σ.σ i A < τσ ↔ τσ ≤ σ.σ i A) ∧ (¬ τN ≤ uNR (d i) (d j) f A ↔ uNR (d i) (d j) f A < τN) ∧
    ((σ.σ i A < τσ ∧ τN ≤ uNR (d i) (d j) f A) ∨ (σ.σ i A < τσ ∧ uNR (d i) (d j) f A < τN) ∨
      (τσ ≤ σ.σ i A ∧ τN ≤ uNR (d i) (d j) f A) ∨ (τσ ≤ σ.σ i A ∧ uNR (d i) (d j) f A < τN)) := by
  refine ⟨not_lt, not_le, ?_⟩
  rcases lt_or_ge (σ.σ i A) τσ with h1 | h1 <;> rcases le_or_gt τN (uNR (d i) (d j) f A) with h2 | h2
  · exact Or.inl ⟨h1, h2⟩
  · exact Or.inr (Or.inl ⟨h1, h2⟩)
  · exact Or.inr (Or.inr (Or.inl ⟨h1, h2⟩))
  · exact Or.inr (Or.inr (Or.inr ⟨h1, h2⟩))

/-- 引理 3.13(i) -/
theorem lemma313_i {τσ' : ENNReal} (hτ : τσ ≤ τσ') :
    {p : I × I | (thresholdType σ τσ τN d f A p.1 p.2).1 = 1} ⊆
      {p : I × I | (thresholdType σ τσ' τN d f A p.1 p.2).1 = 1} := by
  intro p hp
  simp only [mem_setOf_eq, thresholdType_fst_eq_one] at hp ⊢
  exact lt_of_lt_of_le hp hτ

/-- 引理 3.13(ii) -/
theorem lemma313_ii {τN' : ENNReal} (hτ : τN ≤ τN') :
    {p : I × I | (thresholdType σ τσ τN' d f A p.1 p.2).2 = 1} ⊆
      {p : I × I | (thresholdType σ τσ τN d f A p.1 p.2).2 = 1} := by
  intro p hp
  simp only [mem_setOf_eq, thresholdType_snd_eq_one] at hp ⊢
  exact le_trans hτ hp

end Threshold

/-! ### CH03-P17 命题 3.14 -/

/-- 命题 3.14(i) -/
theorem prop314_i (σ : InputScaleFamily X I) (f : Operator X bot) {A B : Set X}
    (hB : B.Nonempty) (hBA : B ⊆ A) (hA : A ⊆ dom f) (i : I) (τσ : ENNReal) :
    σ.σ i B ≤ σ.σ i A ∧ (σ.σ i A < τσ → σ.σ i B < τσ) :=
  ⟨σ.mono i B A hB hBA, fun h => lt_of_le_of_lt (σ.mono i B A hB hBA) h⟩

/-- 命题 3.14(ii-a) -/
theorem prop314_ii_a (f : Operator X bot) {A B : Set X} (hB : B.Nonempty) (hBA : B ⊆ A)
    (hA : A ⊆ dom f) (i j : I) (x : X) (hx : x ∈ B) :
    rN d f i j x A ≤ rN d f i j x B := by
  apply sSup_le_sSup
  rintro r ⟨h0, ht, h⟩
  exact ⟨h0, ht, fun z hz => ⟨hz.2, (h ⟨hz.1, hBA hz.2⟩).2⟩⟩

/-- 命题 3.14(ii-b) -/
theorem prop314_ii_b (f : Operator X bot) {A B : Set X} (hB : B.Nonempty) (hBA : B ⊆ A)
    (hA : A ⊆ dom f) (i j : I) :
    uNR (d i) (d j) f A ≤ uNR (d i) (d j) f B := by
  apply le_uniform
  intro x hx
  exact le_trans (uniform_le_nullRadius (hBA hx)) (prop314_ii_a d f hB hBA hA i j x hx)

/-! ### CH03-P18 命题 3.15 -/

include hd in
/-- 命题 3.15(i) -/
theorem prop315_i (f : Operator X bot) (A : Set X) (hA : A.Nonempty) (hAd : A ⊆ dom f)
    (i j : I) {x y : X} (hx : x ∈ A) (hy : y ∈ A) (h : d i x y < uNR (d i) (d j) f A) :
    d j (f.func x) (f.func y) = 0 :=
  prop311_radius d hd f A hAd i j hx hy (lt_of_lt_of_le h (uniform_le_nullRadius hx))

include hd in
/-- 命题 3.15(ii) -/
theorem prop315_ii (f : Operator X bot) (A : Set X) (hA : A.Nonempty) (hAd : A ⊆ dom f)
    (i j : I) {x y : X} (hx : x ∈ A) (hy : y ∈ A) (h : 0 < d j (f.func x) (f.func y)) :
    uNR (d i) (d j) f A ≤ d i x y := by
  by_contra hlt
  exact h.ne' (prop315_i d hd f A hA hAd i j hx hy (not_le.1 hlt))

/-- 命题 3.15(iii) -/
theorem prop315_iii (f : Operator X bot) (A : Set X) (hA : A.Nonempty) (hAd : A ⊆ dom f)
    (i j : I) (τN : ENNReal) (hτ0 : 0 < τN) (hτt : τN < ⊤) (hlt : uNR (d i) (d j) f A < τN)
    (α : ENNReal) (hα0 : 0 < α) (hαt : α < ⊤) (hα1 : uNR (d i) (d j) f A < α) (hα2 : α < τN) :
    ∃ x ∈ A, ∃ y ∈ A, ∃ ε : ENNReal, 0 < ε ∧ ε < ⊤ ∧ d i x y < α ∧
      ε ≤ d j (f.func x) (f.func y) := by
  obtain ⟨y, hyA, hy⟩ : ∃ y ∈ A, nullRadius (d i) (d j) f y A < α := by
    simpa [uNR, uniformNullRadius, iInf_lt_iff] using hα1
  have hnot : ¬ (ball (d i) y α ∩ A ⊆ {x | x ∈ A ∧ d j (f.func x) (f.func y) = 0}) := by
    intro hsub
    exact absurd (le_nullRadius ⟨hα0, hαt, hsub⟩) (not_le.2 hy)
  obtain ⟨x, ⟨hxb, hxA⟩, hx⟩ := not_subset.1 hnot
  have hpos : 0 < d j (f.func x) (f.func y) := by
    rw [pos_iff_ne_zero]; intro h0; exact hx ⟨hxA, h0⟩
  refine ⟨x, hxA, y, hyA, min (d j (f.func x) (f.func y)) 1, lt_min hpos one_pos,
    lt_of_le_of_lt (min_le_right _ _) ENNReal.one_lt_top, hxb, min_le_left _ _⟩

/-! ### CH03-P19 命题 3.16 -/

include hd in
/-- 命题 3.16(i) -/
theorem prop316_i (f : Operator X bot) (A : Set X) (hA : A.Nonempty) (hAd : A ⊆ dom f)
    (i j : I) (τN : ENNReal) (hτ0 : 0 < τN) (hτt : τN < ⊤) (h : τN ≤ uNR (d i) (d j) f A) :
    ∀ x ∈ A, ball (d i) x τN ∩ A ⊆ Fib1 d f j (f.func x) A := by
  intro x hx z hz
  exact ⟨hz.2, zero_of_lt_nullRadius hz.2
    (lt_of_lt_of_le hz.1 (le_trans h (uniform_le_nullRadius hx)))⟩

include hd in
/-- 命题 3.16(ii-a) -/
theorem prop316_ii_a (f : Operator X bot) (A : Set X) (hA : A.Nonempty) (hAd : A ⊆ dom f)
    (i j : I) (τN : ENNReal) (hτ0 : 0 < τN) (hτt : τN < ⊤) (h : τN ≤ uNR (d i) (d j) f A)
    (B : Set X) (hBA : B ⊆ A) (ε : ENNReal) (hε0 : 0 < ε) (hεt : ε < ⊤) :
    covering_number (d j) ε (f.func '' B) ≤ covering_number (d i) τN B := by
  classical
  apply le_iInf₂
  intro S hS
  have hnet : InternalEpsilonNet (d j) ε (f.func '' B) (S.image f.func) := by
    refine ⟨?_, ?_⟩
    · intro c hc
      obtain ⟨s, hs, rfl⟩ := Finset.mem_image.1 hc
      exact mem_image_of_mem _ (hS.1 hs)
    · rintro _ ⟨a, ha, rfl⟩
      obtain ⟨s, hs, hds⟩ := hS.2 a ha
      have hsA : s ∈ A := hBA (hS.1 hs)
      have hz : a ∈ ball (d i) s τN ∩ A := ⟨hds, hBA ha⟩
      have h0 := (prop316_i d hd f A hA hAd i j τN hτ0 hτt h s hsA hz).2
      exact ⟨f.func s, Finset.mem_image_of_mem _ hs, by rw [h0]; exact hε0⟩
  calc covering_number (d j) ε (f.func '' B) ≤ (S.image f.func).card := covering_number_le_card hnet
    _ ≤ S.card := by exact_mod_cast Finset.card_image_le

include hd in
/-- 命题 3.16(ii-b) -/
theorem prop316_ii_b (f : Operator X bot) (A : Set X) (hA : A.Nonempty) (hAd : A ⊆ dom f)
    (i j : I) (τN : ENNReal) (hτ0 : 0 < τN) (hτt : τN < ⊤) (h : τN ≤ uNR (d i) (d j) f A)
    (B : Set X) (hBA : B ⊆ A) (hB : B.Nonempty) (ε : ENNReal) (hε0 : 0 < ε) (hεt : ε < ⊤) :
    metric_entropy (d j) ε (f.func '' B) ≤ metric_entropy (d i) τN B :=
  metric_entropy_mono_cov (prop316_ii_a d hd f A hA hAd i j τN hτ0 hτt h B hBA ε hε0 hεt)

/-! ### CH03-P21 推论 3.17 -/

/-- 非空集合在全部点对零距时覆盖数恰为 1。 -/
theorem covering_number_eq_one_of_zero {δ : X → X → ENNReal} {ε : ENNReal} {S : Set X}
    (hS : S.Nonempty) (hε : 0 < ε) (h : ∀ a ∈ S, ∀ b ∈ S, δ a b = 0) :
    covering_number δ ε S = 1 := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨s, hs⟩ := hS
  apply le_antisymm
  · have hnet : InternalEpsilonNet δ ε S {s} := by
      refine ⟨by simpa using hs, fun x hx => ⟨s, Finset.mem_singleton_self s, ?_⟩⟩
      rw [h x hx s hs]; exact hε
    simpa using covering_number_le_card hnet
  · exact one_le_covering_number_of_nonempty δ ε S ⟨s, hs⟩

include hd in
/-- 推论 3.17 -/
theorem cor317 (f : Operator X bot) (A : Set X) (hA : A.Nonempty) (hAd : A ⊆ dom f) (i j : I)
    (τσ τN : ENNReal) (hσ0 : 0 < τσ) (hσt : τσ < ⊤) (hN0 : 0 < τN) (hNt : τN < ⊤)
    (hdiam : (diamScale d).σ i A < τσ) (hle : τσ ≤ τN) :
    τN ≤ uNR (d i) (d j) f A ↔ ∀ x ∈ A, ∀ y ∈ A, d j (f.func x) (f.func y) = 0 := by
  constructor
  · intro h x hx y hy
    apply prop315_i d hd f A hA hAd i j hx hy
    calc d i x y ≤ diam (d i) A := le_diam (d i) hx hy
      _ < τσ := hdiam
      _ ≤ τN := hle
      _ ≤ _ := h
  · intro h
    have : uNR (d i) (d j) f A = ⊤ := by
      apply top_unique (le_uniform fun y hy => ?_)
      rw [nullRadius_eq_top_of_forall]
      intro r _ _ x hx
      exact ⟨hx.2, h x hx.2 y hy⟩
    rw [this]; exact le_top

include hd in
/-- 推论 3.17 末段 -/
theorem cor317_cover (f : Operator X bot) (A : Set X) (hA : A.Nonempty) (hAd : A ⊆ dom f)
    (i j : I) (h : ∀ x ∈ A, ∀ y ∈ A, d j (f.func x) (f.func y) = 0) (ε : ENNReal)
    (hε0 : 0 < ε) (hεt : ε < ⊤) : covering_number (d j) ε (f.func '' A) = 1 := by
  apply covering_number_eq_one_of_zero (hA.image _) hε0
  rintro _ ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩ _ ⟨y, hy, rfl⟩
  exact h x hx y hy

/-! ### CH03-P27 习题 3.2 -/

/-- 习题 3.2(i) -/
theorem ex32_i [Fintype I] (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (hU : U.Nonempty) (hUd : U ⊆ dom ϕ)
    (K : I → I → ENNReal) (hK : IsCoLipMatrix d ϕ U K) (i j : I) (hKij : 0 < K i j)
    (y : X) (hy : y ∈ U) (hr : 0 < rN d ϕ i j y U) :
    ∀ x ∈ U, d i x y ∉ Ioo 0 (rN d ϕ i j y U) := by
  rintro x hx ⟨h0, h1⟩
  have hz : d j (ϕ.func x) (ϕ.func y) = 0 := zero_of_lt_nullRadius hx h1
  have hle : K i j * d i x y ≤ d j (ϕ.func x) (ϕ.func y) :=
    le_trans (Finset.single_le_sum (f := fun k => K k j * d k x y) (fun _ _ => zero_le _)
      (Finset.mem_univ i)) (hK.2.2 x hx y hy j)
  rw [hz] at hle
  exact (ENNReal.mul_pos hKij.ne' h0.ne').ne' (le_antisymm hle (zero_le _))

/-- 习题 3.2(ii) -/
theorem ex32_ii [Fintype I] (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (hU : U.Nonempty) (hUd : U ⊆ dom ϕ)
    (K : I → I → ENNReal) (hK : IsCoLipMatrix d ϕ U K) (i j : I) (hKij : 0 < K i j)
    (hr : 0 < uNR (d i) (d j) ϕ U) :
    Disjoint ({0} : Set ENNReal) (Ici (uNR (d i) (d j) ϕ U)) ∧
    ∀ x ∈ U, ∀ y ∈ U, d i x y ∈ ({0} : Set ENNReal) ∪ Ici (uNR (d i) (d j) ϕ U) := by
  refine ⟨?_, fun x hx y hy => ?_⟩
  · rw [Set.disjoint_singleton_left, mem_Ici, not_le]; exact hr
  · by_cases h0 : d i x y = 0
    · left; exact h0
    · right
      have hry : uNR (d i) (d j) ϕ U ≤ rN d ϕ i j y U := uniform_le_nullRadius hy
      have := ex32_i d ϕ U hU hUd K hK i j hKij y hy (lt_of_lt_of_le hr hry) x hx
      simp only [mem_Ioo, not_and, not_lt] at this
      exact le_trans hry (this (pos_iff_ne_zero.2 h0))

end OETCh03



namespace OETCh03

open formal_oet.vol_01.ch_01_foundations
open formal_oet.vol_01.ch_02_operator_bounds
open Set

variable {X I : Type} {bot : X}
variable (d : I → X → X → ENNReal) (hd : ∀ i, ExtendedPseudoMetric X bot (d i))

/-- 有限集以自身为内部中心集 -/
theorem covering_number_le_of_finite {δ : X → X → ENNReal} (hself : ∀ x, δ x x = 0)
    {ε : ENNReal} (hε : 0 < ε) {S : Set X} (hS : S.Finite) :
    covering_number δ ε S ≤ hS.toFinset.card := by
  apply covering_number_le_card
  refine ⟨by simp, fun x hx => ⟨x, by simpa using hx, by rw [hself]; exact hε⟩⟩

theorem covering_number_lt_top_of_finite {δ : X → X → ENNReal} (hself : ∀ x, δ x x = 0)
    {ε : ENNReal} (hε : 0 < ε) {S : Set X} (hS : S.Finite) : covering_number δ ε S < ⊤ :=
  lt_of_le_of_lt (covering_number_le_of_finite hself hε hS) (ENNReal.natCast_lt_top _)

/-! ### CH03-P22 注（有限集上映射熵差的良定性） -/

include hd in
/-- 非空有限 `U` 与任意非空两端子族 -/
theorem remark_finite_wd (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (hU : U.Nonempty) (hUd : U ⊆ dom ϕ)
    (hfin : U.Finite) (Jin Jout : Set I) (hJin : Jin.Nonempty) (hJout : Jout.Nonempty)
    (ε : ENNReal) (hε0 : 0 < ε) (hεt : ε < ⊤) :
    (0 < covering_number (subfamily_pseudometric d Jin) ε U ∧
      covering_number (subfamily_pseudometric d Jin) ε U < ⊤) ∧
    (0 < covering_number (subfamily_pseudometric d Jout) ε (ϕ.func '' U) ∧
      covering_number (subfamily_pseudometric d Jout) ε (ϕ.func '' U) < ⊤) ∧
    (∃ a : ℝ, subfamily_metric_entropy d Jin ε U = a) ∧
    (∃ b : ℝ, subfamily_metric_entropy d Jout ε (ϕ.func '' U) = b) ∧
    ∃ h : EntropyDiffDomain d Jin Jout ε ϕ U, ∃ c : ℝ, mapEntropyDiff d Jin Jout ε ϕ U h = c := by
  have hin := covering_number_lt_top_of_finite (subfamily_pseudometric_self d hd Jin) hε0 hfin
  have hout := covering_number_lt_top_of_finite (subfamily_pseudometric_self d hd Jout) hε0
    (hfin.image ϕ.func)
  have ea := metric_entropy_real hU hin
  have eb := metric_entropy_real (hU.image ϕ.func) hout
  refine ⟨⟨pos_iff_ne_zero.2 (covering_number_ne_zero hU), hin⟩,
    ⟨pos_iff_ne_zero.2 (covering_number_ne_zero (hU.image _)), hout⟩, ⟨_, ea⟩, ⟨_, eb⟩,
    ⟨hU, hUd, hJin, hJout, hε0, hεt, Or.inl hin⟩,
    Real.logb 2 (covering_number (subfamily_pseudometric d Jin) ε U).toReal -
      Real.logb 2 (covering_number (subfamily_pseudometric d Jout) ε (ϕ.func '' U)).toReal, ?_⟩
  simp only [mapEntropyDiff, subfamily_metric_entropy_eq_metric_entropy]
  rw [ea, eb, ← EReal.coe_sub]

/-! ### 前推与拉回中心集 -/

open Classical in
/-- 对 `T ⊆ ϕ(U)` 的每个中心选取 `U` 中的代表元。 -/
noncomputable def preCenters (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (T : Finset X) : Finset X :=
  T.image (fun t => if h : t ∈ ϕ.func '' U then Classical.choose h else t)

theorem preCenters_spec (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (T : Finset X)
    (hT : (↑T : Set X) ⊆ ϕ.func '' U) :
    (↑(preCenters ϕ U T) : Set X) ⊆ U ∧ (preCenters ϕ U T).card ≤ T.card ∧
    ∀ t ∈ T, ∃ c ∈ preCenters ϕ U T, ϕ.func c = t := by
  classical
  refine ⟨?_, Finset.card_image_le, ?_⟩
  · intro c hc
    obtain ⟨t, ht, rfl⟩ := Finset.mem_image.1 hc
    have h := hT ht
    simp only [h, dif_pos]
    exact (Classical.choose_spec h).1
  · intro t ht
    have h := hT ht
    refine ⟨_, Finset.mem_image_of_mem _ ht, ?_⟩
    simp only [h, dif_pos]
    exact (Classical.choose_spec h).2

/-! ### CH03-P23 命题 3.18 -/

include hd in
/-- 命题 3.18(i) -/
theorem prop318_i (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (hU : U.Nonempty) (hUd : U ⊆ dom ϕ)
    (ε : ENNReal) (hε0 : 0 < ε) (hεt : ε < ⊤) (j : I) (hj : j ∈ Iconst d ϕ U) :
    covering_number (d j) ε (ϕ.func '' U) = 1 ∧ metric_entropy (d j) ε (ϕ.func '' U) = 0 ∧
    EntropyDiffDomain d {j} {j} ε ϕ U ∧
    ∀ h : EntropyDiffDomain d {j} {j} ε ϕ U,
      mapEntropyDiff d {j} {j} ε ϕ U h = metric_entropy (d j) ε U := by
  have h1 : covering_number (d j) ε (ϕ.func '' U) = 1 := by
    apply covering_number_eq_one_of_zero (hU.image _) hε0
    rintro _ ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩ _ ⟨y, hy, rfl⟩
    exact hj x hx y hy
  have h2 : metric_entropy (d j) ε (ϕ.func '' U) = 0 := by
    simp only [metric_entropy, h1, log2_ereal_one]
  refine ⟨h1, h2, entropyDiffDomain_single d j j ε ϕ U hU hUd hε0 hεt
    (Or.inr (by rw [h1]; exact ENNReal.one_lt_top)), fun h => ?_⟩
  rw [mapEntropyDiff_single, h2, sub_zero]

include hd in
/-- 命题 3.18(ii-a) -/
theorem prop318_ii_a (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (hUd : U ⊆ dom ϕ) (ε : ENNReal)
    (j : I) (hj : j ∈ Iid d ϕ U) :
    covering_number (d j) ε (ϕ.func '' U) = covering_number (d j) ε U ∧
    metric_entropy (d j) ε (ϕ.func '' U) = metric_entropy (d j) ε U := by
  classical
  have hiso := prop31_iii d hd ϕ U j hj
  have hcov : covering_number (d j) ε (ϕ.func '' U) = covering_number (d j) ε U := by
    apply le_antisymm
    · apply le_iInf₂
      intro S hS
      have hnet : InternalEpsilonNet (d j) ε (ϕ.func '' U) (S.image ϕ.func) := by
        refine ⟨?_, ?_⟩
        · intro c hc
          obtain ⟨s, hs, rfl⟩ := Finset.mem_image.1 hc
          exact mem_image_of_mem _ (hS.1 hs)
        · rintro _ ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩
          obtain ⟨s, hs, hds⟩ := hS.2 x hx
          exact ⟨_, Finset.mem_image_of_mem _ hs, by rw [hiso x hx s (hS.1 hs)]; exact hds⟩
      calc covering_number (d j) ε (ϕ.func '' U) ≤ (S.image ϕ.func).card :=
            covering_number_le_card hnet
        _ ≤ S.card := by exact_mod_cast Finset.card_image_le
    · apply le_iInf₂
      intro T hT
      obtain ⟨hsub, hcard, hpre⟩ := preCenters_spec ϕ U T hT.1
      have hnet : InternalEpsilonNet (d j) ε U (preCenters ϕ U T) := by
        refine ⟨hsub, fun x hx => ?_⟩
        obtain ⟨t, ht, hdt⟩ := hT.2 _ (mem_image_of_mem ϕ.func hx)
        obtain ⟨c, hc, rfl⟩ := hpre t ht
        exact ⟨c, hc, by rw [← hiso x hx c (hsub hc)]; exact hdt⟩
      calc covering_number (d j) ε U ≤ (preCenters ϕ U T).card := covering_number_le_card hnet
        _ ≤ T.card := by exact_mod_cast hcard
  exact ⟨hcov, by simp only [metric_entropy, hcov]⟩

include hd in
/-- 命题 3.18(ii-b) -/
theorem prop318_ii_b (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (hUd : U ⊆ dom ϕ) (ε : ENNReal)
    (j : I) (hj : j ∈ Iid d ϕ U) (h : EntropyDiffDomain d {j} {j} ε ϕ U) :
    mapEntropyDiff d {j} {j} ε ϕ U h = 0 := by
  obtain ⟨hcov, hent⟩ := prop318_ii_a d hd ϕ U hUd ε j hj
  have hfin : covering_number (d j) ε U < ⊤ := by
    have := h.finite_side
    simp only [subfamily_pseudometric_singleton] at this
    rcases this with h1 | h1
    · exact h1
    · rwa [hcov] at h1
  rw [mapEntropyDiff_single, hent, metric_entropy_real h.U_nonempty hfin, ← EReal.coe_sub, sub_self,
    EReal.coe_zero]

/-! ### CH03-P24 命题 3.19 -/

open Classical in
/-- 命题 3.19 -/
theorem lip_push_net (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (i j : I) (ε L : ENNReal)
    (hL0 : 0 < L) (hLt : L < ⊤)
    (hLip : ∀ x ∈ U, ∀ y ∈ U, d j (ϕ.func x) (ϕ.func y) ≤ L * d i x y)
    (S : Finset X) (hS : InternalEpsilonNet (d i) (ε / L) U S) :
    InternalEpsilonNet (d j) ε (ϕ.func '' U) (S.image ϕ.func) ∧ (S.image ϕ.func).card ≤ S.card := by
  classical
  refine ⟨⟨?_, ?_⟩, Finset.card_image_le⟩
  · intro c hc
    obtain ⟨s, hs, rfl⟩ := Finset.mem_image.1 hc
    exact mem_image_of_mem _ (hS.1 hs)
  · rintro _ ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩
    obtain ⟨s, hs, hds⟩ := hS.2 x hx
    refine ⟨_, Finset.mem_image_of_mem _ hs, ?_⟩
    rw [ENNReal.lt_div_iff_mul_lt (Or.inl hL0.ne') (Or.inl hLt.ne)] at hds
    calc d j (ϕ.func x) (ϕ.func s) ≤ L * d i x s := hLip x hx s (hS.1 hs)
      _ = d i x s * L := mul_comm _ _
      _ < ε := hds

theorem prop319_cover (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (i j : I) (ε L : ENNReal)
    (hL0 : 0 < L) (hLt : L < ⊤)
    (hLip : ∀ x ∈ U, ∀ y ∈ U, d j (ϕ.func x) (ϕ.func y) ≤ L * d i x y) :
    covering_number (d j) ε (ϕ.func '' U) ≤ covering_number (d i) (ε / L) U := by
  apply le_iInf₂
  intro S hS
  obtain ⟨hnet, hcard⟩ := lip_push_net d ϕ U i j ε L hL0 hLt hLip S hS
  exact le_trans (covering_number_le_card hnet) (by exact_mod_cast hcard)

/-- 命题 3.19 -/
theorem prop319 (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (hU : U.Nonempty) (hUd : U ⊆ dom ϕ) (i j : I)
    (htb : TotallyBoundedIn (d i) U) (ε : ENNReal) (hε0 : 0 < ε) (hεt : ε < ⊤) (L : ENNReal)
    (hL0 : 0 < L) (hLt : L < ⊤)
    (hLip : ∀ x ∈ U, ∀ y ∈ U, d j (ϕ.func x) (ϕ.func y) ≤ L * d i x y) :
    (∃ a : ℝ, metric_entropy (d i) ε U = a) ∧
    (∃ b : ℝ, metric_entropy (d j) ε (ϕ.func '' U) = b) ∧
    (∃ c : ℝ, metric_entropy (d i) (ε / L) U = c) ∧
    ∃ h : EntropyDiffDomain d {i} {j} ε ϕ U,
      metric_entropy (d i) ε U - metric_entropy (d i) (ε / L) U ≤ mapEntropyDiff d {i} {j} ε ϕ U h := by
  have hεL0 : 0 < ε / L := ENNReal.div_pos hε0.ne' hLt.ne
  have hεLt : ε / L < ⊤ := ENNReal.div_lt_top hεt.ne hL0.ne'
  have hin := htb ε hε0 hεt
  have hinL := htb (ε / L) hεL0 hεLt
  have hcov := prop319_cover d ϕ U i j ε L hL0 hLt hLip
  have hout := lt_of_le_of_lt hcov hinL
  refine ⟨⟨_, metric_entropy_real hU hin⟩, ⟨_, metric_entropy_real (hU.image _) hout⟩,
    ⟨_, metric_entropy_real hU hinL⟩,
    entropyDiffDomain_single d i j ε ϕ U hU hUd hε0 hεt (Or.inl hin), ?_⟩
  rw [mapEntropyDiff_single]
  exact EReal.sub_le_sub le_rfl (metric_entropy_mono_cov hcov)

/-! ### CH03-P25 命题 3.20 -/

/-- 命题 3.20 -/
theorem colip_pull_net [Fintype I] (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (i j : I) (ε : ENNReal)
    (K : I → I → ENNReal) (hK : IsCoLipMatrix d ϕ U K) (hK0 : 0 < K i j) (hKt : K i j < ⊤)
    (T : Finset X) (hT : InternalEpsilonNet (d j) ε (ϕ.func '' U) T) :
    InternalEpsilonNet (d i) (ε / K i j) U (preCenters ϕ U T) ∧
      (preCenters ϕ U T).card ≤ T.card := by
  obtain ⟨hsub, hcard, hpre⟩ := preCenters_spec ϕ U T hT.1
  refine ⟨⟨hsub, fun x hx => ?_⟩, hcard⟩
  obtain ⟨t, ht, hdt⟩ := hT.2 _ (mem_image_of_mem ϕ.func hx)
  obtain ⟨c, hc, rfl⟩ := hpre t ht
  refine ⟨c, hc, ?_⟩
  rw [ENNReal.lt_div_iff_mul_lt (Or.inl hK0.ne') (Or.inl hKt.ne)]
  calc d i x c * K i j = K i j * d i x c := mul_comm _ _
    _ ≤ ∑ k : I, K k j * d k x c :=
      Finset.single_le_sum (f := fun k => K k j * d k x c) (fun _ _ => zero_le _) (Finset.mem_univ i)
    _ ≤ d j (ϕ.func x) (ϕ.func c) := hK.2.2 x hx c (hsub hc) j
    _ < ε := hdt

theorem prop320_cover [Fintype I] (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (i j : I) (ε : ENNReal)
    (K : I → I → ENNReal) (hK : IsCoLipMatrix d ϕ U K) (hK0 : 0 < K i j) (hKt : K i j < ⊤) :
    covering_number (d i) (ε / K i j) U ≤ covering_number (d j) ε (ϕ.func '' U) := by
  apply le_iInf₂
  intro T hT
  obtain ⟨hnet, hcard⟩ := colip_pull_net d ϕ U i j ε K hK hK0 hKt T hT
  calc covering_number (d i) (ε / K i j) U ≤ (preCenters ϕ U T).card := covering_number_le_card hnet
    _ ≤ T.card := by exact_mod_cast hcard

/-- 命题 3.20 -/
theorem prop320 [Fintype I] (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (hU : U.Nonempty) (hUd : U ⊆ dom ϕ)
    (i j : I) (ε : ENNReal) (hε0 : 0 < ε) (hεt : ε < ⊤) (htb : TotallyBoundedIn (d i) U)
    (K : I → I → ENNReal) (hK : IsCoLipMatrix d ϕ U K) (hK0 : 0 < K i j) (hKt : K i j < ⊤) :
    (∃ a : ℝ, metric_entropy (d i) ε U = a) ∧
    (∃ c : ℝ, metric_entropy (d i) (ε / K i j) U = c) ∧
    ∃ h : EntropyDiffDomain d {i} {j} ε ϕ U,
      mapEntropyDiff d {i} {j} ε ϕ U h ≤ metric_entropy (d i) ε U - metric_entropy (d i) (ε / K i j) U := by
  have hεK0 : 0 < ε / K i j := ENNReal.div_pos hε0.ne' hKt.ne
  have hεKt : ε / K i j < ⊤ := ENNReal.div_lt_top hεt.ne hK0.ne'
  have hin := htb ε hε0 hεt
  have hinK := htb _ hεK0 hεKt
  refine ⟨⟨_, metric_entropy_real hU hin⟩, ⟨_, metric_entropy_real hU hinK⟩,
    entropyDiffDomain_single d i j ε ϕ U hU hUd hε0 hεt (Or.inl hin), ?_⟩
  rw [mapEntropyDiff_single]
  exact EReal.sub_le_sub le_rfl (metric_entropy_mono_cov (prop320_cover d ϕ U i j ε K hK hK0 hKt))

end OETCh03



namespace OETCh03

open formal_oet.vol_01.ch_01_foundations
open formal_oet.vol_01.ch_02_operator_bounds
open Set

/-! ### 有限反例的通用合法结构 -/

section ClsDist
variable {Y α : Type} [DecidableEq Y] [DecidableEq α]

/-- 类距离：`b` 与自身距 0，与其余点距 `+∞`；非吸收点按类函数 `c` 同类距 0，异类距 `v`。 -/
noncomputable def clsDist (b : Y) (c : Y → α) (v : ENNReal) : Y → Y → ENNReal :=
  fun x y => if x = b ∧ y = b then 0 else if x = b ∨ y = b then ⊤ else if c x = c y then 0 else v

/-- 类距离满足扩展伪度量四公理（含吸收元隔离）。 -/
theorem clsDist_epm (b : Y) (c : Y → α) (v : ENNReal) (hne : ∃ y : Y, y ≠ b) :
    ExtendedPseudoMetric Y b (clsDist b c v) where
  nonabsorbing := hne
  dist_self x := by unfold clsDist; by_cases hx : x = b <;> simp [hx]
  symm x y := by
    unfold clsDist
    by_cases hx : x = b <;> by_cases hy : y = b <;> simp [hx, hy, eq_comm]
  triangle x y z := by
    unfold clsDist
    by_cases hx : x = b <;> by_cases hy : y = b <;> by_cases hz : z = b <;> simp [hx, hy, hz]
    split_ifs <;> simp_all
  isolate_bot x hx := by unfold clsDist; simp [hx]

end ClsDist

/-! ### CH03-P03 注：两点换位反例 -/

inductive P3 | bot | a | b
  deriving DecidableEq

/-- 单分量（指标集 `Unit`）：`d(a,b) = 1`，自距 0，与吸收元距 `+∞`。 -/
noncomputable def dSwap : Unit → P3 → P3 → ENNReal := fun _ => clsDist P3.bot id 1

theorem dSwap_epm : ∀ i, ExtendedPseudoMetric P3 P3.bot (dSwap i) :=
  fun _ => clsDist_epm _ _ _ ⟨P3.a, by decide⟩

/-- 两点换位算子（保持吸收元）。 -/
def swap3 : Operator P3 P3.bot where
  func := fun x => match x with
    | .bot => .bot
    | .a => .b
    | .b => .a
  preserves_bot := rfl

def U3 : Set P3 := {P3.a, P3.b}

theorem swap3_mul : swap3 * swap3 = 1 := by
  apply Operator.ext; funext x; cases x <;> rfl

theorem swap3_image : swap3.func '' U3 = U3 := by
  ext x; cases x <;> simp [U3, swap3]

theorem U3_sub_dom_swap : U3 ⊆ dom (swap3 * swap3) := by
  rw [swap3_mul]; intro x hx; simp only [U3, mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at hx
  rcases hx with rfl | rfl <;> simp [dom]

theorem swap_not_const : () ∉ Iconst dSwap swap3 U3 :=
  (not_mem_Iconst_iff dSwap).2 ⟨.a, by simp [U3], .b, by simp [U3], by simp [dSwap, clsDist, swap3]⟩

theorem swap_act : Iact dSwap swap3 U3 = {()} ∧ Iid dSwap swap3 U3 = ∅ := by
  have hnid : ¬ ∀ x ∈ U3, dSwap () (swap3.func x) x = 0 := fun h => by
    have := h .a (by simp [U3]); simp [dSwap, clsDist, swap3] at this
  constructor
  · ext i; cases i; simp only [mem_singleton_iff, iff_true]
    exact mem_Iact_iff.2 ⟨swap_not_const, hnid⟩
  · ext i; cases i; simp only [mem_empty_iff_false, iff_false]
    exact fun h => hnid h.2

theorem swap_comp : Iid dSwap (swap3 * swap3) U3 = {()} ∧ Iact dSwap (swap3 * swap3) U3 = ∅ := by
  rw [swap3_mul]
  have hnc : () ∉ Iconst dSwap (1 : Operator P3 P3.bot) U3 :=
    (not_mem_Iconst_iff dSwap).2 ⟨.a, by simp [U3], .b, by simp [U3], by simp [dSwap, clsDist]⟩
  have hid : () ∈ Iid dSwap (1 : Operator P3 P3.bot) U3 :=
    ⟨hnc, fun x _ => (dSwap_epm ()).dist_self x⟩
  constructor
  · ext i; cases i; simp only [mem_singleton_iff, iff_true]; exact hid
  · ext i; cases i; simp only [mem_empty_iff_false, iff_false]
    exact fun h => h.2 (Or.inr hid)

/-- 注（复合分量保持的逆向蕴含一般不成立） -/
theorem remark_swap :
    (∀ i, ExtendedPseudoMetric P3 P3.bot (dSwap i)) ∧ U3 ⊆ dom (swap3 * swap3) ∧
    swap3.func '' U3 = U3 ∧
    Iact dSwap swap3 U3 = {()} ∧ Iid dSwap swap3 U3 = ∅ ∧
    Iid dSwap (swap3 * swap3) U3 = {()} ∧ Iact dSwap (swap3 * swap3) U3 = ∅ ∧
    () ∈ Iact dSwap swap3 U3 ∩ Iact dSwap swap3 (swap3.func '' U3) ∧
    Iact dSwap (swap3 * swap3) U3 ⊂ Iact dSwap swap3 U3 ∪ Iact dSwap swap3 (swap3.func '' U3) ∧
    Iid dSwap swap3 U3 ∩ Iid dSwap swap3 (swap3.func '' U3) ⊂ Iid dSwap (swap3 * swap3) U3 := by
  obtain ⟨h1, h2⟩ := swap_act
  obtain ⟨h3, h4⟩ := swap_comp
  refine ⟨dSwap_epm, U3_sub_dom_swap, swap3_image, h1, h2, h3, h4, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · rw [swap3_image, h1]; simp
  · rw [swap3_image, h1, h4]; simp
  · rw [swap3_image, h2, h3]; simp

/-- 两条逆向包含作为一般命题均不成立。 -/
theorem remark_reverse_fails :
    ¬ (∀ (X I : Type) (bot : X) (d : I → X → X → ENNReal), (∀ i, ExtendedPseudoMetric X bot (d i)) →
        ∀ (ϕ₁ ϕ₂ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X), U ⊆ dom (ϕ₂ * ϕ₁) →
          Iact d ϕ₁ U ∪ Iact d ϕ₂ (ϕ₁.func '' U) ⊆ Iact d (ϕ₂ * ϕ₁) U) ∧
    ¬ (∀ (X I : Type) (bot : X) (d : I → X → X → ENNReal), (∀ i, ExtendedPseudoMetric X bot (d i)) →
        ∀ (ϕ₁ ϕ₂ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X), U ⊆ dom (ϕ₂ * ϕ₁) →
          Iid d (ϕ₂ * ϕ₁) U ⊆ Iid d ϕ₁ U ∩ Iid d ϕ₂ (ϕ₁.func '' U)) := by
  obtain ⟨-, hU, -, -, -, -, -, -, h1, h2⟩ := remark_swap
  exact ⟨fun H => h1.2 (H P3 Unit P3.bot dSwap dSwap_epm swap3 swap3 U3 hU),
    fun H => h2.2 (H P3 Unit P3.bot dSwap dSwap_epm swap3 swap3 U3 hU)⟩

/-! ### CH03-P04-02 命题 3.3(ii)：七点、双分量反例 -/

inductive P7 | bot | a | b | p | q | r | s
  deriving DecidableEq

inductive I2 | i | m
  deriving DecidableEq, Fintype

/-- `d_i` 的零距类：把 `q` 并入 `p`。 -/
def cls7 : P7 → P7 := fun x => if x = .q then .p else x

/-- `d_i`：`p,q` 零距，其余不同点距 1；`d_m`：离散度量；吸收元隔离。 -/
noncomputable def d7 : I2 → P7 → P7 → ENNReal
  | .i => clsDist P7.bot cls7 1
  | .m => clsDist P7.bot id 1

theorem d7_epm : ∀ k, ExtendedPseudoMetric P7 P7.bot (d7 k) := by
  intro k; cases k
  · exact clsDist_epm _ _ _ ⟨P7.a, by decide⟩
  · exact clsDist_epm _ _ _ ⟨P7.a, by decide⟩

def phi7 : Operator P7 P7.bot where
  func := fun x => match x with
    | .a => .p
    | .b => .q
    | _ => .bot
  preserves_bot := rfl

def psi7 : Operator P7 P7.bot where
  func := fun x => match x with
    | .p => .r
    | .q => .s
    | _ => .bot
  preserves_bot := rfl

def U7 : Set P7 := {P7.a, P7.b}

/-- 命题 3.3(ii) -/
theorem prop33_ii_example :
    (∀ k, ExtendedPseudoMetric P7 P7.bot (d7 k)) ∧ U7 ⊆ dom (psi7 * phi7) ∧
    d7 .i P7.p P7.q = 0 ∧ d7 .m P7.p P7.q = 1 ∧ d7 .i P7.r P7.s = 1 ∧
    I2.i ∈ Iconst d7 phi7 U7 ∧ I2.i ∉ Iconst d7 (psi7 * phi7) U7 := by
  refine ⟨d7_epm, ?_, by simp [d7, clsDist, cls7], by simp [d7, clsDist],
    by simp [d7, clsDist, cls7], ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro x hx; simp only [U7, mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at hx
    rcases hx with rfl | rfl <;> simp [dom, psi7, phi7]
  · intro x hx y hy
    simp only [U7, mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at hx hy
    rcases hx with rfl | rfl <;> rcases hy with rfl | rfl <;> simp [d7, clsDist, cls7, phi7]
  · exact (not_mem_Iconst_iff d7).2 ⟨.a, by simp [U7], .b, by simp [U7],
      by simp [d7, clsDist, cls7, phi7, psi7]⟩

/-- 命题 3.3(ii) -/
theorem prop33_ii :
    ¬ (∀ (X I : Type) (bot : X) (d : I → X → X → ENNReal), (∀ i, ExtendedPseudoMetric X bot (d i)) →
        ∀ (ϕ ψ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (i : I), U ⊆ dom (ψ * ϕ) →
          i ∈ Iconst d ϕ U → i ∈ Iconst d (ψ * ϕ) U) := by
  intro H
  obtain ⟨-, hU, -, -, -, h1, h2⟩ := prop33_ii_example
  exact h2 (H P7 I2 P7.bot d7 d7_epm phi7 psi7 U7 I2.i hU h1)

/-! ### CH03-P11-03 命题 3.8(ii)：无穷输入距离的两点反例 -/

/-- 指标集任意（`DecidableEq`），给定输入分量 `i₀`：`d_{i₀}(a,b) = +∞`，其余分量 `d_k(a,b) = 1`。 -/
noncomputable def dInf {I : Type} [DecidableEq I] (i₀ : I) : I → P3 → P3 → ENNReal :=
  fun k => clsDist P3.bot id (if k = i₀ then ⊤ else 1)

theorem dInf_epm {I : Type} [DecidableEq I] (i₀ : I) :
    ∀ k, ExtendedPseudoMetric P3 P3.bot (dInf i₀ k) :=
  fun _ => clsDist_epm _ _ _ ⟨P3.a, by decide⟩

/-- 命题 3.8(ii) -/
theorem prop38_ii_example {I : Type} [DecidableEq I] (i j : I) :
    (∀ k, ExtendedPseudoMetric P3 P3.bot (dInf i k)) ∧ U3.Nonempty ∧
    U3 ⊆ dom (1 : Operator P3 P3.bot) ∧
    (∀ y ∈ U3, rN (dInf i) (1 : Operator P3 P3.bot) i j y U3 = ⊤) ∧
    nullMatrix (dInf i) (1 : Operator P3 P3.bot) U3 j i = ⊤ ∧ j ∉ Iconst (dInf i) (1 : Operator P3 P3.bot) U3 := by
  have hpt : ∀ y ∈ U3, rN (dInf i) (1 : Operator P3 P3.bot) i j y U3 = ⊤ := by
    intro y hy
    apply nullRadius_eq_top_of_forall
    intro r _ hrt x hx
    refine ⟨hx.2, ?_⟩
    have hxy : x = y := by
      by_contra hne
      have h1 := hx.1
      simp only [ball, mem_setOf_eq, U3, mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at h1 hy
      have hx2 := hx.2
      simp only [U3, mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at hx2
      rcases hx2 with rfl | rfl <;> rcases hy with rfl | rfl <;>
        simp_all [dInf, clsDist]
    subst hxy; exact (dInf_epm i j).dist_self _
  refine ⟨dInf_epm i, ⟨P3.a, by simp [U3]⟩, ?_, hpt, ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro x hx; simp only [U3, mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at hx
    rcases hx with rfl | rfl <;> simp [dom]
  · exact top_unique (le_uniform fun y hy => (hpt y hy).ge)
  · refine (not_mem_Iconst_iff (dInf i)).2 ⟨.a, by simp [U3], .b, by simp [U3], ?_⟩
    by_cases h : j = i <;> simp [dInf, clsDist, h]

/-- 命题 3.8(ii) -/
theorem prop38_ii :
    (¬ ∀ (X I : Type) [Fintype I] (bot : X) (d : I → X → X → ENNReal),
        (∀ k, ExtendedPseudoMetric X bot (d k)) → ∀ (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (i j : I),
          U.Nonempty → U ⊆ dom ϕ → i = j → nullMatrix d ϕ U j i = ⊤ → j ∈ Iconst d ϕ U) ∧
    (¬ ∀ (X I : Type) [Fintype I] (bot : X) (d : I → X → X → ENNReal),
        (∀ k, ExtendedPseudoMetric X bot (d k)) → ∀ (ϕ : Operator X bot) (U : Set X) (i j : I),
          U.Nonempty → U ⊆ dom ϕ → i ≠ j → nullMatrix d ϕ U j i = ⊤ → j ∈ Iconst d ϕ U) := by
  constructor
  · intro H
    obtain ⟨h0, hne, hdom, -, hN, hj⟩ := prop38_ii_example (I := Unit) () ()
    exact hj (H P3 Unit P3.bot _ h0 1 U3 () () hne hdom rfl hN)
  · intro H
    obtain ⟨h0, hne, hdom, -, hN, hj⟩ := prop38_ii_example (I := Bool) true false
    exact hj (H P3 Bool P3.bot _ h0 1 U3 true false hne hdom (by decide) hN)

end OETCh03



namespace OETCh03

open formal_oet.vol_01.ch_01_foundations
open formal_oet.vol_01.ch_02_operator_bounds
open Set

/-! ### 覆盖数的具体下界工具 -/

section CoverTools
variable {X : Type}

/-- 两点无公共内部中心时覆盖数至少为 2。 -/
theorem two_le_covering_number {δ : X → X → ENNReal} {ε : ENNReal} {U : Set X} {x y : X}
    (hx : x ∈ U) (hy : y ∈ U) (h : ∀ c ∈ U, δ x c < ε → δ y c < ε → False) :
    2 ≤ covering_number δ ε U := by
  apply le_iInf₂
  intro S hS
  obtain ⟨cx, hcx, hdx⟩ := hS.2 x hx
  obtain ⟨cy, hcy, hdy⟩ := hS.2 y hy
  have hne : cx ≠ cy := by rintro rfl; exact h cx (hS.1 hcx) hdx hdy
  have : 2 ≤ S.card := Finset.one_lt_card.2 ⟨cx, hcx, cy, hcy, hne⟩
  exact_mod_cast this

/-- 若 `T` 中每点只能被自身作为内部中心覆盖，则覆盖数至少为 `|T|`。 -/
theorem card_le_covering_number_of_isolated {δ : X → X → ENNReal} {ε : ENNReal} {U : Set X}
    (T : Finset X) (hT : (↑T : Set X) ⊆ U) (h : ∀ x ∈ T, ∀ c ∈ U, δ x c < ε → c = x) :
    (T.card : ENNReal) ≤ covering_number δ ε U := by
  apply le_iInf₂
  intro S hS
  have hsub : T ⊆ S := by
    intro x hx
    obtain ⟨c, hc, hd⟩ := hS.2 x (hT hx)
    rw [← h x hx c (hS.1 hc) hd]; exact hc
  exact_mod_cast Finset.card_le_card hsub

theorem covering_number_le_two {δ : X → X → ENNReal} {ε : ENNReal} {U : Set X} [DecidableEq X]
    (a b : X) (h : InternalEpsilonNet δ ε U {a, b}) : covering_number δ ε U ≤ 2 :=
  le_trans (covering_number_le_card h) (by exact_mod_cast Finset.card_le_two)

theorem covering_number_le_one {δ : X → X → ENNReal} {ε : ENNReal} {U : Set X}
    (a : X) (h : InternalEpsilonNet δ ε U {a}) : covering_number δ ε U ≤ 1 :=
  le_trans (covering_number_le_card h) (by simp)

end CoverTools

theorem log2_ereal_two : log2_ereal 2 = 1 := by
  simp [log2_ereal]

/-! ### 带吸收元的实直线 `ℝ ⊔ {⊥}` -/

/-- `ℝ ⊔ {⊥}`：`none` 为吸收元，`some x` 为实数点。 -/
abbrev RB := Option ℝ

/-- 把实数部分的距离按吸收元约定延拓：`d(⊥,⊥) = 0`，`⊥` 与实数点距离 `+∞`。 -/
noncomputable def liftDist (ρ : ℝ → ℝ → ENNReal) : RB → RB → ENNReal
  | none, none => 0
  | some _, none => ⊤
  | none, some _ => ⊤
  | some x, some y => ρ x y

@[simp] theorem liftDist_some (ρ : ℝ → ℝ → ENNReal) (x y : ℝ) :
    liftDist ρ (some x) (some y) = ρ x y := rfl

theorem liftDist_epm (ρ : ℝ → ℝ → ENNReal) (h0 : ∀ x, ρ x x = 0) (hs : ∀ x y, ρ x y = ρ y x)
    (ht : ∀ x y z, ρ x z ≤ ρ x y + ρ y z) : ExtendedPseudoMetric RB none (liftDist ρ) where
  nonabsorbing := ⟨some 0, by simp⟩
  dist_self x := by cases x <;> simp [liftDist, h0]
  symm x y := by cases x <;> cases y <;> simp [liftDist, hs]
  triangle x y z := by cases x <;> cases y <;> cases z <;> simp [liftDist, ht]
  isolate_bot x hx := by cases x <;> simp_all [liftDist]

/-- 通常距离 `|x - y|`。 -/
noncomputable def dR : RB → RB → ENNReal := liftDist (fun x y => ENNReal.ofReal |x - y|)

/-- 绝对值读数距离 `||x| - |y||`。 -/
noncomputable def dAbs : RB → RB → ENNReal := liftDist (fun x y => ENNReal.ofReal |(|x| - |y|)|)

@[simp] theorem dR_some (x y : ℝ) : dR (some x) (some y) = ENNReal.ofReal |x - y| := rfl
@[simp] theorem dAbs_some (x y : ℝ) : dAbs (some x) (some y) = ENNReal.ofReal |(|x| - |y|)| := rfl

theorem dR_epm : ExtendedPseudoMetric RB none dR :=
  liftDist_epm _ (fun x => by simp) (fun x y => by rw [abs_sub_comm])
    (fun x y z => (ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal (abs_sub_le x y z)).trans ENNReal.ofReal_add_le)

theorem dAbs_epm : ExtendedPseudoMetric RB none dAbs :=
  liftDist_epm _ (fun x => by simp) (fun x y => by rw [abs_sub_comm])
    (fun x y z => (ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal (abs_sub_le |x| |y| |z|)).trans ENNReal.ofReal_add_le)

/-- 单分量规范族（指标集 `Unit`）。 -/
noncomputable def gR : Unit → RB → RB → ENNReal := fun _ => dR
noncomputable def gAbs : Unit → RB → RB → ENNReal := fun _ => dAbs

theorem gR_epm : ∀ i, ExtendedPseudoMetric RB none (gR i) := fun _ => dR_epm
theorem gAbs_epm : ∀ i, ExtendedPseudoMetric RB none (gAbs i) := fun _ => dAbs_epm

/-- 实值全映射提升为保持吸收元的算子。 -/
def realOp (h : ℝ → ℝ) : Operator RB none := ⟨fun z => z.map h, rfl⟩

/-- 偏映射（`none` 表示未定义）提升为保持吸收元的算子。 -/
def partialOp (g : ℝ → Option ℝ) : Operator RB none := ⟨fun z => z.bind g, rfl⟩

@[simp] theorem realOp_some (h : ℝ → ℝ) (x : ℝ) : (realOp h).func (some x) = some (h x) := rfl
@[simp] theorem partialOp_some (g : ℝ → Option ℝ) (x : ℝ) :
    (partialOp g).func (some x) = g x := rfl

theorem some_mem_dom_realOp (h : ℝ → ℝ) (x : ℝ) : some x ∈ dom (realOp h) := by simp [dom]

theorem image_some_sub_dom_realOp (h : ℝ → ℝ) (S : Set ℝ) : some '' S ⊆ dom (realOp h) := by
  rintro _ ⟨x, -, rfl⟩; exact some_mem_dom_realOp h x

theorem ofReal_eq_toReal {ε : ENNReal} (h0 : 0 < ε) (ht : ε < ⊤) :
    ∃ e : ℝ, 0 < e ∧ ε = ENNReal.ofReal e :=
  ⟨ε.toReal, ENNReal.toReal_pos h0.ne' ht.ne, (ENNReal.ofReal_toReal ht.ne).symm⟩

/-! ### CH03-P10 反例 3.1：阶跃算子 -/

/-- 阶跃函数 `f(x) = 0 (x < 0)`，`1 (x ≥ 0)`。 -/
noncomputable def stepF (x : ℝ) : ℝ := if x < 0 then 0 else 1

noncomputable def stepOp : Operator RB none := realOp stepF

def stepSet : Set ℝ := Ico (-1) 0 ∪ Ioc 0 1

/-- `U = [-1,0) ∪ (0,1] ⊆ ℝ ⊔ {⊥}`。 -/
def Ustep : Set RB := some '' stepSet

/-- 反例 3.1 的结构合法性 -/
theorem ex_step_structure :
    (∀ i, ExtendedPseudoMetric RB none (gR i)) ∧ stepOp.func none = none ∧
    Ustep ⊆ dom stepOp ∧ Ustep.Nonempty ∧ none ∉ Ustep :=
  ⟨gR_epm, rfl, image_some_sub_dom_realOp _ _,
    ⟨some 1, mem_image_of_mem _ (Or.inr ⟨one_pos, le_rfl⟩)⟩, by simp [Ustep]⟩

theorem stepF_eq_of_close {x y : ℝ} (hy : y ≠ 0) (h : |x - y| < |y|) : stepF x = stepF y := by
  rcases lt_or_gt_of_ne hy with hy | hy
  · rw [abs_of_neg hy] at h
    have : x < 0 := by linarith [le_abs_self (x - y)]
    simp [stepF, this, hy]
  · rw [abs_of_pos hy] at h
    have : 0 < x := by linarith [neg_abs_le (x - y)]
    simp [stepF, not_lt.2 this.le, not_lt.2 hy.le]

theorem stepSet_ne_zero {y : ℝ} (hy : y ∈ stepSet) : y ≠ 0 := by
  rcases hy with ⟨-, h⟩ | ⟨h, -⟩ <;> [exact h.ne; exact h.ne']

/-- 半径超过 `|y|` 时，存在 `U` 中异号点落入开球。 -/
theorem step_witness {y : ℝ} (hy : y ∈ stepSet) {r : ℝ} (hr : |y| < r) :
    ∃ x ∈ stepSet, |x - y| < r ∧ stepF x ≠ stepF y := by
  obtain ⟨m, hm0, hm1, hm2⟩ : ∃ m : ℝ, 0 < m ∧ m ≤ 1 ∧ m ≤ r - |y| :=
    ⟨min (r - |y|) 1, lt_min (by linarith) one_pos, min_le_right _ _, min_le_left _ _⟩
  rcases hy with ⟨hy1, hy2⟩ | ⟨hy1, hy2⟩
  · rw [abs_of_neg hy2] at hm2
    refine ⟨m / 2, Or.inr ⟨by linarith, by linarith⟩, ?_, ?_⟩
    · rw [abs_of_pos (by linarith)]; linarith
    · simp [stepF, hy2, not_lt.2 (by linarith : (0:ℝ) ≤ m / 2)]
  · rw [abs_of_pos hy1] at hm2
    refine ⟨-(m / 2), Or.inl ⟨by linarith, by linarith⟩, ?_, ?_⟩
    · rw [abs_of_neg (by linarith)]; linarith
    · simp [stepF, not_lt.2 hy1.le, (by linarith : -(m / 2) < 0)]

/-- 反例 3.1 -/
theorem ex_step_adm (y : ℝ) (hy : y ∈ stepSet) :
    admRadii dR dR stepOp (some y) Ustep = Ioc 0 (ENNReal.ofReal |y|) := by
  ext r
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨h0, ht, hsub⟩
    refine ⟨h0, ?_⟩
    by_contra hlt
    rw [not_le] at hlt
    obtain ⟨e, he0, rfl⟩ := ofReal_eq_toReal h0 ht
    have hye : |y| < e := ((ENNReal.ofReal_lt_ofReal_iff he0).1 hlt)
    obtain ⟨x, hx, hxy, hne⟩ := step_witness hy hye
    have hmem : some x ∈ ball dR (some y) (ENNReal.ofReal e) ∩ Ustep :=
      ⟨by simpa [ball] using (ENNReal.ofReal_lt_ofReal_iff he0).2 hxy, mem_image_of_mem _ hx⟩
    have h0' := (hsub hmem).2
    simp only [stepOp, realOp_some, dR_some, ENNReal.ofReal_eq_zero] at h0'
    exact hne (by linarith [abs_nonneg (stepF x - stepF y), abs_le.1 (le_of_le_of_eq h0' rfl)])
  · rintro ⟨h0, hle⟩
    refine ⟨h0, lt_of_le_of_lt hle ENNReal.ofReal_lt_top, ?_⟩
    rintro _ ⟨hb, ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩⟩
    refine ⟨mem_image_of_mem _ hx, ?_⟩
    have hb' : ENNReal.ofReal |x - y| < ENNReal.ofReal |y| := lt_of_lt_of_le hb hle
    have := stepF_eq_of_close (stepSet_ne_zero hy) (ENNReal.ofReal_lt_ofReal_iff'.1 hb').1
    simp [stepOp, this]

/-- 反例 3.1 -/
theorem ex_step_radius (y : ℝ) (hy : y ∈ stepSet) :
    nullRadius dR dR stepOp (some y) Ustep = ENNReal.ofReal |y| ∧
    0 < nullRadius dR dR stepOp (some y) Ustep := by
  have hpos : 0 < ENNReal.ofReal |y| :=
    ENNReal.ofReal_pos.2 (abs_pos.2 (stepSet_ne_zero hy))
  have : nullRadius dR dR stepOp (some y) Ustep = ENNReal.ofReal |y| := by
    rw [nullRadius, ex_step_adm y hy, csSup_Ioc hpos]
  exact ⟨this, this ▸ hpos⟩

/-- 反例 3.1 -/
theorem ex_step_uniform : uNR dR dR stepOp Ustep = 0 ∧ nullMatrix gR stepOp Ustep () () = 0 := by
  have h : uNR dR dR stepOp Ustep = 0 := by
    apply le_antisymm _ (zero_le _)
    apply ENNReal.le_of_forall_pos_le_add
    intro ε hε _
    rw [zero_add]
    have hε' : (0 : ℝ) < ε := NNReal.coe_pos.2 hε
    have hy : min 1 (ε : ℝ) ∈ stepSet := Or.inr ⟨lt_min one_pos hε', min_le_left _ _⟩
    calc uNR dR dR stepOp Ustep ≤ nullRadius dR dR stepOp (some (min 1 (ε : ℝ))) Ustep :=
          uniform_le_nullRadius (mem_image_of_mem _ hy)
      _ = ENNReal.ofReal |min 1 (ε : ℝ)| := (ex_step_radius _ hy).1
      _ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal ε := ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal
          (by rw [abs_of_pos (lt_min one_pos hε')]; exact min_le_right _ _)
      _ = ε := ENNReal.ofReal_coe_nnreal
  exact ⟨h, h⟩

/-- 反例 3.1 总结 -/
theorem counterexample31 :
    (∀ y ∈ Ustep, 0 < nullRadius dR dR stepOp y Ustep) ∧ uNR dR dR stepOp Ustep = 0 := by
  refine ⟨?_, ex_step_uniform.1⟩
  rintro _ ⟨y, hy, rfl⟩
  exact (ex_step_radius y hy).2

/-! ### CH03-P17-04 命题 3.14(iii)：直径尺度的二点/单点反例 -/

theorem diam_pair_01 : diam dR {some 0, some 1} = 1 := by
  apply le_antisymm
  · refine iSup₂_le fun x hx => iSup₂_le fun y hy => ?_
    simp only [mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at hx hy
    rcases hx with rfl | rfl <;> rcases hy with rfl | rfl <;> norm_num
  · simpa using le_diam dR (A := {some 0, some 1}) (x := some 1) (y := some 0) (by simp) (by simp)

theorem diam_single_0 : diam dR {some 0} = 0 := by
  apply le_antisymm _ (zero_le _)
  refine iSup₂_le fun x hx => iSup₂_le fun y hy => ?_
  simp only [mem_singleton_iff] at hx hy
  subst hx; subst hy; simp

/-- 命题 3.14(iii) -/
theorem prop314_iii :
    let A : Set RB := {some 0, some 1}
    let B : Set RB := {some 0}
    B.Nonempty ∧ B ⊆ A ∧ A ⊆ dom (1 : Operator RB none) ∧
    (diamScale gR).σ () B = 0 ∧ (diamScale gR).σ () A = 1 ∧
    (diamScale gR).σ () B < 1 / 2 ∧ 1 / 2 ≤ (diamScale gR).σ () A := by
  intro A B
  have hB : (diamScale gR).σ () B = 0 := diam_single_0
  have hA : (diamScale gR).σ () A = 1 := diam_pair_01
  refine ⟨⟨some 0, rfl⟩, by simp [A, B], ?_, hB, hA, ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro x hx; simp only [A, mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at hx
    rcases hx with rfl | rfl <;> simp [dom]
  · rw [hB]; norm_num
  · rw [hA]; exact ENNReal.half_le_self

/-- 命题 3.14(iii) -/
theorem prop314_iii_not :
    ¬ ∀ (A B : Set RB), B.Nonempty → B ⊆ A → A ⊆ dom (1 : Operator RB none) →
      (diamScale gR).σ () B < 1 / 2 → (diamScale gR).σ () A < 1 / 2 := by
  intro H
  obtain ⟨h1, h2, h3, -, -, h6, h7⟩ := prop314_iii
  exact absurd (H _ _ h1 h2 h3 h6) (not_lt.2 h7)

/-! ### CH03-P20 命题 3.16 后的二点覆盖反例 -/

theorem cov_pair_01_half : covering_number dR (1 / 2) {some 0, some 1} = 2 := by
  classical
  apply le_antisymm
  · apply covering_number_le_two (some 0) (some 1)
    refine ⟨by simp, fun x hx => ?_⟩
    simp only [mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at hx
    rcases hx with rfl | rfl
    · exact ⟨some 0, by simp, by simp⟩
    · exact ⟨some 1, by simp, by simp⟩
  · apply two_le_covering_number (x := some 0) (y := some 1) (by simp) (by simp)
    intro c hc h1 h2
    simp only [mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at hc
    rcases hc with rfl | rfl
    · norm_num at h2
    · norm_num at h1

/-- 命题 3.16 后正文反例 -/
theorem remark316 (τ : ENNReal) (hτ0 : 0 < τ) (hτt : τ < ⊤) :
    ∃ a : ℝ, 0 < a ∧ a ≤ 1 ∧ ENNReal.ofReal (2 * a) < τ ∧
      ({some (-a), some a} : Set RB) ⊆ Ustep ∧
      covering_number dR τ {some (-a), some a} = 1 ∧
      stepOp.func '' {some (-a), some a} = {some 0, some 1} ∧
      covering_number dR (1 / 2) (stepOp.func '' {some (-a), some a}) = 2 := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨e, he0, rfl⟩ := ofReal_eq_toReal hτ0 hτt
  set a := min 1 (e / 4) with ha
  have ha0 : 0 < a := lt_min one_pos (by linarith)
  have ha1 : a ≤ 1 := min_le_left _ _
  have ha2 : a ≤ e / 4 := min_le_right _ _
  have h2a : ENNReal.ofReal (2 * a) < ENNReal.ofReal e :=
    (ENNReal.ofReal_lt_ofReal_iff he0).2 (by linarith)
  have himg : stepOp.func '' {some (-a), some a} = {some 0, some 1} := by
    rw [image_insert_eq, image_singleton]
    simp [stepOp, stepF, ha0, not_lt.2 ha0.le]
  refine ⟨a, ha0, ha1, h2a, ?_, ?_, himg, by rw [himg]; exact cov_pair_01_half⟩
  · intro x hx
    simp only [mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at hx
    rcases hx with rfl | rfl
    · exact mem_image_of_mem _ (Or.inl ⟨by linarith, by linarith⟩)
    · exact mem_image_of_mem _ (Or.inr ⟨ha0, ha1⟩)
  · apply le_antisymm
    · apply covering_number_le_one (some a)
      refine ⟨by simp, fun x hx => ⟨some a, by simp, ?_⟩⟩
      simp only [mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at hx
      rcases hx with rfl | rfl
      · simp only [dR_some]
        rw [show -a - a = -(2 * a) by ring, abs_neg, abs_of_pos (by linarith)]
        exact h2a
      · simpa using ENNReal.ofReal_pos.2 he0
    · exact one_le_covering_number_of_nonempty _ _ _ ⟨some a, by simp⟩

/-- 点态正半径不能代替统一阈值 -/
theorem remark316_no_uniform_scale (τ : ENNReal) (hτ0 : 0 < τ) (hτt : τ < ⊤) :
    ∃ B ⊆ Ustep, covering_number dR τ B < covering_number dR (1 / 2) (stepOp.func '' B) := by
  obtain ⟨a, -, -, -, hB, h1, -, h2⟩ := remark316 τ hτ0 hτt
  refine ⟨_, hB, ?_⟩
  rw [h1, h2]; norm_num

end OETCh03



namespace OETCh03

open formal_oet.vol_01.ch_01_foundations
open formal_oet.vol_01.ch_02_operator_bounds
open Set

theorem dR_lt_iff (x y e : ℝ) (he : 0 < e) :
    dR (some x) (some y) < ENNReal.ofReal e ↔ |x - y| < e := by
  rw [dR_some, ENNReal.ofReal_lt_ofReal_iff he]

theorem dAbs_lt_iff (x y e : ℝ) (he : 0 < e) :
    dAbs (some x) (some y) < ENNReal.ofReal e ↔ |(|x| - |y|)| < e := by
  rw [dAbs_some, ENNReal.ofReal_lt_ofReal_iff he]

/-! ### CH03-P26 习题 3.1 -/

/-- `ϕ(x) = 2x - 1`，`ϕ(⊥) = ⊥`。 -/
noncomputable def affOp : Operator RB none := realOp (fun x => 2 * x - 1)

def U01 : Set RB := {some 0, some 1}

theorem U01_sub_dom_realOp (h : ℝ → ℝ) : U01 ⊆ dom (realOp h) := by
  intro x hx; simp only [U01, mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at hx
  rcases hx with rfl | rfl <;> exact some_mem_dom_realOp _ _

/-- 习题 3.1(i-a) -/
theorem ex31_i_a :
    (∀ i, ExtendedPseudoMetric RB none (gAbs i)) ∧ affOp.func none = none ∧ U01 ⊆ dom affOp ∧
    affOp.func (some 0) = some (-1) ∧ affOp.func (some 1) = some 1 ∧
    Fib gAbs affOp {()} (affOp.func (some 0)) U01 = U01 ∧ () ∈ Iconst gAbs affOp U01 := by
  have hc : () ∈ Iconst gAbs affOp U01 := by
    intro x hx y hy
    simp only [U01, mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at hx hy
    rcases hx with rfl | rfl <;> rcases hy with rfl | rfl <;> norm_num [gAbs, affOp]
  refine ⟨gAbs_epm, rfl, U01_sub_dom_realOp _, by norm_num [affOp], by norm_num [affOp], ?_, hc⟩
  exact (prop35_vi_b gAbs gAbs_epm affOp U01 () hc _ (mem_image_of_mem _ (by simp [U01]))).1

/-- 习题 3.1(i-b) -/
theorem ex31_i_b :
    U01 ⊆ dom (affOp * affOp) ∧ (affOp * affOp).func (some 0) = some (-3) ∧
    (affOp * affOp).func (some 1) = some 1 ∧
    Fib gAbs (affOp * affOp) {()} ((affOp * affOp).func (some 0)) U01 = {some 0} ∧
    () ∈ Iact gAbs (affOp * affOp) U01 := by
  have e0 : (affOp * affOp).func (some 0) = some (-3) := by norm_num [affOp]
  have e1 : (affOp * affOp).func (some 1) = some 1 := by norm_num [affOp]
  refine ⟨?_, e0, e1, ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro x hx; simp only [U01, mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at hx
    rcases hx with rfl | rfl
    · show (affOp * affOp).func (some 0) ≠ none; rw [e0]; simp
    · show (affOp * affOp).func (some 1) ≠ none; rw [e1]; simp
  · rw [Fib_singleton, e0]
    ext x
    simp only [Fib1, U01, mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff, mem_setOf_eq]
    constructor
    · rintro ⟨hx, h⟩
      rcases hx with rfl | rfl
      · rfl
      · rw [e1] at h; norm_num [gAbs] at h
    · rintro rfl; exact ⟨Or.inl rfl, by rw [e0]; simp [gAbs]⟩
  · rw [mem_Iact_iff]
    constructor
    · rw [not_mem_Iconst_iff]
      exact ⟨some 0, by simp [U01], some 1, by simp [U01], by rw [e0, e1]; norm_num [gAbs]⟩
    · intro h
      have := h (some 0) (by simp [U01])
      rw [e0] at this; norm_num [gAbs] at this

/-- 习题 3.1(ii) -/
theorem ex31_ii (ψ : Operator RB none) (c : ℝ) (h0 : ψ.func (some 0) = some c)
    (h1 : ψ.func (some 1) = some c) :
    (ψ * ψ).func (some 0) = (ψ * ψ).func (some 1) ∧
    gAbs () ((ψ * ψ).func (some 0)) ((ψ * ψ).func (some 1)) = 0 ∧
    Fib gAbs (ψ * ψ) {()} ((ψ * ψ).func (some 0)) U01 =
      Fib gAbs (ψ * ψ) {()} ((ψ * ψ).func (some 1)) U01 ∧
    (ψ.func (some c) ≠ none → U01 ⊆ dom (ψ * ψ) ∧
      Fib gAbs (ψ * ψ) {()} ((ψ * ψ).func (some 0)) U01 = U01 ∧ () ∈ Iconst gAbs (ψ * ψ) U01) ∧
    (ψ.func (some c) = none → U01 ∩ dom (ψ * ψ) = ∅ ∧ ¬ U01 ⊆ dom (ψ * ψ)) := by
  have e : (ψ * ψ).func (some 0) = (ψ * ψ).func (some 1) := by simp [h0, h1]
  have hall : ∀ x ∈ U01, (ψ * ψ).func x = ψ.func (some c) := by
    intro x hx; simp only [U01, mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at hx
    rcases hx with rfl | rfl <;> simp [h0, h1]
  have hconst : () ∈ Iconst gAbs (ψ * ψ) U01 := by
    intro x hx y hy; rw [hall x hx, hall y hy]; exact dAbs_epm.dist_self _
  refine ⟨e, by rw [e]; exact dAbs_epm.dist_self _, by rw [e], fun hne => ⟨?_, ?_, hconst⟩,
    fun hn => ⟨?_, ?_⟩⟩
  · intro x hx; show (ψ * ψ).func x ≠ none; rw [hall x hx]; exact hne
  · exact (prop35_vi_b gAbs gAbs_epm _ U01 () hconst _
      (mem_image_of_mem _ (by simp [U01] : some 0 ∈ U01))).1
  · ext x
    simp only [mem_inter_iff, mem_empty_iff_false, iff_false, not_and]
    intro hx hd; exact hd (by rw [hall x hx]; exact hn)
  · intro hsub
    exact hsub (by simp [U01] : some 0 ∈ U01) (by rw [hall _ (by simp [U01])]; exact hn)

/-! ### CH03-P28 习题 3.3 -/

/-- 平移 `ϕ(x) = x + 1`。 -/
noncomputable def shiftOp : Operator RB none := realOp (fun x => x + 1)

def U33 : Set RB := {some (-3), some (-2), some 0, some 2}

theorem mem_U33 {x : RB} :
    x ∈ U33 ↔ x = some (-3) ∨ x = some (-2) ∨ x = some 0 ∨ x = some 2 := by
  simp [U33]

theorem U33_sub_dom_realOp (h : ℝ → ℝ) : U33 ⊆ dom (realOp h) := by
  intro x hx; rw [mem_U33] at hx
  rcases hx with rfl | rfl | rfl | rfl <;> exact some_mem_dom_realOp _ _

theorem U33_sub_dom_one : U33 ⊆ dom (1 : Operator RB none) := by
  intro x hx; rw [mem_U33] at hx
  rcases hx with rfl | rfl | rfl | rfl <;> simp [dom]

theorem fib33 (h : ℝ → ℝ) (t : ℝ) :
    Fib gAbs (realOp h) {()} (some t) U33 =
      {x | x ∈ U33 ∧ ∃ s, x = some s ∧ |(|h s| - |t|)| ≤ 0} := by
  rw [Fib_singleton]
  ext x
  simp only [Fib1, mem_setOf_eq]
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨hx, hd⟩
    have hx' := hx; rw [mem_U33] at hx'
    rcases hx' with rfl | rfl | rfl | rfl <;>
      exact ⟨hx, _, rfl, by simpa [gAbs, ENNReal.ofReal_eq_zero] using hd⟩
  · rintro ⟨hx, s, rfl, hs⟩
    exact ⟨hx, by simpa [gAbs, ENNReal.ofReal_eq_zero] using hs⟩

theorem one_eq_realOp_id : (1 : Operator RB none) = realOp id := by
  apply Operator.ext; funext z; cases z <;> rfl

/-- 习题 3.3(i) -/
theorem ex33_i :
    (∀ i, ExtendedPseudoMetric RB none (gAbs i)) ∧ U33 ⊆ dom (1 : Operator RB none) ∧
    U33 ⊆ dom shiftOp ∧
    (fun y => Fib gAbs (1 : Operator RB none) {()} y U33) '' ((1 : Operator RB none).func '' U33) =
      {{some (-3)}, {some (-2), some 2}, {some 0}} ∧
    (fun y => Fib gAbs shiftOp {()} y U33) '' (shiftOp.func '' U33) =
      {{some (-3)}, {some (-2), some 0}, {some 2}} ∧
    IsPartitionOf U33 {{some (-3)}, {some (-2), some 2}, {some 0}} ∧
    IsPartitionOf U33 {{some (-3)}, {some (-2), some 0}, {some 2}} := by
  have hid : (fun y => Fib gAbs (1 : Operator RB none) {()} y U33) '' ((1 : Operator RB none).func '' U33) =
      {{some (-3)}, {some (-2), some 2}, {some 0}} := by
    rw [one_eq_realOp_id]
    simp only [U33, image_insert_eq, image_singleton, realOp_some, id]
    rw [← U33]
    simp only [fib33]
    have e1 : {x | x ∈ U33 ∧ ∃ s, x = some s ∧ |(|id s| - |(-3 : ℝ)|)| ≤ 0} = {some (-3)} := by
      ext x; simp only [mem_setOf_eq, mem_singleton_iff, mem_U33, id]
      constructor
      · rintro ⟨hx, s, rfl, hs⟩
        rcases hx with h | h | h | h <;> simp only [Option.some.injEq] at h <;> subst h <;> (revert hs; norm_num)
      · rintro rfl; exact ⟨Or.inl rfl, _, rfl, by norm_num⟩
    have e2 : ∀ t : ℝ, |t| = 2 → {x | x ∈ U33 ∧ ∃ s, x = some s ∧ |(|id s| - |t|)| ≤ 0} = {some (-2), some 2} := by
      intro t ht
      ext x; simp only [mem_setOf_eq, mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff, mem_U33, id, ht]
      constructor
      · rintro ⟨hx, s, rfl, hs⟩
        rcases hx with h | h | h | h <;> simp only [Option.some.injEq] at h <;> subst h <;> (revert hs; norm_num)
      · rintro (rfl | rfl)
        · exact ⟨Or.inr (Or.inl rfl), _, rfl, by norm_num⟩
        · exact ⟨Or.inr (Or.inr (Or.inr rfl)), _, rfl, by norm_num⟩
    have e3 : {x | x ∈ U33 ∧ ∃ s, x = some s ∧ |(|id s| - |(0 : ℝ)|)| ≤ 0} = {some 0} := by
      ext x; simp only [mem_setOf_eq, mem_singleton_iff, mem_U33, id]
      constructor
      · rintro ⟨hx, s, rfl, hs⟩
        rcases hx with h | h | h | h <;> simp only [Option.some.injEq] at h <;> subst h <;> (revert hs; norm_num)
      · rintro rfl; exact ⟨Or.inr (Or.inr (Or.inl rfl)), _, rfl, by norm_num⟩
    rw [e1, e2 (-2) (by norm_num), e2 2 (by norm_num), e3]
    ext S; simp only [mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff]; tauto
  have hsh : (fun y => Fib gAbs shiftOp {()} y U33) '' (shiftOp.func '' U33) =
      {{some (-3)}, {some (-2), some 0}, {some 2}} := by
    simp only [shiftOp, U33, image_insert_eq, image_singleton, realOp_some]
    rw [← U33]
    simp only [fib33]
    have e1 : {x | x ∈ U33 ∧ ∃ s, x = some s ∧ |(|s + 1| - |(-3 : ℝ) + 1|)| ≤ 0} = {some (-3)} := by
      ext x; simp only [mem_setOf_eq, mem_singleton_iff, mem_U33]
      constructor
      · rintro ⟨hx, s, rfl, hs⟩
        rcases hx with h | h | h | h <;> simp only [Option.some.injEq] at h <;> subst h <;> (revert hs; norm_num)
      · rintro rfl; exact ⟨Or.inl rfl, _, rfl, by norm_num⟩
    have e2 : ∀ t : ℝ, |t + 1| = 1 → {x | x ∈ U33 ∧ ∃ s, x = some s ∧ |(|s + 1| - |t + 1|)| ≤ 0} = {some (-2), some 0} := by
      intro t ht
      ext x; simp only [mem_setOf_eq, mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff, mem_U33, ht]
      constructor
      · rintro ⟨hx, s, rfl, hs⟩
        rcases hx with h | h | h | h <;> simp only [Option.some.injEq] at h <;> subst h <;> (revert hs; norm_num)
      · rintro (rfl | rfl)
        · exact ⟨Or.inr (Or.inl rfl), _, rfl, by norm_num⟩
        · exact ⟨Or.inr (Or.inr (Or.inl rfl)), _, rfl, by norm_num⟩
    have e3 : {x | x ∈ U33 ∧ ∃ s, x = some s ∧ |(|s + 1| - |(2 : ℝ) + 1|)| ≤ 0} = {some 2} := by
      ext x; simp only [mem_setOf_eq, mem_singleton_iff, mem_U33]
      constructor
      · rintro ⟨hx, s, rfl, hs⟩
        rcases hx with h | h | h | h <;> simp only [Option.some.injEq] at h <;> subst h <;> (revert hs; norm_num)
      · rintro rfl; exact ⟨Or.inr (Or.inr (Or.inr rfl)), _, rfl, by norm_num⟩
    rw [e1, e2 (-2) (by norm_num), e2 0 (by norm_num), e3]
    ext S; simp only [mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff]; tauto
  refine ⟨gAbs_epm, U33_sub_dom_one, U33_sub_dom_realOp _, hid, hsh, ?_, ?_⟩
  · rw [← hid]; exact (prop35_ii_b gAbs gAbs_epm _ U33 {()}).2
  · rw [← hsh]; exact (prop35_ii_b gAbs gAbs_epm _ U33 {()}).2

theorem ex33_image : shiftOp.func '' U33 = {some (-2), some (-1), some 1, some 3} := by
  simp only [shiftOp, U33, image_insert_eq, image_singleton, realOp_some]; norm_num

theorem ofReal_three_halves : ENNReal.ofReal (3 / 2) = 3 / 2 := by
  rw [ENNReal.ofReal_div_of_pos two_pos]; simp

/-- 习题 3.3(ii) -/
theorem ex33_ii :
    covering_number dAbs (ENNReal.ofReal (3 / 2)) U33 = 2 ∧
    covering_number dAbs (ENNReal.ofReal (3 / 2)) (shiftOp.func '' U33) = 1 ∧
    ∃ h : EntropyDiffDomain gAbs {()} {()} (ENNReal.ofReal (3 / 2)) shiftOp U33,
      mapEntropyDiff gAbs {()} {()} (ENNReal.ofReal (3 / 2)) shiftOp U33 h = 1 := by
  classical
  have h32 : (0 : ℝ) < 3 / 2 := by norm_num
  have hcU : covering_number dAbs (ENNReal.ofReal (3 / 2)) U33 = 2 := by
    apply le_antisymm
    · apply covering_number_le_two (some 0) (some 2)
      refine ⟨?_, fun x hx => ?_⟩
      · intro c hc; simp only [Finset.coe_insert, Finset.coe_singleton, mem_insert_iff,
          mem_singleton_iff] at hc
        rcases hc with rfl | rfl <;> simp [U33]
      · rw [mem_U33] at hx
        rcases hx with rfl | rfl | rfl | rfl
        · exact ⟨some 2, by simp, by rw [dAbs_lt_iff _ _ _ h32]; norm_num⟩
        · exact ⟨some 2, by simp, by rw [dAbs_lt_iff _ _ _ h32]; norm_num⟩
        · exact ⟨some 0, by simp, by rw [dAbs_lt_iff _ _ _ h32]; norm_num⟩
        · exact ⟨some 2, by simp, by rw [dAbs_lt_iff _ _ _ h32]; norm_num⟩
    · apply two_le_covering_number (x := some 0) (y := some 2) (by simp [U33]) (by simp [U33])
      intro c hc h1 h2
      rw [mem_U33] at hc
      rcases hc with rfl | rfl | rfl | rfl <;>
        rw [dAbs_lt_iff _ _ _ h32] at h1 h2 <;> revert h1 h2 <;> norm_num
  have hcI : covering_number dAbs (ENNReal.ofReal (3 / 2)) (shiftOp.func '' U33) = 1 := by
    rw [ex33_image]
    apply le_antisymm
    · apply covering_number_le_one (some (-2))
      refine ⟨by simp, fun x hx => ⟨some (-2), by simp, ?_⟩⟩
      simp only [mem_insert_iff, mem_singleton_iff] at hx
      rcases hx with rfl | rfl | rfl | rfl <;> rw [dAbs_lt_iff _ _ _ h32] <;> norm_num
    · exact one_le_covering_number_of_nonempty _ _ _ ⟨some (-2), by simp⟩
  refine ⟨hcU, hcI, entropyDiffDomain_single gAbs () () _ shiftOp U33 ⟨some 0, by simp [U33]⟩
    (U33_sub_dom_realOp _) (by simpa using h32) ENNReal.ofReal_lt_top
    (Or.inl (by show covering_number dAbs _ U33 < ⊤; rw [hcU]; exact ENNReal.ofNat_lt_top)), ?_⟩
  rw [mapEntropyDiff_single]
  show log2_ereal (covering_number dAbs _ U33) - log2_ereal (covering_number dAbs _ _) = 1
  rw [hcU, hcI, log2_ereal_two, log2_ereal_one, sub_zero]

end OETCh03



namespace OETCh03

open formal_oet.vol_01.ch_01_foundations
open formal_oet.vol_01.ch_02_operator_bounds
open Set

/-! ### CH03-P29 习题 3.4 -/

/-- `f(x) = ⌊x⌋`，`f(⊥) = ⊥`。 -/
noncomputable def floorOp : Operator RB none := realOp (fun x => ((⌊x⌋ : ℤ) : ℝ))

@[simp] theorem floorOp_some (x : ℝ) : floorOp.func (some x) = some ((⌊x⌋ : ℤ) : ℝ) := rfl
